  - Create `e2e/support/env-guard.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// The E2E guard (spec §5 "The database", §11 "Uploads" and "The approval settings"): pure checks on the environment the
// app under test reads, on its upload folder, on the approval settings and on the run id. tests/e2e-guard.test.ts runs
// them on stand-in values. Every message names a database, a login or a setting, never a password or a connection
// string.
import path from "node:path"
import {
  parseWorkflowApprovalPolicy,
  workflowApprovalDefaults,
  workflowApprovalSettingKeys,
  type WorkflowApprovalPolicy,
} from "../../src/lib/workflow-approval.ts"

export const DEV_DATABASE = "asset_management_dev"
export const DEV_LOGIN = "asset_dev"
export const PRODUCTION_DATABASE = "asset_management"

type Env = Record<string, string | undefined>

export class E2eGuardError extends Error {
  name = "E2eGuardError"
}

// As src/lib/db-config.ts reads a variable: trimmed, without surrounding double quotes.
function clean(value: string | undefined): string | undefined {
  const cleaned = value?.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, "")
  return cleaned ? cleaned : undefined
}

// The database and the login the app connects with, read as src/lib/db-config.ts reads them: the database from
// DATABASE_URL's database=…, the login from DB_USER.
export function databaseTarget(env: Env): { database?: string; login?: string } {
  const database = clean(env.DATABASE_URL)?.match(/(?:^|;)database=([^;]+)/i)?.[1]
  return { database: database ? decodeURIComponent(database) : undefined, login: clean(env.DB_USER) }
}

// Stops on anything but the dev database with the dev login, above all on asset_management (Production).
export function assertDevDatabase(env: Env): { database: string; login: string } {
  const { database, login } = databaseTarget(env)
  const problems: string[] = []
  if (!database) problems.push("DATABASE_URL names no database")
  else if (database.toLowerCase() === PRODUCTION_DATABASE) problems.push(`the database is ${PRODUCTION_DATABASE}, the Production database`)
  else if (database.toLowerCase() !== DEV_DATABASE) problems.push(`the database is ${database}, not ${DEV_DATABASE}`)
  if (!login) problems.push("DB_USER is empty")
  else if (login.toLowerCase() !== DEV_LOGIN) problems.push(`the login is ${login}, not ${DEV_LOGIN}`)
  for (const key of ["DB_SERVER", "DB_PASSWORD"]) if (!clean(env[key])) problems.push(`${key} is empty`)
  if (problems.length > 0 || !database || !login) {
    throw new E2eGuardError(`E2E guard stopped the run: ${problems.join("; ")}. The suite runs only on ${DEV_DATABASE} with the login ${DEV_LOGIN}.`)
  }
  return { database, login }
}

// The upload folder must be a local folder inside the project (spec §11 "Uploads": UPLOAD_DIR is the relative ./uploads
// on this machine), never a network share or a server path such as /var/www/asset-system/uploads.
export function assertLocalUploadDir(env: Env, root: string): { setting: string; resolved: string } {
  const setting = clean(env.UPLOAD_DIR) ?? "./uploads"
  if (/^(?:\\\\|\/\/)/.test(setting)) {
    throw new E2eGuardError(`E2E guard stopped the run: UPLOAD_DIR is a network path. The suite uploads only into a folder inside the project.`)
  }
  const resolved = path.resolve(root, setting)
  const relative = path.relative(root, resolved)
  if (relative === "" || relative.startsWith("..") || path.isAbsolute(relative)) {
    throw new E2eGuardError(`E2E guard stopped the run: UPLOAD_DIR (${setting}) is not a folder inside the project. The suite uploads only into a folder inside the project.`)
  }
  return { setting, resolved }
}

// The approval settings, read with a GET before the run (spec §11): the suite asserts the defaults (segregation on)
// and never changes them, because the settings save is off limits. Any difference stops the run with what differs.
// GET /api/admin/settings answers every known setting: a stored row with its own id, and a default the database does
// not hold with its key as its id (src/app/api/admin/settings/route.ts, missingDefaults). storedKeys are the rows.
export function checkApprovalPolicy(settings: readonly { key: string; value: string; id?: string }[]): { policy: WorkflowApprovalPolicy; storedKeys: string[] } {
  const keys = new Set<string>(workflowApprovalSettingKeys)
  const approval = settings.filter((setting) => keys.has(setting.key))
  const stored = approval.filter((setting) => setting.id !== undefined && setting.id !== setting.key)
  const policy = parseWorkflowApprovalPolicy(approval)
  const differences = (Object.keys(workflowApprovalDefaults) as (keyof WorkflowApprovalPolicy)[])
    .filter((key) => policy[key] !== workflowApprovalDefaults[key])
    .map((key) => `${key} is ${policy[key]} (the default is ${workflowApprovalDefaults[key]})`)
  if (differences.length > 0) {
    throw new E2eGuardError(`E2E guard stopped the run: the approval settings differ from the defaults the suite asserts: ${differences.join("; ")}. The suite never changes them; ask the user.`)
  }
  return { policy, storedKeys: stored.map((setting) => setting.key).sort() }
}

// The run id (spec §5: "E2E-" plus a run id, for example E2E-1019A): E2E-, the month and day, and the letter after the
// last one used that day; after Z, T and the time. Earlier runs are found in the codes and usernames they left.
const RUN_ID_IN_CODE = /E2E-(\d{4})(T\d{4}|[A-Z])(?![A-Za-z0-9])/gi
const RUN_ID_IN_USERNAME = /^e2e\.[a-z-]+-(\d{4})(t\d{4}|[a-z])$/i
export function runIdsIn(values: readonly string[]): string[] {
  const ids = new Set<string>()
  for (const value of values) {
    for (const match of value.matchAll(RUN_ID_IN_CODE)) ids.add(`E2E-${match[1]}${match[2].toUpperCase()}`)
    const username = value.match(RUN_ID_IN_USERNAME)
    if (username) ids.add(`E2E-${username[1]}${username[2].toUpperCase()}`)
  }
  return [...ids].sort()
}

const pad = (value: number) => String(value).padStart(2, "0")
export function nextRunId(now: Date, existing: readonly string[]): string {
  const day = `${pad(now.getMonth() + 1)}${pad(now.getDate())}`
  const letters = runIdsIn(existing)
    .filter((id) => id.startsWith(`E2E-${day}`) && id.length === 9)
    .map((id) => id.charCodeAt(8))
  const next = letters.length === 0 ? 65 : Math.max(...letters) + 1
  if (next <= 90) return `E2E-${day}${String.fromCharCode(next)}`
  return `E2E-${day}T${pad(now.getHours())}${pad(now.getMinutes())}`
}

// The run id without its E2E- prefix, lowercased: the tail of the run's usernames and role keys (1019a).
export function runSuffix(runId: string): string {
  return runId.replace(/^E2E-/, "").toLowerCase()
}
```

  - Create `e2e/support/app-env.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// The environment `next start` gives the app under test: Next's own loader (@next/env, production mode) over the
// project's env files (.env.production.local, .env.local, .env.production, .env; the process environment wins), read
// without printing or keeping a value. playwright.config.ts runs the guard on it before anything else starts.
import * as nextEnvModule from "@next/env"
import { assertDevDatabase, assertLocalUploadDir } from "./env-guard.ts"

// @next/env is CommonJS marked __esModule: Playwright's loader sees its named exports, Node's ESM loader only a default.
const nextEnv: typeof nextEnvModule =
  "loadEnvConfig" in nextEnvModule ? nextEnvModule : (nextEnvModule as unknown as { default: typeof nextEnvModule }).default
const silent = { info: () => undefined, error: () => undefined }

export function readAppEnv(root: string): Record<string, string | undefined> {
  const { combinedEnv } = nextEnv.loadEnvConfig(root, false, silent, true)
  const env = { ...combinedEnv }
  // loadEnvConfig writes the files into process.env: put it back, so no value reaches the runner, a worker or the
  // web server through this read (next start loads the same files itself).
  nextEnv.resetEnv()
  return env
}

export function guardAppEnv(root: string) {
  const env = readAppEnv(root)
  return { ...assertDevDatabase(env), uploads: assertLocalUploadDir(env, root) }
}
```

  - Create `e2e/support/sign-in.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// One sign-in through the login page, with a known account, and never a second try (spec §11 "No failed login
// anywhere"): a local password that fails is passed on to LDAP (src/lib/auth.ts), and the dev settings point at the
// company AD. So this helper submits only once the form is live, only with the whole password in its field, and stops
// the run on a refusal. The password goes into the field through the DOM, so no step title, trace or report holds it.
import type { Page } from "@playwright/test"

export class SignInRefusedError extends Error {
  name = "SignInRefusedError"
}

export async function signInOnce(page: Page, username: string, password: string, locale = "th"): Promise<void> {
  // A submit before hydration would be the browser's own GET of the form, with the password in the URL.
  const leaks: string[] = []
  const watchLeak = (request: { url(): string }) => {
    if (/[?&]password=/i.test(request.url())) leaks.push(request.url().split("?")[0])
  }
  page.on("request", watchLeak)
  try {
    await page.goto(`/${locale}/login`)
    const usernameField = page.locator("#login-username")
    const passwordField = page.locator("#login-password")
    // React marks a hydrated element with its props; the form handles the submit only from then on.
    await page.waitForFunction(() => {
      const input = document.querySelector("#login-username")
      return Boolean(input && Object.keys(input).some((key) => key.startsWith("__reactProps$")))
    })
    await usernameField.fill(username)
    await passwordField.evaluate((input, value) => {
      const field = input as HTMLInputElement
      Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(HTMLInputElement.prototype, "value")?.set?.call(field, value)
      field.dispatchEvent(new Event("input", { bubbles: true }))
    }, password)
    const held = await passwordField.evaluate((input) => (input as HTMLInputElement).value.length)
    const heldUsername = await usernameField.inputValue()
    if (held !== password.length || heldUsername !== username) {
      throw new SignInRefusedError(`Sign-in for ${username} not submitted: the form did not hold the account. The run stops here.`)
    }
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "เข้าสู่ระบบ", exact: true }).click()
    const refusal = page.locator("form [role=alert]")
    const outcome = await Promise.race([
      page.waitForURL((url) => !url.pathname.endsWith("/login"), { timeout: 30_000 }).then(() => "signed-in" as const),
      refusal.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 30_000 }).then(() => "refused" as const),
    ]).catch(() => "no-answer" as const)
    if (outcome !== "signed-in") {
      throw new SignInRefusedError(
        `Sign-in for ${username} ${outcome === "refused" ? "was refused" : "had no answer in 30 s"}. The run stops and never tries again: a failed local sign-in goes on to the company AD.`
      )
    }
  } finally {
    page.off("request", watchLeak)
  }
  if (leaks.length > 0) throw new SignInRefusedError(`Sign-in for ${username} sent the password in a URL (${leaks.join(", ")}). The run stops here.`)
}
```

  - Create `e2e/support/state.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// The run's state on disk (e2e/.state, git-ignored): run.json names the current run (the global setup writes it);
// each run keeps what its setup created, with the users' generated passwords (run-data.json), and its sessions in its
// own folder. Nothing here prints a value.
import { mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs"
import path from "node:path"
import type { WorkflowApprovalPolicy } from "../../src/lib/workflow-approval.ts"
import { fromRoot, RUN_FILE } from "./paths.ts"

export type RunState = {
  runId: string
  startedAt: string
  baseURL: string
  database: string
  login: string
  approval: { policy: WorkflowApprovalPolicy; storedKeys: string[] }
}

function writeJson(relative: string, value: unknown) {
  const file = fromRoot(relative)
  mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true })
  writeFileSync(file, `${JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)}\n`)
}

function readJson<T>(relative: string): T {
  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(fromRoot(relative), "utf8")) as T
}

export function writeRunState(state: RunState) {
  writeJson(RUN_FILE, state)
}

export function readRunState(): RunState {
  return readJson<RunState>(RUN_FILE)
}
```

```ts
// The guard (spec §5 "A guard runs before any test", §11): runs once per `npm run test:e2e`, after next start is up and
// before the setup project. playwright.config.ts has already checked the env files when it loaded; this checks them
// again, prints the names it checked (never a value), signs the seed admin in once, reads the approval settings with a
// GET and asserts the defaults, and writes the run id to e2e/.state/run.json.
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from "node:fs"
import path from "node:path"
import { chromium, type FullConfig } from "@playwright/test"
import { parse } from "dotenv"
import { guardAppEnv } from "./support/app-env.ts"
import { checkApprovalPolicy, nextRunId } from "./support/env-guard.ts"
import { ADMIN_STATE_FILE, e2eBaseUrl, fromRoot, RUN_FILE, SEED_LOGIN_FILE } from "./support/paths.ts"
import { signInOnce } from "./support/sign-in.ts"
import { writeRunState } from "./support/state.ts"

function readSeedLogin(root: string) {
  const file = path.resolve(root, SEED_LOGIN_FILE)
  if (!existsSync(file)) throw new Error(`E2E guard stopped the run: ${SEED_LOGIN_FILE} is missing (E2E_SEED_USERNAME and E2E_SEED_PASSWORD, the seed admin's sign-in).`)
  const values = parse(readFileSync(file))
  const username = values.E2E_SEED_USERNAME?.trim()
  const password = values.E2E_SEED_PASSWORD
  if (!username || !password) throw new Error(`E2E guard stopped the run: ${SEED_LOGIN_FILE} needs E2E_SEED_USERNAME and E2E_SEED_PASSWORD.`)
  return { username, password }
}

export default async function globalSetup(config: FullConfig) {
  const root = config.configFile ? path.dirname(config.configFile) : process.cwd()
  // A run that stops before the run id is written leaves no run id behind for the reporter or a later step.
  rmSync(fromRoot(RUN_FILE, root), { force: true })
  const target = guardAppEnv(root)
  console.log(`E2E guard: database ${target.database}, login ${target.login}, uploads ${target.uploads.setting} (inside the project)`)
  const seed = readSeedLogin(root)
  const baseURL = e2eBaseUrl()

  const browser = await chromium.launch()
  try {
    const context = await browser.newContext({ baseURL, locale: "th-TH", timezoneId: "Asia/Bangkok" })
    const page = await context.newPage()
    await signInOnce(page, seed.username, seed.password)
    mkdirSync(path.dirname(fromRoot(ADMIN_STATE_FILE, root)), { recursive: true })
    await context.storageState({ path: fromRoot(ADMIN_STATE_FILE, root) })

    // The approval settings (spec §11): a GET, never a save.
    const settingsResponse = await context.request.get("/api/admin/settings")
    if (!settingsResponse.ok()) throw new Error(`E2E guard stopped the run: GET /api/admin/settings answered ${settingsResponse.status()}`)
    const approval = checkApprovalPolicy((await settingsResponse.json()) as { key: string; value: string; id?: string }[])
    console.log(
      `E2E guard: approval settings ${approval.storedKeys.length === 0 ? "not stored, the defaults apply" : `stored (${approval.storedKeys.join(", ")})`}; segregation ${approval.policy.segregationRequired ? "on" : "off"}`
    )

    // The run id: the letter after the last run of the day, found in the E2E departments and usernames.
    const now = new Date()
    const day = `E2E-${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`
    const departments = await context.request.get(`/api/departments?search=${encodeURIComponent(day)}`)
    const users = await context.request.get("/api/admin/users")
    if (!departments.ok() || !users.ok()) throw new Error(`E2E guard stopped the run: the run id lookup answered ${departments.status()} and ${users.status()}`)
    const codes = ((await departments.json()) as { code: string }[]).map((department) => department.code)
    const usernames = ((await users.json()) as { data: { username: string }[] }).data.map((user) => user.username)
    const runId = nextRunId(now, [...codes, ...usernames])
    writeRunState({
      runId,
      startedAt: now.toISOString(),
      baseURL,
      database: target.database,
      login: target.login,
      approval,
    })
    console.log(`E2E run ${runId}`)
  } finally {
    await browser.close()
  }
}
```

  - Create `e2e/support/run-plan.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// What the setup step creates once per run (spec §7), as plain data: the E2E department, location and employees, the
// four E2E roles with exactly the permissions of the spec's table, and the four users. Every code, name and username
// carries the run id, so repeat runs never collide on a unique value and a person sees the marker in any list.
// tests/e2e-setup.test.ts pins it; e2e/setup/run-data.setup.ts creates it through the app as the seed admin.
import { runSuffix } from "./env-guard.ts"

export type E2eUserKey = "itStaff" | "itHead" | "viewer" | "employee"

// Spec §7, the users table. IT staff: no approve; the IT head: the IT staff set plus audit and disposal approve; the
// viewer: view only, on the modules the IT staff work in; the employee: no permission (lands on My Assets).
const IT_STAFF_PERMISSIONS = [
  "asset:view", "asset:create", "asset:edit",
  "audit:view", "audit:create", "audit:edit",
  "maintenance:view", "maintenance:create", "maintenance:edit",
  "disposal:view", "disposal:create", "disposal:edit",
  "report:view", "report:export",
] as const

export const E2E_ROLE_PERMISSIONS: Record<E2eUserKey, readonly string[]> = {
  itStaff: IT_STAFF_PERMISSIONS,
  itHead: [...IT_STAFF_PERMISSIONS, "audit:approve", "disposal:approve"],
  viewer: ["asset:view", "audit:view", "maintenance:view", "disposal:view", "report:view"],
  employee: [],
}

type UserPlan = {
  slug: string
  linked: boolean
  roleTh: string
  roleEn: string
  // Where "/th" sends the user (src/lib/default-home.ts): the work dashboard, or My Assets for an employee with no
  // overview permission.
  landingPath: string
}

export const E2E_USER_PLANS: Record<E2eUserKey, UserPlan> = {
  itStaff: { slug: "it-staff", linked: true, roleTh: "เจ้าหน้าที่ IT", roleEn: "IT staff", landingPath: "/th/dashboard" },
  itHead: { slug: "it-head", linked: true, roleTh: "หัวหน้า IT", roleEn: "IT head", landingPath: "/th/dashboard" },
  // Spec §7 has the viewer unlinked, but the app cannot create a second user with no employee link (finding A1:
  // users.employeeId is a unique constraint, and SQL Server lets it hold one NULL, the seed admin's). Linked until A1 is
  // fixed; run-data.setup.ts keeps an expected-to-fail test of an unlinked user.
  viewer: { slug: "viewer", linked: true, roleTh: "ผู้ดูอย่างเดียว", roleEn: "Viewer", landingPath: "/th/dashboard" },
  employee: { slug: "employee", linked: true, roleTh: "พนักงาน", roleEn: "Employee", landingPath: "/th/my-assets" },
}

export const E2E_USER_KEYS = Object.keys(E2E_USER_PLANS) as E2eUserKey[]

// e2e.it-staff-1019a (spec §7: e2e.it-staff-<run>).
export function usernameFor(key: E2eUserKey, runId: string): string {
  return `e2e.${E2E_USER_PLANS[key].slug}-${runSuffix(runId)}`
}

// A role key takes a-z, 0-9 and _ only (src/lib/validations/admin-role.ts): e2e_it_staff_1019a.
export function roleNameFor(key: E2eUserKey, runId: string): string {
  return `e2e_${E2E_USER_PLANS[key].slug.replaceAll("-", "_")}_${runSuffix(runId)}`
}

export function roleDisplayFor(key: E2eUserKey, runId: string) {
  const plan = E2E_USER_PLANS[key]
  return { displayName: `${runId} ${plan.roleEn}`, displayNameTh: `${runId} ${plan.roleTh}` }
}

export type EmployeePlan = { key: string; code: string; fullNameTh: string; fullNameEn: string; userKey?: E2eUserKey }

// The department, the location (a room) and the employees: one for each linked user, and two receivers for the
// handovers. Codes stay within the schemas' limits (department and employee 20, location 50).
export function runRecords(runId: string) {
  const employees: EmployeePlan[] = [
    ...E2E_USER_KEYS.filter((key) => E2E_USER_PLANS[key].linked).map((key, index) => ({
      key,
      code: `${runId}-E${index + 1}`,
      fullNameTh: `${runId} ${E2E_USER_PLANS[key].roleTh}`,
      fullNameEn: `${runId} ${E2E_USER_PLANS[key].roleEn}`,
      userKey: key,
    })),
    { key: "receiver1", code: `${runId}-R1`, fullNameTh: `${runId} ผู้รับมอบ 1`, fullNameEn: `${runId} Receiver 1` },
    { key: "receiver2", code: `${runId}-R2`, fullNameTh: `${runId} ผู้รับมอบ 2`, fullNameEn: `${runId} Receiver 2` },
  ]
  return {
    department: { code: `${runId}-D`, name: `${runId} แผนกทดสอบ E2E` },
    location: { code: `${runId}-L`, name: `${runId} ห้องทดสอบ E2E`, locationType: "Room" as const },
    employees,
  }
}

// A generated password: 24 random characters from the bytes given, plus one of each class; never printed.
export function passwordFrom(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyz23456789"
  if (bytes.length < 24) throw new Error("passwordFrom needs 24 random bytes")
  const body = Array.from(bytes.slice(0, 24), (byte) => alphabet[byte % alphabet.length]).join("")
  return `${body}Aa9-`
}
```

  - Create `e2e/setup/run-data.setup.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// The setup step (spec §7): once per run, through the app's own pages and APIs, signed in as the seed admin (the
// storage state the global setup saved). No direct SQL write, so the app's own rules apply. It creates the E2E
// department and location, the employees, the four roles with exactly the permissions of the spec (on the role
// form, which has no API to list permissions), the four users with generated passwords, and one storage state per user.
import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto"
import { mkdirSync } from "node:fs"
import path from "node:path"
import { expect, test as setup, type APIRequestContext, type Page } from "@playwright/test"
import { runSuffix } from "../support/env-guard.ts"
import { ADMIN_STATE_FILE, authStateFile, fromRoot } from "../support/paths.ts"
import {
  E2E_ROLE_PERMISSIONS,
  E2E_USER_KEYS,
  E2E_USER_PLANS,
  passwordFrom,
  roleDisplayFor,
  roleNameFor,
  runRecords,
  usernameFor,
  type E2eUserKey,
} from "../support/run-plan.ts"
import { signInOnce } from "../support/sign-in.ts"
import { readRunData, readRunState, writeRunData, type RunData, type RunUser } from "../support/state.ts"

setup.use({ storageState: fromRoot(ADMIN_STATE_FILE) })

// The role form's action labels (messages/th.json adminRolesPage.actions).
const ACTION_LABELS: Record<string, string> = { view: "ดู", create: "เพิ่ม", edit: "แก้ไข", delete: "ลบ", export: "ส่งออก", approve: "อนุมัติ" }

async function created<T>(request: APIRequestContext, url: string, data: unknown): Promise<T> {
  const response = await request.post(url, { data })
  if (response.status() !== 201) throw new Error(`POST ${url} answered ${response.status()}: ${(await response.text()).slice(0, 300)}`)
  return (await response.json()) as T
}

// One role on the role form, ticking exactly its permissions; the stored set is read back on the edit page.
async function createRole(page: Page, key: E2eUserKey, runId: string): Promise<{ id: string; name: string }> {
  const name = roleNameFor(key, runId)
  const display = roleDisplayFor(key, runId)
  await page.goto("/th/admin/roles/new")
  await page.locator("#role-key").fill(name)
  await page.locator("#role-display-name").fill(display.displayName)
  await page.locator("#role-display-name-th").fill(display.displayNameTh)
  for (const permission of E2E_ROLE_PERMISSIONS[key]) {
    const [moduleName, action] = permission.split(":")
    const row = page.locator("tr").filter({ has: page.locator("span.tag", { hasText: new RegExp(`^${moduleName}$`) }) })
    await row.getByRole("checkbox", { name: ACTION_LABELS[action], exact: true }).check()
  }
  const saved = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith("/api/admin/roles") && response.request().method() === "POST")
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึกสิทธิ์" }).click()
  const response = await saved
  if (response.status() !== 201) throw new Error(`the role form's save of ${name} answered ${response.status()}: ${(await response.text()).slice(0, 300)}`)
  const role = (await response.json()) as { id: string; name: string }
  await page.waitForURL("**/th/admin/roles")

  // Read back: the edit page ticks exactly the stored permissions.
  await page.goto(`/th/admin/roles/${role.id}/edit`)
  await expect(page.locator("#role-key")).toHaveValue(name)
  const ticked: string[] = []
  for (const row of await page.locator("tbody tr").all()) {
    const moduleName = (await row.locator("span.tag").first().textContent())?.trim()
    for (const [action, label] of Object.entries(ACTION_LABELS)) {
      if (await row.getByRole("checkbox", { name: label, exact: true }).isChecked()) ticked.push(`${moduleName}:${action}`)
    }
  }
  expect(ticked.sort(), `the stored permissions of ${name}`).toEqual([...E2E_ROLE_PERMISSIONS[key]].sort())
  return role
}

setup("the run's department, location, employees, roles and users, and a session for each user", async ({ page, browser }) => {
  setup.setTimeout(300_000)
  const { runId, baseURL } = readRunState()
  const records = runRecords(runId)
  const request = page.request

  // The company and branch the E2E records sit in: the first active company with an asset tag code and a branch.
  const companies = (await (await request.get("/api/companies")).json()) as { id: string; code: string; assetTagCode: string | null }[]
  const branches = (await (await request.get("/api/branches")).json()) as { id: string; code: string; company: { id: string } }[]
  const company = companies
    .filter((candidate) => candidate.assetTagCode && branches.some((branch) => branch.company.id === candidate.id))
    .sort((left, right) => left.code.localeCompare(right.code))[0]
  if (!company) throw new Error("no active company with an asset tag code and a branch")
  const branch = branches.filter((candidate) => candidate.company.id === company.id).sort((left, right) => left.code.localeCompare(right.code))[0]

  // The department first: its code claims the run id (the global setup reads it to find the next one).
  const department = await created<{ id: string }>(request, "/api/departments", { ...records.department, companyId: company.id, isActive: true })
  const location = await created<{ id: string }>(request, "/api/locations", { ...records.location, branchId: branch.id, isActive: true })
  const employees: RunData["employees"] = {}
  for (const employee of records.employees) {
    const row = await created<{ id: string }>(request, "/api/employees", {
      code: employee.code,
      fullNameTh: employee.fullNameTh,
      fullNameEn: employee.fullNameEn,
      // The schema takes a missing email only when the key is there (the employee form sends "").
      email: null,
      companyId: company.id,
      branchId: branch.id,
      departmentId: department.id,
      employmentStatus: "active",
      isActive: true,
    })
    employees[employee.key] = { id: row.id, code: employee.code, fullNameTh: employee.fullNameTh }
  }

  const data: RunData = { companyId: company.id, branchId: branch.id, departmentId: department.id, locationId: location.id, employees, users: {} }
  for (const key of E2E_USER_KEYS) {
    const role = await createRole(page, key, runId)
    const plan = E2E_USER_PLANS[key]
    const user: RunUser = {
      key,
      username: usernameFor(key, runId),
      password: passwordFrom(randomBytes(24)),
      displayName: roleDisplayFor(key, runId).displayNameTh,
      employeeId: plan.linked ? employees[key].id : null,
      roleId: role.id,
      roleName: role.name,
      landingPath: plan.landingPath,
    }
    // Saved before the user exists, so a generated password is never lost.
    data.users[key] = user
    writeRunData(runId, data)
    const createdUser = await created<{ id: string }>(request, "/api/admin/users", {
      username: user.username,
      password: user.password,
      displayName: user.displayName,
      email: null,
      employeeId: user.employeeId,
      roleIds: [role.id],
      isActive: true,
    })
    user.userId = createdUser.id
    writeRunData(runId, data)
  }

  // One session per user: a sign-in with the password just set (never a failed one), then the storage state.
  for (const key of E2E_USER_KEYS) {
    const user = data.users[key]
    if (!user) throw new Error(`the ${key} user was not created`)
    const context = await browser.newContext({ baseURL, locale: "th-TH", timezoneId: "Asia/Bangkok" })
    const userPage = await context.newPage()
    await signInOnce(userPage, user.username, user.password)
    await userPage.goto("/th")
    await expect(userPage).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`${user.landingPath}(?:[?#]|$)`))
    const file = fromRoot(authStateFile(runId, key))
    mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true })
    await context.storageState({ path: file })
    await context.close()
  }
})

// Finding A1 (expected to fail until it is fixed): Admin > Users cannot create a second user with no employee link.
// users.employeeId is a unique constraint (users_employeeId_key, no filter), SQL Server lets it hold one NULL, and the
// seed admin holds it: the save answers 500. When A1 is fixed this test passes, Playwright reports it, and the viewer
// goes back to unlinked (run-plan.ts).
setup("a second user with no employee link can be created (finding A1)", async ({ page }) => {
  setup.fail(true, "A1: users.employeeId allows one NULL on SQL Server")
  const { runId } = readRunState()
  const viewer = readRunData(runId).users.viewer
  if (!viewer) throw new Error("the run has no viewer role: the setup test did not finish")
  const response = await page.request.post("/api/admin/users", {
    data: {
      username: `e2e.unlinked-${runSuffix(runId)}`,
      password: passwordFrom(randomBytes(24)),
      displayName: `${runId} ไม่ผูกพนักงาน`,
      email: null,
      employeeId: null,
      roleIds: [viewer.roleId],
      isActive: true,
    },
  })
  expect(response.status(), "a user with no employee link is saved").toBe(201)
})
```

```ts

test("every test is measured: console errors, failed requests, axe serious and critical, load times, screenshots, one summary (e2e foundation task 4, EF4)", async () => {
  const core = await import("../e2e/support/measure-core.ts")
  const { ALLOW_LIST } = await import("../e2e/support/allow-list.ts")
  const page = "http://localhost:3100/th/dashboard"
  const refusal = { method: "POST", url: /\/api\/assets\/[^/]+\/checkout$/, status: 409, reason: "a second handover of the same asset" }
  const records = {
    console: [
      { kind: "console" as const, text: "Failed to load resource: the server responded with a status of 409 (Conflict)", url: "http://localhost:3100/api/assets/a1/checkout", pageUrl: page },
      { kind: "console" as const, text: "Failed to load resource: the server responded with a status of 500 (Internal Server Error)", url: "http://localhost:3100/api/reports", pageUrl: page },
      { kind: "pageerror" as const, text: "TypeError: x is undefined", pageUrl: page },
    ],
    responses: [
      { method: "POST", url: "http://localhost:3100/api/assets/a1/checkout", status: 409, resourceType: "fetch", pageUrl: page },
      { method: "GET", url: "http://localhost:3100/api/reports", status: 500, resourceType: "fetch", pageUrl: page },
    ],
    failures: [
      { method: "GET", url: "http://localhost:3100/th/assets?_rsc=abc", errorText: "net::ERR_ABORTED", resourceType: "fetch", pageUrl: page },
      { method: "GET", url: "http://localhost:3100/api/assets", errorText: "net::ERR_ABORTED", resourceType: "fetch", pageUrl: page },
      { method: "GET", url: "http://localhost:3100/th/assets?_rsc=abc", errorText: "net::ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED", resourceType: "fetch", pageUrl: page },
    ],
  }
  const unmet = { url: /\/api\/disposal-requests$/, status: 403, reason: "the viewer may not request a disposal" }
  const classified = core.classify(records, ALLOW_LIST, [refusal, unmet])
  // The refused handover and its console line are the test's own; the 500 and its console line, the page error, the
  // aborted API call and the refused connection are not; the cancelled prefetch is on the reviewed list.
  assert.deepEqual(classified.expected, [{ reason: refusal.reason, method: "POST", url: "http://localhost:3100/api/assets/a1/checkout", status: 409 }])
  assert.deepEqual(classified.unexpectedResponses.map((response) => response.status), [500])
  assert.deepEqual(classified.unexpectedConsole.map((message) => message.kind), ["console", "pageerror"])
  assert.deepEqual(classified.unexpectedFailures.map((failure) => `${failure.url} ${failure.errorText}`), [
    "http://localhost:3100/api/assets net::ERR_ABORTED",
    "http://localhost:3100/th/assets?_rsc=abc net::ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED",
  ])
  assert.deepEqual(classified.allowed, [{ id: "next-prefetch-cancelled", kind: "requestfailed", url: "http://localhost:3100/th/assets?_rsc=abc" }])
  assert.deepEqual(classified.unmetRefusals.map((item) => item.reason), [unmet.reason])

  // axe: serious and critical fail the test, moderate and minor are counted only.
  const node = (target: string) => ({ target: [target] })
  const axe = core.axeCounts([
    { id: "color-contrast", impact: "serious", help: "Elements must meet minimum color contrast", helpUrl: "", nodes: [node(".a"), node(".b")] },
    { id: "button-name", impact: "critical", help: "Buttons must have discernible text", helpUrl: "", nodes: [node("#x")] },
    { id: "region", impact: "moderate", help: "All page content should be contained by landmarks", helpUrl: "", nodes: [node("div")] },
  ])
  assert.deepEqual([axe.serious, axe.critical, axe.moderate, axe.minor], [1, 1, 1, 0])
  assert.deepEqual(axe.failing.map((violation) => `${violation.impact} ${violation.id} ${violation.targets.join(",")}`), ["serious color-contrast .a,.b", "critical button-name #x"])
  assert.deepEqual(core.AXE_TAGS, ["wcag2a", "wcag2aa", "wcag21a", "wcag21aa"])
  const problems = core.measurementProblems(classified, [{ label: "landing", url: page, loadMs: 300, domContentLoadedMs: 200, axe }])
  assert.equal(problems.length, 2 + 1 + 2 + 1 + 2)
  assert.ok(problems.some((problem) => problem.startsWith("axe serious color-contrast on landing")))
  assert.ok(problems.some((problem) => problem.startsWith("expected refusal not seen: any")))

  // The run summary: per project, an expected failure is its own count, never a pass or a failure.
  const summaryRow = { title: "t", file: "f", device: "phone", theme: "dark", browser: "webkit", user: null, durationMs: 1, pages: [], timings: [], allowed: [], expected: [], problems: [], screenshots: [], errors: [] }
  const row = (project: string, status: string, expectedStatus = "passed") => ({ ...summaryRow, project, status, expectedStatus })
  const summary = core.buildRunSummary("E2E-1019A", "2026-10-19T00:00:00.000Z", "passed", [row("phone-dark", "passed"), row("phone-dark", "failed"), row("setup", "failed", "failed"), row("setup", "skipped")])
  assert.deepEqual(summary.projects, { "phone-dark": { passed: 1, failed: 1, skipped: 0, expectedFailures: 0 }, setup: { passed: 0, failed: 0, skipped: 1, expectedFailures: 1 } })

  // Every allow-list entry is reviewed and says why; there are few of them.
  assert.ok(ALLOW_LIST.length <= 5, "the allow-list stays short")
  for (const entry of ALLOW_LIST) {
    assert.match(entry.reviewed, /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/, entry.id)
    assert.ok(entry.reason.length >= 40, `${entry.id} says why`)
  }
  // Every test gets the measurements (an auto fixture), the end screenshot, and the reporter collects the rows.
  const fixtures = read("e2e/support/fixtures.ts")
  assert.match(fixtures, /measure: \[\n[\s\S]*?\{ auto: true \},\n  \],/)
  assert.match(read("e2e/support/measure.ts"), /await this\.screenshot\("end"\)/)
  // The login page smoke runs the measurements with no one signed in.
  const loginSmoke = read("e2e/smoke/login-page.spec.ts")
  assert.ok(loginSmoke.includes(`import { expect, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"`), "the login page smoke uses the suite's fixtures")
  assert.ok(loginSmoke.includes(`await measure.visit("/th/login", "login page")`), "the login page is a measured visit")
  const config = read("playwright.config.ts")
  assert.match(config, /\["\.\/e2e\/support\/summary-reporter\.ts"\]/)
  assert.match(config, /trace: "retain-on-failure",\n    screenshot: "only-on-failure",/)
  // The report's trace viewer is bundled JS: ESLint leaves the report and the results alone.
  const eslint = read("eslint.config.mjs")
  for (const folder of [`"e2e-report/**",`, `"e2e-results/**",`]) assert.ok(eslint.includes(folder), `eslint ignores ${folder}`)
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** (exact):

  - Create `e2e/support/measure-core.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// The measurements every workflow records (spec §3.3, §8 "Per step"), as pure rules: which console errors and failed
// requests a test may have (the reviewed allow-list and the refusals the test itself expects), which axe violations
// fail it (serious and critical, WCAG 2.1 AA), and the summary row of a test. tests/e2e-measure.test.ts pins them;
// e2e/support/measure.ts collects the records in the browser.

export type ConsoleRecord = { kind: "console" | "pageerror"; text: string; url?: string; pageUrl: string }
export type ResponseRecord = { method: string; url: string; status: number; resourceType: string; pageUrl: string }
export type FailureRecord = { method: string; url: string; errorText: string; resourceType: string; pageUrl: string }

// A refusal the test asserts itself (a 403 for a role without the permission, a 409 for a double checkout): it is not
// a failed request, and the browser's console line about it is not a console error.
export type ExpectedRefusal = { method?: string; url: RegExp; status: number; reason: string }

// One reviewed allow-list entry (e2e/support/allow-list.ts): what it lets through, and why that is not the app failing.
export type AllowEntry = {
  id: string
  kind: "console" | "response" | "requestfailed"
  text?: RegExp
  url?: RegExp
  status?: readonly number[]
  errorText?: RegExp
  resourceType?: readonly string[]
  reason: string
  reviewed: string
}

export type AxeViolation = { id: string; impact?: string | null; help: string; helpUrl: string; nodes: readonly { target: readonly unknown[] }[] }

export type PageRecord = {
  label: string
  url: string
  loadMs: number | null
  domContentLoadedMs: number | null
  axe: { serious: number; critical: number; moderate: number; minor: number; failing: { id: string; impact: string; help: string; targets: string[] }[] } | null
}

export type Classified = {
  unexpectedConsole: ConsoleRecord[]
  unexpectedResponses: ResponseRecord[]
  unexpectedFailures: FailureRecord[]
  allowed: { id: string; kind: AllowEntry["kind"]; url?: string; text?: string }[]
  expected: { reason: string; method: string; url: string; status: number }[]
  unmetRefusals: { reason: string; method?: string; url: string; status: number }[]
}

// The browser's own console line for a response with an error status (Chromium and WebKit both write one).
const RESOURCE_ERROR = /^Failed to load resource: the server responded with a status of (\d{3})/

export function refusalMatches(refusal: ExpectedRefusal, response: { method: string; url: string; status: number }): boolean {
  return response.status === refusal.status && (!refusal.method || refusal.method === response.method) && refusal.url.test(response.url)
}

function allowFor(entries: readonly AllowEntry[], kind: AllowEntry["kind"], fields: { text?: string; url?: string; status?: number; errorText?: string; resourceType?: string }) {
  return entries.find(
    (entry) =>
      entry.kind === kind &&
      (!entry.text || (fields.text !== undefined && entry.text.test(fields.text))) &&
      (!entry.url || (fields.url !== undefined && entry.url.test(fields.url))) &&
      (!entry.status || (fields.status !== undefined && entry.status.includes(fields.status))) &&
      (!entry.errorText || (fields.errorText !== undefined && entry.errorText.test(fields.errorText))) &&
      (!entry.resourceType || (fields.resourceType !== undefined && entry.resourceType.includes(fields.resourceType)))
  )
}

export function classify(
  records: { console: readonly ConsoleRecord[]; responses: readonly ResponseRecord[]; failures: readonly FailureRecord[] },
  allowList: readonly AllowEntry[],
  refusals: readonly ExpectedRefusal[]
): Classified {
  const result: Classified = { unexpectedConsole: [], unexpectedResponses: [], unexpectedFailures: [], allowed: [], expected: [], unmetRefusals: [] }
  const met = new Set<ExpectedRefusal>()
  const refusedUrls = new Set<string>()
  const allowedUrls = new Set<string>()
  for (const response of records.responses) {
    const refusal = refusals.find((candidate) => refusalMatches(candidate, response))
    if (refusal) {
      met.add(refusal)
      refusedUrls.add(`${response.status} ${response.url}`)
      result.expected.push({ reason: refusal.reason, method: response.method, url: response.url, status: response.status })
      continue
    }
    const entry = allowFor(allowList, "response", { url: response.url, status: response.status, resourceType: response.resourceType })
    if (entry) {
      allowedUrls.add(`${response.status} ${response.url}`)
      result.allowed.push({ id: entry.id, kind: "response", url: response.url })
      continue
    }
    result.unexpectedResponses.push(response)
  }
  for (const failure of records.failures) {
    const entry = allowFor(allowList, "requestfailed", { url: failure.url, errorText: failure.errorText, resourceType: failure.resourceType })
    if (entry) result.allowed.push({ id: entry.id, kind: "requestfailed", url: failure.url })
    else result.unexpectedFailures.push(failure)
  }
  for (const message of records.console) {
    // The console line of a refused or allowed response goes with that response.
    const status = message.text.match(RESOURCE_ERROR)?.[1]
    if (status && message.url && (refusedUrls.has(`${status} ${message.url}`) || allowedUrls.has(`${status} ${message.url}`))) continue
    const entry = allowFor(allowList, "console", { text: message.text, url: message.url })
    if (entry) result.allowed.push({ id: entry.id, kind: "console", text: message.text })
    else result.unexpectedConsole.push(message)
  }
  result.unmetRefusals = refusals
    .filter((refusal) => !met.has(refusal))
    .map((refusal) => ({ reason: refusal.reason, method: refusal.method, url: String(refusal.url), status: refusal.status }))
  return result
}

// Spec §8: no serious or critical violation on any page a workflow visits; moderate and minor are recorded only.
export function axeCounts(violations: readonly AxeViolation[]): NonNullable<PageRecord["axe"]> {
  const count = (impact: string) => violations.filter((violation) => violation.impact === impact).length
  return {
    serious: count("serious"),
    critical: count("critical"),
    moderate: count("moderate"),
    minor: count("minor"),
    failing: violations
      .filter((violation) => violation.impact === "serious" || violation.impact === "critical")
      .map((violation) => ({
        id: violation.id,
        impact: String(violation.impact),
        help: violation.help,
        targets: violation.nodes.slice(0, 5).map((node) => node.target.map(String).join(" ")),
      })),
  }
}

// The attachment that carries a test's summary row from its fixture to the summary reporter.
export const SUMMARY_ATTACHMENT = "e2e-summary"

// The axe rule set of spec §8: WCAG 2.0 and 2.1, levels A and AA.
export const AXE_TAGS = ["wcag2a", "wcag2aa", "wcag21a", "wcag21aa"] as const

// What fails a test once its steps are done, as one readable list (empty when the measurements pass).
export function measurementProblems(classified: Classified, pages: readonly PageRecord[]): string[] {
  const problems: string[] = []
  for (const message of classified.unexpectedConsole) problems.push(`console ${message.kind}: ${message.text.slice(0, 300)} (on ${message.pageUrl})`)
  for (const response of classified.unexpectedResponses) problems.push(`request ${response.method} ${response.url} answered ${response.status} (on ${response.pageUrl})`)
  for (const failure of classified.unexpectedFailures) problems.push(`request ${failure.method} ${failure.url} failed: ${failure.errorText} (on ${failure.pageUrl})`)
  for (const refusal of classified.unmetRefusals) problems.push(`expected refusal not seen: ${refusal.method ?? "any"} ${refusal.url} ${refusal.status} (${refusal.reason})`)
  for (const page of pages) {
    for (const violation of page.axe?.failing ?? []) problems.push(`axe ${violation.impact} ${violation.id} on ${page.label}: ${violation.help} [${violation.targets.join(" | ")}]`)
  }
  return problems
}

export type TestSummary = {
  title: string
  file: string
  project: string
  device: string
  theme: string
  browser: string
  user: string | null
  status: string
  expectedStatus: string
  durationMs: number
  pages: PageRecord[]
  timings: { label: string; ms: number }[]
  allowed: Classified["allowed"]
  expected: Classified["expected"]
  problems: string[]
  screenshots: string[]
  errors: string[]
}

// The run summary the controller turns into the Thai report: every test with its outcome, and per project the counts.
export function buildRunSummary(runId: string | null, startedAt: string, status: string, tests: readonly TestSummary[]) {
  const projects: Record<string, { passed: number; failed: number; skipped: number; expectedFailures: number }> = {}
  for (const test of tests) {
    const counts = (projects[test.project] ??= { passed: 0, failed: 0, skipped: 0, expectedFailures: 0 })
    if (test.status === "skipped") counts.skipped++
    else if (test.expectedStatus === "failed" && test.status === "failed") counts.expectedFailures++
    else if (test.status === test.expectedStatus) counts.passed++
    else counts.failed++
  }
  return { runId, startedAt, finishedAt: new Date().toISOString(), status, projects, tests }
}
```

  - Create `e2e/support/measure.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// The measurements of one test, collected in the browser (spec §3.3, §8 "Per step"): console errors and page errors,
// responses with an error status and requests that failed, axe on every page the test visits, the page load time, a
// screenshot at the end, and the test's summary row. The rules live in measure-core.ts; the fixture in fixtures.ts
// starts a Measure before the test and finishes it after, so every test is measured.
import AxeBuilder from "@axe-core/playwright"
import type { Page, Request, Response, TestInfo } from "@playwright/test"
import { ALLOW_LIST } from "./allow-list.ts"
import {
  AXE_TAGS,
  axeCounts,
  classify,
  measurementProblems,
  type ConsoleRecord,
  type ExpectedRefusal,
  type FailureRecord,
  type PageRecord,
  type ResponseRecord,
  type TestSummary,
  SUMMARY_ATTACHMENT,
} from "./measure-core.ts"

export class Measure {
  readonly page: Page
  readonly testInfo: TestInfo
  readonly context: { device: string; theme: string; browser: string; user: string | null }
  readonly consoleRecords: ConsoleRecord[] = []
  readonly responses: ResponseRecord[] = []
  readonly failures: FailureRecord[] = []
  readonly pages: PageRecord[] = []
  readonly timings: { label: string; ms: number }[] = []
  readonly refusals: ExpectedRefusal[] = []
  readonly screenshots: string[] = []

  constructor(page: Page, testInfo: TestInfo, context: Measure["context"]) {
    this.page = page
    this.testInfo = testInfo
    this.context = context
  }

  start() {
    this.page.on("console", (message) => {
      if (message.type() !== "error") return
      this.consoleRecords.push({ kind: "console", text: message.text(), url: message.location().url || undefined, pageUrl: this.page.url() })
    })
    this.page.on("pageerror", (error) => {
      this.consoleRecords.push({ kind: "pageerror", text: `${error.name}: ${error.message}`, pageUrl: this.page.url() })
    })
    this.page.on("response", (response: Response) => {
      if (response.status() < 400) return
      const request = response.request()
      this.responses.push({ method: request.method(), url: response.url(), status: response.status(), resourceType: request.resourceType(), pageUrl: this.page.url() })
    })
    this.page.on("requestfailed", (request: Request) => {
      this.failures.push({
        method: request.method(),
        url: request.url(),
        errorText: request.failure()?.errorText ?? "unknown",
        resourceType: request.resourceType(),
        pageUrl: this.page.url(),
      })
    })
  }

  // A refusal the test itself asserts (spec §3.3: "other than the expected refusals"): call it before the step.
  expectRefusal(refusal: ExpectedRefusal) {
    this.refusals.push(refusal)
  }

  // Open a page as a person does: navigate, wait for it to load, record its load time, and run axe on it.
  async visit(path: string, label = path): Promise<PageRecord> {
    await this.page.goto(path, { waitUntil: "load" })
    return this.recordPage(label)
  }

  // Record the page on screen now (after a client-side navigation or with a dialog open): timing when it is a fresh
  // document, and axe.
  async recordPage(label: string): Promise<PageRecord> {
    const timing = await this.page.evaluate(() => {
      const [navigation] = performance.getEntriesByType("navigation") as PerformanceNavigationTiming[]
      if (!navigation || navigation.loadEventEnd === 0) return null
      return { loadMs: Math.round(navigation.loadEventEnd - navigation.startTime), domContentLoadedMs: Math.round(navigation.domContentLoadedEventEnd - navigation.startTime) }
    })
    const record: PageRecord = { label, url: this.page.url(), loadMs: timing?.loadMs ?? null, domContentLoadedMs: timing?.domContentLoadedMs ?? null, axe: null }
    const results = await new AxeBuilder({ page: this.page }).withTags([...AXE_TAGS]).analyze()
    record.axe = axeCounts(results.violations)
    this.pages.push(record)
    return record
  }

  // Time a step that is not a page load (a client-side navigation, a save): recorded, never asserted (spec §8).
  async timed<T>(label: string, step: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    const started = Date.now()
    const result = await step()
    this.timings.push({ label, ms: Date.now() - started })
    return result
  }

  async screenshot(name: string) {
    const file = this.testInfo.outputPath(`${name}.png`)
    await this.page.screenshot({ path: file, fullPage: true })
    this.screenshots.push(file)
    await this.testInfo.attach(name, { path: file, contentType: "image/png" })
  }

  // After the test: the end screenshot (spec §8: at each workflow's end and on any failure), the summary row, then the
  // measurements' verdict. A problem fails the test, with every problem listed.
  async finish() {
    if (!this.page.isClosed()) await this.screenshot("end").catch(() => undefined)
    const classified = classify({ console: this.consoleRecords, responses: this.responses, failures: this.failures }, ALLOW_LIST, this.refusals)
    const problems = measurementProblems(classified, this.pages)
    const summary: Omit<TestSummary, "status" | "expectedStatus" | "durationMs" | "errors"> = {
      title: this.testInfo.titlePath.slice(1).join(" › "),
      file: this.testInfo.file,
      project: this.testInfo.project.name,
      device: this.context.device,
      theme: this.context.theme,
      browser: this.context.browser,
      user: this.context.user,
      pages: this.pages,
      timings: this.timings,
      allowed: classified.allowed,
      expected: classified.expected,
      problems,
      screenshots: this.screenshots,
    }
    await this.testInfo.attach(SUMMARY_ATTACHMENT, { body: JSON.stringify(summary), contentType: "application/json" })
    if (problems.length > 0) throw new Error(`Measurements failed (${problems.length}):\n- ${problems.join("\n- ")}`)
  }
}
```

```ts
// The reviewed allow-list (spec §8: "none allowed, except a short reviewed allow-list"; §10: each entry reviewed and
// justified). An entry lets one kind of record through when every field it names matches; its reason says why the
// record is not the app failing, and reviewed is the day a person (or the controller) checked it. Keep it short: a new
// entry needs the same review, and tests/e2e-measure.test.ts checks every entry has both.
import type { AllowEntry } from "./measure-core.ts"

export const ALLOW_LIST: readonly AllowEntry[] = [
  {
    id: "next-prefetch-cancelled",
    kind: "requestfailed",
    url: /[?&]_rsc=/,
    errorText: /^(?:net::ERR_ABORTED|cancelled|Load request cancelled)$/,
    reason:
      "Next.js prefetches the routes of the links on screen (RSC payloads, ?_rsc=) and cancels the prefetches it no longer needs; Chromium reports each cancelled one as net::ERR_ABORTED. A cancelled prefetch is the router's choice, not a failed request; a prefetch answered with an error status is still a failure (a response record).",
    reviewed: "2026-10-19",
  },
]
```

```ts
// The suite's test object: every spec imports test and expect from here, never from @playwright/test. It gives each
// test:
// - the project's device and theme (playwright.config.ts sets e2eDevice and e2eTheme from e2e/support/matrix.ts);
// - its browser: Chromium, or WebKit on the phone for a file that calls runPhoneOnWebKit() (spec §8), with the
//   phone's user agent of that browser;
// - the theme cookie, set before the first page (spec §8 "Themes"), and the matching color scheme;
// - its measurements (measure.ts), started before it and checked after it.
// The fixtures name their callback provide, not use: the React hooks lint reads a call of use as a hook.
import { test as base, expect } from "@playwright/test"
import { browserFor, phoneUserAgent, type E2eBrowser, type E2eTestOptions, type E2eWorkerOptions } from "./matrix.ts"
import { Measure } from "./measure.ts"

export const test = base.extend<E2eTestOptions & { measure: Measure }, E2eWorkerOptions>({
  e2eDevice: ["desktop", { option: true, scope: "worker" }],
  webkitOnPhone: [false, { option: true, scope: "worker" }],
  e2eTheme: ["light", { option: true }],
  browserName: [async ({ e2eDevice, webkitOnPhone }, provide) => provide(browserFor(e2eDevice, webkitOnPhone)), { scope: "worker" }],
  userAgent: async ({ e2eDevice, browserName }, provide) => provide(phoneUserAgent(e2eDevice, browserName as E2eBrowser)),
  colorScheme: async ({ e2eTheme }, provide) => provide(e2eTheme),
  context: async ({ context, e2eTheme, baseURL }, provide) => {
    if (!baseURL) throw new Error("playwright.config.ts sets use.baseURL")
    await context.addCookies([{ name: "theme", value: e2eTheme, url: baseURL }])
    await provide(context)
  },
  measure: [
    async ({ page, e2eDevice, e2eTheme, browserName }, provide, testInfo) => {
      const measure = new Measure(page, testInfo, { device: e2eDevice, theme: e2eTheme, browser: browserName, user: null })
      measure.start()
      await provide(measure)
      await measure.finish()
    },
    { auto: true },
  ],
})

export { expect }

// The file's phone runs use WebKit (spec §8: the field audit walk and the desk delivery); its desktop runs stay on
// Chromium. Call it at the top level of the spec file: the browser is a worker option.
export function runPhoneOnWebKit() {
  test.use({ webkitOnPhone: true })
}
```

  - Create `e2e/support/summary-reporter.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// Collects every test's summary row (the e2e-summary attachment of measure.ts) into one file, e2e-results/summary.json
// (spec §8 "The report": the file the controller turns into the Thai report), and prints one line per project.
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs"
import path from "node:path"
import type { FullResult, Reporter, TestCase, TestResult } from "@playwright/test/reporter"
import { buildRunSummary, SUMMARY_ATTACHMENT, type TestSummary } from "./measure-core.ts"
import { fromRoot, RUN_FILE, SUMMARY_FILE } from "./paths.ts"
import { readRunState } from "./state.ts"

export default class SummaryReporter implements Reporter {
  readonly startedAt = new Date().toISOString()
  readonly tests: TestSummary[] = []

  onTestEnd(test: TestCase, result: TestResult) {
    const attachment = result.attachments.find((item) => item.name === SUMMARY_ATTACHMENT && item.body)
    const measured = attachment?.body ? (JSON.parse(attachment.body.toString("utf8")) as Partial<TestSummary>) : {}
    const project = test.parent.project()?.name ?? ""
    this.tests.push({
      title: measured.title ?? test.titlePath().slice(3).join(" › "),
      file: path.relative(process.cwd(), test.location.file),
      project,
      device: measured.device ?? "",
      theme: measured.theme ?? "",
      browser: measured.browser ?? "",
      user: measured.user ?? null,
      status: result.status,
      expectedStatus: test.expectedStatus,
      durationMs: result.duration,
      pages: measured.pages ?? [],
      timings: measured.timings ?? [],
      allowed: measured.allowed ?? [],
      expected: measured.expected ?? [],
      problems: measured.problems ?? [],
      screenshots: (measured.screenshots ?? []).map((file) => path.relative(process.cwd(), file)),
      errors: result.errors.map((error) => (error.message ?? String(error.value ?? "")).split("\n")[0].slice(0, 500)),
    })
  }

  onEnd(result: FullResult) {
    // A listing (--list) or a run stopped before any test leaves the last summary as it was.
    if (this.tests.length === 0) return
    const runId = existsSync(fromRoot(RUN_FILE)) ? readRunState().runId : null
    const summary = buildRunSummary(runId, this.startedAt, result.status, this.tests)
    const file = fromRoot(SUMMARY_FILE)
    mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true })
    writeFileSync(file, `${JSON.stringify(summary, null, 2)}\n`)
    for (const [project, counts] of Object.entries(summary.projects)) {
      console.log(`E2E ${project}: ${counts.passed} passed, ${counts.failed} failed, ${counts.expectedFailures} expected to fail, ${counts.skipped} skipped`)
    }
    console.log(`E2E summary (run ${runId ?? "none"}): ${SUMMARY_FILE}`)
  }

  printsToStdio() {
    return false
  }
}
```

```ts
// The smoke check of one E2E user: the run's session signs them in with no login, "/th" sends them to their landing
// page (src/lib/default-home.ts), the page carries the project's theme, and the measurements of the visit pass.
import type { Page } from "@playwright/test"
import { expect } from "./fixtures.ts"
import type { E2eTheme } from "./matrix.ts"
import type { Measure } from "./measure.ts"
import { E2E_USER_PLANS, type E2eUserKey } from "./run-plan.ts"
import { readRunData } from "./state.ts"

export async function expectLanding(page: Page, measure: Measure, key: E2eUserKey, theme: E2eTheme) {
  const user = readRunData().users[key]
  if (!user) throw new Error(`the run has no ${key} user: the setup project did not finish`)
  const landing = await measure.visit("/th", `${key} landing`)
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`${E2E_USER_PLANS[key].landingPath}(?:[?#]|$)`))
  expect(landing.loadMs, "the landing page's load time is recorded").not.toBeNull()
  await expect(page.locator("html")).toHaveAttribute("data-theme", theme)
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toBeVisible()
  // The session is this user's (the display name carries the run id).
  const session = (await (await page.request.get("/api/auth/session")).json()) as { user?: { name?: string } }
  expect(session.user?.name).toBe(user.displayName)
}
```

  - Replace `e2e/support/fixtures.ts` (a file EF4 created) with exactly (it adds the `e2eUser` option, the `storageState` it gives, the user in the measurements, and `asUser`):

```ts
// The suite's test object: every spec imports test and expect from here, never from @playwright/test. It gives each
// test:
// - the project's device and theme (playwright.config.ts sets e2eDevice and e2eTheme from e2e/support/matrix.ts);
// - its browser: Chromium, or WebKit on the phone for a file that calls runPhoneOnWebKit() (spec §8), with the
//   phone's user agent of that browser;
// - the theme cookie, set before the first page (spec §8 "Themes"), and the matching color scheme;
// - the signed-in user of its file (asUser), from the run's storage states, so no test signs in;
// - its measurements (measure.ts), started before it and checked after it.
// The fixtures name their callback provide, not use: the React hooks lint reads a call of use as a hook.
import { test as base, expect } from "@playwright/test"
import { browserFor, phoneUserAgent, type E2eBrowser, type E2eTestOptions, type E2eWorkerOptions } from "./matrix.ts"
import { Measure } from "./measure.ts"
import { authStateFile, fromRoot } from "./paths.ts"
import type { E2eUserKey } from "./run-plan.ts"
import { readRunState } from "./state.ts"

type SuiteTestOptions = E2eTestOptions & { e2eUser: E2eUserKey | null }

export const test = base.extend<SuiteTestOptions & { measure: Measure }, E2eWorkerOptions>({
  e2eDevice: ["desktop", { option: true, scope: "worker" }],
  webkitOnPhone: [false, { option: true, scope: "worker" }],
  e2eTheme: ["light", { option: true }],
  e2eUser: [null, { option: true }],
  browserName: [async ({ e2eDevice, webkitOnPhone }, provide) => provide(browserFor(e2eDevice, webkitOnPhone)), { scope: "worker" }],
  userAgent: async ({ e2eDevice, browserName }, provide) => provide(phoneUserAgent(e2eDevice, browserName as E2eBrowser)),
  colorScheme: async ({ e2eTheme }, provide) => provide(e2eTheme),
  storageState: async ({ e2eUser }, provide) => provide(e2eUser ? fromRoot(authStateFile(readRunState().runId, e2eUser)) : undefined),
  context: async ({ context, e2eTheme, baseURL }, provide) => {
    if (!baseURL) throw new Error("playwright.config.ts sets use.baseURL")
    await context.addCookies([{ name: "theme", value: e2eTheme, url: baseURL }])
    await provide(context)
  },
  measure: [
    async ({ page, e2eDevice, e2eTheme, browserName, e2eUser }, provide, testInfo) => {
      const measure = new Measure(page, testInfo, { device: e2eDevice, theme: e2eTheme, browser: browserName, user: e2eUser })
      measure.start()
      await provide(measure)
      await measure.finish()
    },
    { auto: true },
  ],
})

export { expect }

// The file's phone runs use WebKit (spec §8: the field audit walk and the desk delivery); its desktop runs stay on
// Chromium. Call it at the top level of the spec file: the browser is a worker option.
export function runPhoneOnWebKit() {
  test.use({ webkitOnPhone: true })
}

// The file's tests run as this E2E user (spec §7), signed in through the run's storage state: no test signs in.
export function asUser(user: E2eUserKey) {
  test.use({ e2eUser: user })
}
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import test from "node:test"

// The E2E suite's asset transaction area (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-e2e-workflow-suite-design.md §6.1):
// the pure plan and the known findings are loaded; the specs, which run under Playwright, are read as text.
const read = (file: string) => readFileSync(file, "utf8").replace(/\r/g, "")

// Every transaction spec runs as IT staff, takes test from the suite's fixtures (measured, themed, signed in) and never
// from @playwright/test.
function readTxSpec(file: string): string {
  const source = read(file)
  assert.ok(source.split("\n").includes(`asUser("itStaff")`), `${file}: as IT staff`)
  assert.match(source, /from "\.\.\/support\/fixtures\.ts"/, `${file}: the suite's test`)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /from "@playwright\/test"/, `${file}: not Playwright's own test`)
  return source
}

// The expected-to-fail tests of a file, by the finding each names.
function expectedFailures(source: string): string[] {
  return [...source.matchAll(/test\.fail\(true, "(TX-A\d+): /g)].map((match) => match[1])
}

test("an asset registered on the form with a photo and a purchase document, a wrong entry first; the area's records carry the run and the project (e2e transactions task 1, TX1)", async () => {
  const plan = await import("../e2e/transactions/tx-plan.ts")
  const { E2E_PROJECTS } = await import("../e2e/support/matrix.ts")
  // One code per project of the foundation's matrix, so the four projects never share an asset.
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(plan.PROJECT_CODES), E2E_PROJECTS.map((project) => project.name))
  assert.equal(new Set(Object.values(plan.PROJECT_CODES)).size, 4)
  assert.equal(plan.txAssetTag("E2E-1019T2329", "phone-dark", "REG"), "E2E-1019T2329-PD-REG")
  assert.equal(plan.txAssetName("E2E-1019A", "desktop-light", "โน้ตบุ๊ก"), "E2E-1019A DL โน้ตบุ๊ก")
  assert.ok(plan.txAssetTag("E2E-1019T2329", "desktop-light", "BM3").length <= 50)
  assert.throws(() => plan.txAssetTag("E2E-1019A", "setup", "X"), /four device × theme projects/)
  assert.deepEqual(plan.secondLocation("E2E-1019A"), { code: "E2E-1019A-L2", name: "E2E-1019A ห้องปลายทาง E2E", locationType: "Room" })
  // Asia/Bangkok is UTC+7: 17:30 UTC is already the next day there.
  assert.equal(plan.bangkokDate(0, new Date("2026-10-19T17:30:00Z")), "2026-10-20")
  assert.equal(plan.bangkokDate(-1, new Date("2026-10-19T10:00:00Z")), "2026-10-18")
  assert.equal(plan.bangkokDate(7, new Date("2026-10-19T10:00:00Z")), "2026-10-26")
  assert.deepEqual(plan.PHONE_KEYBOARD_OPEN, { width: 390, height: 360 })

  // A known finding drops only its own rule on its own element, keeps axe's counts, and says so on the test.
  const known = await import("../e2e/transactions/known-findings.ts")
  const violation = (target: string) => ({ id: "svg-img-alt", impact: "serious", help: "", targets: [target] })
  const record = {
    label: "asset page",
    url: "/th/assets/1",
    loadMs: 1,
    domContentLoadedMs: 1,
    axe: { serious: 2, critical: 0, moderate: 0, minor: 0, failing: [violation(`svg[height="160"]`), violation("svg.chart")] },
  }
  const annotations: { type: string; description?: string }[] = []
  known.dropKnownAxe(record, known.KNOWN_AXE, { annotations } as never)
  assert.deepEqual(record.axe.failing.map((item) => item.targets[0]), ["svg.chart"])
  assert.equal(record.axe.serious, 2, "axe's counts stay in the summary")
  assert.match(annotations[0]?.description ?? "", /^TX-A2: /)
  // Every known finding has its expected-to-fail test.
  const findings = readTxSpec("e2e/transactions/findings.spec.ts")
  for (const entry of known.KNOWN_AXE) assert.ok(expectedFailures(findings).includes(entry.finding), entry.finding)

  // The register's thumbnails (the shared allow-list, ruling TX-R9): a 500 or a cancelled image of
  // /api/attachments/<id>/thumbnail passes; the same answer of the attachment itself does not.
  const core = await import("../e2e/support/measure-core.ts")
  const { ALLOW_LIST } = await import("../e2e/support/allow-list.ts")
  const register = "http://localhost:3100/th/assets"
  const attachment = "http://localhost:3100/api/attachments/23f49e63-83eb-44fb-ba55-4f77bef5b552"
  const classified = core.classify(
    {
      console: [],
      responses: [
        { method: "GET", url: `${attachment}/thumbnail`, status: 500, resourceType: "image", pageUrl: register },
        { method: "GET", url: attachment, status: 500, resourceType: "image", pageUrl: register },
      ],
      failures: [{ method: "GET", url: `${attachment}/thumbnail`, errorText: "net::ERR_ABORTED", resourceType: "image", pageUrl: register }],
    },
    ALLOW_LIST,
    [],
  )
  assert.deepEqual(classified.allowed.map((item) => item.id), ["foreign-attachment-thumbnail", "thumbnail-cancelled-on-leave"])
  assert.deepEqual(classified.unexpectedResponses.map((response) => response.url), [attachment])

  // The register spec: the wrong entry first (no room), the photo and the PDF, the save bar with the keyboard open.
  const spec = readTxSpec("e2e/transactions/register-asset.spec.ts")
  assert.match(spec, /await expectFieldError\(page, "asset-current-location", "กรุณาระบุข้อมูลนี้"\)/)
  assert.match(spec, /expect\(posted, "the wrong entry posts nothing"\)\.toBe\(0\)/)
  assert.match(spec, /setInputFiles\(PDF_FIXTURE\)/)
  assert.match(spec, /setInputFiles\(PHOTO_FIXTURE\)/)
  assert.match(spec, /if \(e2eDevice === "phone"\) await openPhoneKeyboard\(page\)/)
  assert.match(spec, /await expectReachable\(save\)/)
  // Every measured page of the area goes through the settle and the known list, never the bare Measure calls.
  const support = read("e2e/transactions/tx-support.ts")
  assert.match(support, /return dropKnownAxe\(await measure\.recordPage\(label\), KNOWN_AXE, testInfo\)/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(spec, /measure\.(visit|recordPage)\(/)
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** (exact; every file with a file tool, LF):

  - Create `e2e/transactions/tx-plan.ts` with exactly:

```ts
// The asset transaction area's records (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-e2e-workflow-suite-design.md §6.1), as
// plain data and pure functions: every asset tag and name carries the run id and the project's code, so the four
// projects never share an asset (spec §11 "Run order") and repeat runs never collide on a tag (spec §5). Imports
// nothing, so tests/e2e-transactions.test.ts (node --test) can load it.

// The project's two letters in a tag: desktop or phone, light or dark.
export const PROJECT_CODES: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = {
  "desktop-light": "DL",
  "desktop-dark": "DD",
  "phone-light": "PL",
  "phone-dark": "PD",
}

export function projectCode(projectName: string): string {
  const code = PROJECT_CODES[projectName]
  if (!code) throw new Error(`the transaction specs run in the four device × theme projects, not in ${projectName}`)
  return code
}

// E2E-1019N-DL-HO1: the run, the project, the workflow's key. The tag column holds 50 characters.
export function txAssetTag(runId: string, projectName: string, key: string): string {
  const tag = `${runId}-${projectCode(projectName)}-${key}`
  if (tag.length > 50) throw new Error(`asset tag ${tag} is longer than 50 characters`)
  return tag
}

// E2E-1019N DL โน้ตบุ๊กส่งมอบ: the marker a person sees in any list (spec §5).
export function txAssetName(runId: string, projectName: string, label: string): string {
  return `${runId} ${projectCode(projectName)} ${label}`
}

// The second room of the run, where returns, transfers and bulk moves take an asset (the setup's room is the first).
export function secondLocation(runId: string) {
  return { code: `${runId}-L2`, name: `${runId} ห้องปลายทาง E2E`, locationType: "Room" as const }
}

// A date input's value (YYYY-MM-DD) in the app's time zone, Asia/Bangkok (UTC+7, no daylight saving), days from now.
export function bangkokDate(offsetDays = 0, now: Date = new Date()): string {
  const bangkok = new Date(now.getTime() + 7 * 60 * 60 * 1000 + offsetDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)
  return bangkok.toISOString().slice(0, 10)
}

// The phone with the on-screen keyboard open (spec §8 "the keyboard-open height for the forms with a sticky save
// bar"): 390 wide, 360 of the 664 visible points left above the keyboard.
export const PHONE_KEYBOARD_OPEN = { width: 390, height: 360 } as const
```

  - Create `e2e/transactions/known-findings.ts` with exactly (TX2 and TX3 replace it, each adding one finding):

```ts
// App findings of the transaction rehearsal that every visit of a page would trip (details and evidence in the plan's
// findings file). Each one has its own expected-to-fail test in findings.spec.ts, which passes, and so is
// reported, once the app is fixed; until then a workflow test that visits the page drops exactly that violation (the
// rule on its known element), so the workflow's other measurements still count. Any other violation still fails.
import type { TestInfo } from "@playwright/test"
import type { PageRecord } from "../support/measure-core.ts"

export type KnownAxe = { finding: string; rule: string; target: RegExp; why: string }

// TX-A2: the asset page's QR code (qrcode.react's QRCodeSVG draws role="img" and the app gives it no title).
export const QR_WITHOUT_NAME: KnownAxe = {
  finding: "TX-A2",
  rule: "svg-img-alt",
  target: /^svg\[height="160"\]$/,
  why: "the asset page's QR code is an image with no text alternative",
}

export const KNOWN_AXE: readonly KnownAxe[] = [QR_WITHOUT_NAME]

// Drop the known violations from a recorded page (the counts stay as axe gave them, so the summary still shows them),
// and say so on the test.
export function dropKnownAxe(record: PageRecord, known: readonly KnownAxe[], testInfo: TestInfo): PageRecord {
  if (!record.axe) return record
  record.axe.failing = record.axe.failing.filter((violation) => {
    const match = known.find((entry) => entry.rule === violation.id && violation.targets.every((target) => entry.target.test(target)))
    if (!match) return true
    testInfo.annotations.push({ type: "known app finding", description: `${match.finding}: ${match.why} (axe ${violation.id} on ${record.label})` })
    return false
  })
  return record
}
```
