```ts
// The asset transaction area's browser helpers (spec §6.1). Each test builds its own E2E assets through the app as the
// signed-in IT staff (the asset API the form posts to), walks the workflow on the form, and reads the outcome back in
// the UI and with GETs. The run's second room is made once, as the seed admin, through the location API (spec §7: the
// data comes from the app's own APIs, never from SQL).
import { request, type Locator, type Page, type TestInfo } from "@playwright/test"
import { expect } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import type { E2eDevice } from "../support/matrix.ts"
import type { Measure } from "../support/measure.ts"
import { ADMIN_STATE_FILE, fromRoot } from "../support/paths.ts"
import { readRunData, readRunState } from "../support/state.ts"
import { dropKnownAxe, KNOWN_AXE } from "./known-findings.ts"
import { bangkokDate, PHONE_KEYBOARD_OPEN, secondLocation, txAssetName, txAssetTag } from "./tx-plan.ts"

export const PHOTO_FIXTURE = fromRoot("e2e/fixtures/photo.png")
export const PDF_FIXTURE = fromRoot("e2e/fixtures/evidence.pdf")

// The master data the new asset form offers (ids by their Thai labels): read from the form itself, which IT staff may
// open, since the status and condition masters have no API of their own.
export type AssetMasters = { categoryId: string; readyStatusId: string; goodConditionId: string }

export async function readAssetMasters(page: Page): Promise<AssetMasters> {
  await page.goto("/th/assets/new")
  const options = async (id: string) =>
    page.locator(`#${id} option`).evaluateAll((elements) => elements.map((element) => ({ value: (element as HTMLOptionElement).value, label: element.textContent?.trim() ?? "" })))
  const categories = await options("asset-category")
  const statuses = await options("asset-status")
  const conditions = await options("asset-condition")
  const category = categories.find((option) => /คอมพิวเตอร์พกพา/.test(option.label))
  const ready = statuses.find((option) => option.label === "พร้อมใช้งาน")
  const good = conditions.find((option) => option.label === "ดี")
  if (!category?.value || !ready?.value || !good?.value) throw new Error("the new asset form offers no Notebook category, Ready status or Good condition")
  return { categoryId: category.value, readyStatusId: ready.value, goodConditionId: good.value }
}

export type CreatedAsset = { id: string; assetTag: string; name: string }

// The new asset form's empty values (src/components/assets/asset-form.tsx emptyAsset).
const EMPTY_ASSET_BODY = {
  assetTag: "", name: "", categoryId: "", brandId: "", modelId: "", serialNumber: "", licenseTotalSeats: "", licenseUsedSeats: "",
  licenseAssignedAssetId: "", companyId: "", branchId: "", ownershipType: "personal", departmentId: "", custodianId: "",
  homeLocationId: "", currentLocationId: "", statusId: "", conditionId: "", purchaseDate: "", purchasePrice: "", supplierId: "",
  warrantyStartDate: "", warrantyEndDate: "", fixedAssetCode: "", poNumber: "", invoiceNumber: "", remark: "", customFieldsJson: "",
  isActive: true,
}

// One personal E2E asset, Ready, in the run's department and first room, through the asset API as the signed-in user.
export async function createAsset(page: Page, masters: AssetMasters, tag: string, name: string): Promise<CreatedAsset> {
  const data = readRunData()
  // Every key of the form's body, as the form sends it: the schema reads a missing optional key as missing (N3).
  const response = await page.request.post("/api/assets", {
    data: {
      ...EMPTY_ASSET_BODY,
      assetTag: tag,
      name,
      categoryId: masters.categoryId,
      ownershipType: "personal",
      companyId: data.companyId,
      branchId: data.branchId,
      departmentId: data.departmentId,
      currentLocationId: data.locationId,
      homeLocationId: data.locationId,
      statusId: masters.readyStatusId,
      conditionId: masters.goodConditionId,
      isActive: true,
    },
  })
  expect(response.status(), `POST /api/assets ${tag}: ${(await response.text()).slice(0, 300)}`).toBe(201)
  const asset = (await response.json()) as { id: string; assetTag: string }
  return { id: asset.id, assetTag: asset.assetTag, name }
}

// The project's asset for one workflow step: E2E-<run>-<project>-<key>.
export async function createTxAsset(page: Page, masters: AssetMasters, projectName: string, key: string, label: string) {
  const { runId } = readRunState()
  return createAsset(page, masters, txAssetTag(runId, projectName, key), txAssetName(runId, projectName, label))
}

// A permanent handover through the checkout API (the form's own route, JSON body), for the workflows that start from
// an asset already handed over (return, cancel).
export async function handoverByApi(page: Page, assetId: string, custodianId: string, conditionId: string) {
  const response = await page.request.post(`/api/assets/${assetId}/checkout`, {
    data: {
      // Every key of the form's body (N3): the schema reads a missing optional key as missing.
      handoverMode: "permanent_assignment",
      checkoutType: "user",
      custodianId,
      departmentId: "",
      locationId: "",
      parentAssetId: "",
      checkoutDate: bangkokDate(),
      expectedReturnDate: "",
      conditionBefore: conditionId,
      remark: "E2E setup handover",
      receiverSignature: "",
    },
  })
  expect(response.status(), `POST /api/assets/${assetId}/checkout: ${(await response.text()).slice(0, 300)}`).toBe(201)
  return (await response.json()) as { id: string; documentNo: string }
}

export type AssetRead = {
  id: string
  assetTag: string
  status: { name: string; nameTh: string }
  custodianId: string | null
  currentLocationId: string
  custodian: { code: string; fullNameTh: string } | null
  currentLocation: { code: string; name: string } | null
}

export async function readAsset(page: Page, assetId: string): Promise<AssetRead> {
  const response = await page.request.get(`/api/assets/${assetId}`)
  expect(response.status(), `GET /api/assets/${assetId}`).toBe(200)
  return (await response.json()) as AssetRead
}

// The run's second room, created once per run as the seed admin (found again by its code on every later call).
export async function ensureSecondLocation(): Promise<{ id: string; code: string; name: string }> {
  const { runId, baseURL } = readRunState()
  const planned = secondLocation(runId)
  const admin = await request.newContext({ baseURL, storageState: fromRoot(ADMIN_STATE_FILE) })
  try {
    const found = (await (await admin.get(`/api/locations?search=${encodeURIComponent(planned.code)}`)).json()) as { id: string; code: string; name: string }[]
    const existing = found.find((location) => location.code === planned.code)
    if (existing) return existing
    const response = await admin.post("/api/locations", { data: { ...planned, branchId: readRunData().branchId, isActive: true } })
    if (response.status() !== 201) throw new Error(`POST /api/locations ${planned.code} answered ${response.status()}: ${(await response.text()).slice(0, 300)}`)
    return (await response.json()) as { id: string; code: string; name: string }
  } finally {
    await admin.dispose()
  }
}

// A SearchableSelect (a button that opens a searchable list), by its id or its locator: open it, type, pick the option.
export async function pickSearchable(page: Page, trigger: string | Locator, search: string, option: string | RegExp) {
  const button = typeof trigger === "string" ? page.locator(`#${trigger}`) : trigger
  await button.click()
  const input = page.getByRole("dialog").last().getByRole("combobox")
  await input.fill(search)
  await page.getByRole("option", { name: option }).first().click()
  await expect(button).toContainText(typeof option === "string" ? option : search)
}

// The field's error under it (the V track): its text, in the element the field names with aria-describedby.
export async function expectFieldError(page: Page, fieldId: string, message: string | RegExp) {
  await expect(page.locator(`#${fieldId}-error`)).toHaveText(message)
}

// One stroke on a signature pad: the mouse on the desktop, a finger on the phone (spec §6.1.2). Playwright 1.61.1 has
// no touch drag, so the finger is Chromium's own touch input (CDP Input.dispatchTouchEvent); it reaches the pad as
// pointer events of type "touch", as a real finger does. The pad's clear button turns on once it holds a stroke.
export async function signOnPad(page: Page, group: Locator, device: E2eDevice, browser: string) {
  const canvas = group.locator("canvas")
  await canvas.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded()
  await canvas.evaluate((element) => {
    const seen: string[] = []
    ;(element as HTMLCanvasElement & { __e2ePointerTypes?: string[] }).__e2ePointerTypes = seen
    element.addEventListener("pointerdown", (event) => seen.push((event as PointerEvent).pointerType), { capture: true })
  })
  const box = await canvas.boundingBox()
  if (!box) throw new Error("the signature pad has no box on screen")
  const points = [0.15, 0.3, 0.45, 0.6, 0.75].map((share, index) => ({ x: box.x + box.width * share, y: box.y + box.height * (index % 2 === 0 ? 0.35 : 0.65) }))
  if (device === "desktop") {
    await page.mouse.move(points[0].x, points[0].y)
    await page.mouse.down()
    for (const point of points.slice(1)) await page.mouse.move(point.x, point.y, { steps: 4 })
    await page.mouse.up()
  } else {
    if (browser !== "chromium") throw new Error("a finger stroke needs Chromium's touch input (Playwright has no touch drag on WebKit)")
    const cdp = await page.context().newCDPSession(page)
    await cdp.send("Input.dispatchTouchEvent", { type: "touchStart", touchPoints: [points[0]] })
    for (const point of points.slice(1)) await cdp.send("Input.dispatchTouchEvent", { type: "touchMove", touchPoints: [point] })
    await cdp.send("Input.dispatchTouchEvent", { type: "touchEnd", touchPoints: [] })
    await cdp.detach()
    // Emulation, not the app (ruling TX-R4): after a touch drag sent through this second CDP session, Chromium drops
    // the click of Playwright's next tap (a plain page with no app code does the same). One tap on the pad's title takes
    // that lost click, so the person's next tap counts.
    await group.locator("div[id]").first().tap()
  }
  const pointerTypes = await canvas.evaluate((element) => (element as HTMLCanvasElement & { __e2ePointerTypes?: string[] }).__e2ePointerTypes ?? [])
  expect(pointerTypes, "the stroke reached the pad as the device's own pointer").toEqual([device === "desktop" ? "mouse" : "touch"])
  await expect(group.getByRole("button", { name: "ล้างลายเซ็น" })).toBeEnabled()
}

// The phone with the keyboard open: the visible height left above it (spec §8), and back.
export async function openPhoneKeyboard(page: Page) {
  await page.setViewportSize(PHONE_KEYBOARD_OPEN)
}
export async function closePhoneKeyboard(page: Page) {
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 390, height: 664 })
}

// A control a person can reach: scrolled into the visible area, and the topmost element at its centre (no bar, sheet
// or keyboard-sized gap covers it).
export async function expectReachable(locator: Locator) {
  await locator.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded()
  await expect(locator).toBeInViewport()
  const onTop = await locator.evaluate((element) => {
    const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect()
    const hit = document.elementFromPoint(rect.left + rect.width / 2, rect.top + rect.height / 2)
    return Boolean(hit && (hit === element || element.contains(hit)))
  })
  expect(onTop, "nothing covers the control").toBe(true)
}

// The print page's print button calls window.print(); the stub counts the calls (headless has no print dialog).
export async function expectPrints(page: Page, buttonName: string) {
  await page.evaluate(() => {
    const target = window as Window & { __e2ePrints?: number }
    target.__e2ePrints = 0
    window.print = () => {
      target.__e2ePrints = (target.__e2ePrints ?? 0) + 1
    }
  })
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: buttonName }).click()
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as Window & { __e2ePrints?: number }).__e2ePrints)).toBe(1)
}

// The area's measured pages (spec §8). The page settles first: axe reads a toast or dialog still fading in as low
// contrast, so it waits until no finite animation runs (a spinner's endless one does not count). Then axe, less the known
// findings of known-findings.ts on their own elements; any other violation still fails the test.
export async function settle(page: Page) {
  await page.waitForFunction(() =>
    document.getAnimations().every((animation) => animation.playState !== "running" || animation.effect?.getComputedTiming().iterations === Infinity),
  )
}
export async function record(measure: Measure, label: string, testInfo: TestInfo) {
  await settle(measure.page)
  return dropKnownAxe(await measure.recordPage(label), KNOWN_AXE, testInfo)
}
// Measure.visit's steps (the page loaded, then recorded), with the settle and the known findings.
export async function visit(measure: Measure, path: string, label: string, testInfo: TestInfo) {
  await measure.page.goto(path, { waitUntil: "load" })
  return record(measure, label, testInfo)
}
export async function visitAssetPage(measure: Measure, assetId: string, label: string, testInfo: TestInfo, view?: "custody" | "operations" | "audit") {
  return visit(measure, `/th/assets/${assetId}${view ? `?view=${view}` : ""}`, label, testInfo)
}
```

```ts
// The transaction rehearsal's axe findings, one expected-to-fail test each (known-findings.ts): the workflow tests drop
// exactly these violations, and these tests keep them in view. Each passes once the app is fixed, and Playwright then
// reports it ("expected to fail, but passed"): the fix task removes the test.fail line and the known entry together.

test("the asset page's QR code has a text alternative (finding TX-A2)", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
```

```ts
// Spec §6.1.1: IT staff register an E2E asset on the asset form, with a photo and a purchase document. A wrong entry
// first (no room) shows its error under the field (spec §6.7); the corrected form saves the asset, its purchase

// The partial-save follow-up failure is not part of this run (spec §6.1.1: its unit test covers it).
import { asUser, expect, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { readRunData, readRunState } from "../support/state.ts"
import { txAssetName, txAssetTag } from "./tx-plan.ts"
import {
  closePhoneKeyboard,
  expectFieldError,
  expectReachable,

asUser("itStaff")

test("a new asset with a photo and a purchase document: no room first shows its error under the field, then the asset saves Ready with both", async ({ page, measure, e2eDevice }, testInfo) => {
  const { runId } = readRunState()
  const data = readRunData()
  const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
  const tag = txAssetTag(runId, testInfo.project.name, "REG")
  const name = txAssetName(runId, testInfo.project.name, "โน้ตบุ๊กลงทะเบียนใหม่")
  const documentNo = `${tag}-PO`

  await visit(measure, "/th/assets/new", "new asset form", testInfo)
  await page.locator("#asset-tag").fill(tag)
  await page.locator("#asset-category").selectOption(masters.categoryId)
  await page.locator("#asset-company").selectOption(data.companyId)
  await page.locator("#asset-branch").selectOption(data.branchId)
  await page.locator("#asset-department").selectOption(data.departmentId)
  await page.locator("#asset-status").selectOption(masters.readyStatusId)
  await page.locator("#asset-condition").selectOption(masters.goodConditionId)
  await page.locator("#asset-purchase-document-no").fill(documentNo)
  await page.locator("#asset-purchase-document-file").setInputFiles(PDF_FIXTURE)
  await page.locator("#asset-photo-file").setInputFiles(PHOTO_FIXTURE)
  await expect(page.getByText("evidence.pdf")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText("photo.png")).toBeVisible()
  const save = page.locator("[data-slot=sticky-form-footer]").getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true })
  // The tag's duplicate check runs while it is typed; the save waits for it (its reason shows beside the button).
  await expect(page.locator("#asset-save-reason")).toHaveCount(0)

  // A wrong entry first: no room. Nothing is posted; the error shows under the field and the field takes focus.
  let posted = 0
  page.on("request", (request) => {
    if (request.method() === "POST" && new URL(request.url()).pathname === "/api/assets") posted += 1
  })
  await save.click()
  await expectFieldError(page, "asset-current-location", "กรุณาระบุข้อมูลนี้")
  await expect(page.locator("#asset-current-location")).toBeFocused()
  expect(posted, "the wrong entry posts nothing").toBe(0)
  await record(measure, "new asset form with its error", testInfo)

  await page.locator("#asset-current-location").selectOption(data.locationId)
  await expect(page.locator("#asset-current-location-error")).toHaveCount(0)

  // The name (the form suggests the category's; IT staff type their own). On the phone the keyboard opens over the form
  // while it is typed, and the sticky save bar stays reachable.
  if (e2eDevice === "phone") await openPhoneKeyboard(page)
  await page.locator("#asset-name").fill(name)
  if (e2eDevice === "phone") {
    await expectReachable(save)
    await measure.screenshot("asset form with the keyboard open")
  }

  const created = page.waitForResponse((response) => new URL(response.url()).pathname === "/api/assets" && response.request().method() === "POST")
  const photoSaved = page.waitForResponse((response) => /\/api\/assets\/[^/]+\/attachments$/.test(new URL(response.url()).pathname) && response.request().method() === "POST")
  const documentLinked = page.waitForResponse((response) => /\/api\/assets\/[^/]+\/purchase-documents$/.test(new URL(response.url()).pathname) && response.request().method() === "POST")
  await measure.timed("asset save with its follow-ups", async () => {
    await save.click()
    await Promise.all([created, photoSaved, documentLinked])
  })
  if (e2eDevice === "phone") await closePhoneKeyboard(page)
  expect((await created).status()).toBe(201)
  expect((await photoSaved).ok(), "the photo is saved").toBe(true)
  expect((await documentLinked).ok(), "the purchase document is linked").toBe(true)
  const asset = (await (await created).json()) as { id: string; assetTag: string }
  expect(asset.assetTag).toBe(tag)
  await expect(page.locator("[data-sonner-toast]").filter({ hasText: "บันทึกสำเร็จ" })).toBeVisible()
  await page.waitForURL("**/th/assets")
  // The register loads its list and thumbnails; leaving before they arrive would cancel them.
  await page.waitForLoadState("networkidle")

  // The saved asset: Ready, with its photo and its purchase document.
  const after = await readAsset(page, asset.id)
  expect(after.status.name).toBe("Ready")
  await visitAssetPage(measure, asset.id, "the new asset", testInfo)
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name })).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByTestId("asset-identity-summary")).toContainText("พร้อมใช้งาน")
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText(documentNo)
  const photo = page.locator('main img[src*="/api/attachments/"]').first()
  await expect(photo).toBeVisible()
  expect(await photo.evaluate((image) => (image as HTMLImageElement).naturalWidth), "the photo loads").toBeGreaterThan(0)
})
```

```ts
// The reviewed allow-list (spec §8: "none allowed, except a short reviewed allow-list"; §10: each entry reviewed and
// justified). An entry lets one kind of record through when every field it names matches; its reason says why the
// record is not the app failing, and reviewed is the day a person (or the controller) checked it. Keep it short: a new
// entry needs the same review, and tests/e2e-measure.test.ts checks every entry has both.
import type { AllowEntry } from "./measure-core.ts"

export const ALLOW_LIST: readonly AllowEntry[] = [
  {
    id: "next-prefetch-cancelled",
    kind: "requestfailed",
    url: /[?&]_rsc=/,
    errorText: /^(?:net::ERR_ABORTED|cancelled|Load request cancelled)$/,
    reason:
      "Next.js prefetches the routes of the links on screen (RSC payloads, ?_rsc=) and cancels the prefetches it no longer needs; Chromium reports each cancelled one as net::ERR_ABORTED. A cancelled prefetch is the router's choice, not a failed request; a prefetch answered with an error status is still a failure (a response record).",
    reviewed: "2026-10-19",
  },
  {
    id: "foreign-attachment-thumbnail",
    kind: "response",
    url: /\/api\/attachments\/[0-9a-f-]{36}\/thumbnail$/,
    status: [500],
    reason:
      "An attachment row stores the absolute path of the upload folder that wrote it. The dev database is a copy of Production, so many rows hold Production paths (wiki #15, #25, #43), and rows written by another checkout of the app hold that checkout's folder; the thumbnail route refuses a path outside this app's UPLOAD_DIR with 500 Invalid attachment path. The asset register shows the newest assets' thumbnails, so a workflow that the app sends there meets them. A test's own photo is checked on its own asset page, not through these thumbnails.",
    reviewed: "2026-10-19",
  },
  {
    id: "thumbnail-cancelled-on-leave",
    kind: "requestfailed",
    url: /\/api\/attachments\/[0-9a-f-]{36}\/thumbnail$/,
    errorText: /^(?:net::ERR_ABORTED|cancelled|Load request cancelled)$/,
    reason:
      "A list page loads its thumbnails as images; when the workflow moves on to the next page, the browser cancels the images still loading (Chromium net::ERR_ABORTED). A cancelled image is the browser leaving the page, not a failed request; a thumbnail answered with an error status is still a response record.",
    reviewed: "2026-10-19",
  },
]
```

```ts
// App findings of the transaction rehearsal that every visit of a page would trip (details and evidence in the plan's
// findings file). Each one has its own expected-to-fail test in findings.spec.ts, which passes, and so is
// reported, once the app is fixed; until then a workflow test that visits the page drops exactly that violation (the
// rule on its known element), so the workflow's other measurements still count. Any other violation still fails.
import type { TestInfo } from "@playwright/test"
import type { PageRecord } from "../support/measure-core.ts"

export type KnownAxe = { finding: string; rule: string; target: RegExp; why: string }

// TX-A2: the asset page's QR code (qrcode.react's QRCodeSVG draws role="img" and the app gives it no title).
export const QR_WITHOUT_NAME: KnownAxe = {
  finding: "TX-A2",
  rule: "svg-img-alt",
  target: /^svg\[height="160"\]$/,
  why: "the asset page's QR code is an image with no text alternative",
}

// TX-A3: the A4 documents' fields (operation-document-print.tsx) are <dt>/<dd> pairs in a <div> grid with no <dl>. axe
// names a pair's cell by its tag or by its classes, inside the field's grid-cols-[38%_1fr] row.
export const PRINT_FIELDS_WITHOUT_DL: KnownAxe = {
  finding: "TX-A3",
  rule: "dlitem",
  target: /grid-cols-\\\[38\\%_1fr\\\]/,
  why: "the A4 document's field pairs are dt and dd outside a dl",
}

export const KNOWN_AXE: readonly KnownAxe[] = [QR_WITHOUT_NAME, PRINT_FIELDS_WITHOUT_DL]

// Drop the known violations from a recorded page (the counts stay as axe gave them, so the summary still shows them),
// and say so on the test.
export function dropKnownAxe(record: PageRecord, known: readonly KnownAxe[], testInfo: TestInfo): PageRecord {
  if (!record.axe) return record
  record.axe.failing = record.axe.failing.filter((violation) => {
    const match = known.find((entry) => entry.rule === violation.id && violation.targets.every((target) => entry.target.test(target)))
    if (!match) return true
    testInfo.annotations.push({ type: "known app finding", description: `${match.finding}: ${match.why} (axe ${violation.id} on ${record.label})` })
    return false
  })
  return record
}
```

  - Replace `e2e/transactions/findings.spec.ts` with exactly:

```ts
// The transaction rehearsal's axe findings, one expected-to-fail test each (known-findings.ts): the workflow tests drop
// exactly these violations, and these tests keep them in view. Each passes once the app is fixed, and Playwright then
// reports it ("expected to fail, but passed"): the fix task removes the test.fail line and the known entry together.
import { asUser, expect, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { readRunData } from "../support/state.ts"
import { createTxAsset, handoverByApi, readAssetMasters } from "./tx-support.ts"

asUser("itStaff")

test("the asset page's QR code has a text alternative (finding TX-A2)", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
  test.fail(true, "TX-A2: axe svg-img-alt, the QR code's svg has role img and no title")
  const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
  const asset = await createTxAsset(page, masters, testInfo.project.name, "QR1", "โน้ตบุ๊กตรวจป้าย QR")
  const record = await measure.visit(`/th/assets/${asset.id}`, "asset page")
  expect(record.axe?.failing.filter((violation) => violation.id === "svg-img-alt") ?? []).toEqual([])
})

test("an A4 document's fields are a description list (finding TX-A3)", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {

  const record = await measure.visit(`/th/asset-management/checkouts/${handover.id}`, "HO- document")
  expect(record.axe?.failing.filter((violation) => violation.id === "dlitem") ?? []).toEqual([])
})
```

```ts
// Spec §6.1.2–3: IT staff hand an E2E asset over (ส่งมอบ) to an E2E employee, permanently and as a temporary loan,
// with the receiver's signature (a mouse stroke on the desktop, a finger on the phone) and a photo, confirmed in the
// review dialog; the HO- document prints; a second handover of the same asset is refused. A wrong entry first shows
// its error under the field (spec §6.7).
import { asUser, expect, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { readRunData } from "../support/state.ts"
import { bangkokDate } from "./tx-plan.ts"
import {
  createTxAsset,
  expectFieldError,
  expectPrints,
  PHOTO_FIXTURE,
  pickSearchable,
  readAsset,
  readAssetMasters,
  record,
  signOnPad,
  visit,
  visitAssetPage,
} from "./tx-support.ts"

asUser("itStaff")

test("a permanent handover: the receiver signs, a photo is attached, the review confirms; the asset is In Use with the receiver as custodian and the HO- document prints", async ({ page, measure, e2eDevice, browserName }, testInfo) => {
  const receiver = readRunData().employees.receiver1
  const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
  const asset = await createTxAsset(page, masters, testInfo.project.name, "HO1", "โน้ตบุ๊กส่งมอบประจำ")

  // From the asset page's quick action, as IT staff start a handover.
  await visitAssetPage(measure, asset.id, "asset before the handover", testInfo)
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: "ส่งมอบ", exact: true }).first().click()
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`/th/asset-management/checkout\\?assetId=${asset.id}`))
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "ส่งมอบทรัพย์สิน" })).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.locator("#checkout-asset")).toContainText(asset.assetTag)
  await record(measure, "handover form", testInfo)

  // A wrong entry first: no receiver and no handover mode; each error shows under its field and the first takes focus.
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
  await expectFieldError(page, "checkout-destination", "กรุณาระบุข้อมูลนี้")
  await expectFieldError(page, "checkout-handover-mode", "กรุณาเลือกลักษณะการส่งมอบก่อนดำเนินการต่อ")
  await expectFieldError(page, "checkout-condition", "กรุณาระบุข้อมูลนี้")
  await expect(page.locator("#checkout-destination")).toBeFocused()

  await pickSearchable(page, "checkout-destination", receiver.code, `${receiver.code} - ${receiver.fullNameTh}`)
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: /มอบหมายใช้งานประจำ/ }).check()
  await page.locator("#checkout-condition").selectOption({ label: "ดี" })
  await expect(page.locator("#checkout-destination-error")).toHaveCount(0)
  await expect(page.locator("#checkout-handover-mode-error")).toHaveCount(0)
  await page.locator("form input[type=file]").setInputFiles(PHOTO_FIXTURE)
  await expect(page.getByText("เลือกรูปแล้ว พร้อมบันทึกพร้อมรายการส่งมอบ")).toBeVisible()
  await signOnPad(page, page.getByRole("group", { name: "ลายเซ็นผู้รับ" }), e2eDevice, browserName)

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
  const review = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ตรวจสอบก่อนส่งมอบ" })
  await expect(review).toBeVisible()
  await expect(review).toContainText(asset.assetTag)
  await expect(review).toContainText(receiver.fullNameTh)
  await expect(review).toContainText("มอบหมายใช้งานประจำ")
  await expect(review).toContainText("ใช้งานอยู่")
  await expect(review).toContainText("มีรูปหรือ ลายเซ็นแนบแล้ว")
  await record(measure, "handover review", testInfo)
  const saved = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith(`/api/assets/${asset.id}/checkout`) && response.request().method() === "POST")
  await measure.timed("handover save", async () => {
    await review.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันส่งมอบ" }).click()
    await saved
  })
  const checkout = (await (await saved).json()) as { id: string; documentNo: string; receiverSignature: string | null; photoBefore: string | null }
  expect(checkout.documentNo).toMatch(/^HO-/)
  expect(checkout.receiverSignature, "the signature is stored with the handover").toBeTruthy()
  expect(checkout.photoBefore, "the photo is stored with the handover").toBeTruthy()

  // The HO- document opens on its own and prints.
  await page.waitForURL(`**/th/asset-management/checkouts/${checkout.id}`)
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "ใบส่งมอบทรัพย์สิน" })).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText(checkout.documentNo, { exact: true }).first()).toBeVisible()
  const signature = page.getByRole("img", { name: "ผู้รับทรัพย์สิน" })
  await expect(signature).toBeVisible()
  expect(await signature.evaluate((image) => (image as HTMLImageElement).naturalWidth), "the signature image loads").toBeGreaterThan(0)
  await record(measure, "HO- document", testInfo)
  await expectPrints(page, "พิมพ์ใบส่งมอบ")

  // The outcome: In Use, the receiver holds it, in the UI and in the API.
  const after = await readAsset(page, asset.id)
  expect(after.status.name).toBe("In Use")
  expect(after.custodianId).toBe(receiver.id)
  await visitAssetPage(measure, asset.id, "asset after the handover", testInfo)
  await expect(page.getByTestId("asset-identity-summary")).toContainText("ใช้งานอยู่")
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText(receiver.fullNameTh)
  // The handover action waits for the return now.
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "ส่งมอบ", exact: true }).first()).toBeDisabled()
})

test("a temporary loan: a due date before the handover date shows its error under the field; corrected, the asset is Checked Out with its due date", async ({ page, measure, e2eDevice, browserName }, testInfo) => {
  const receiver = readRunData().employees.receiver2
  const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
  const asset = await createTxAsset(page, masters, testInfo.project.name, "HO2", "โน้ตบุ๊กยืมชั่วคราว")
  const today = bangkokDate()
  const dueDate = bangkokDate(7)

  await visit(measure, `/th/asset-management/checkout?assetId=${asset.id}`, "loan form", testInfo)
  await pickSearchable(page, "checkout-destination", receiver.code, `${receiver.code} - ${receiver.fullNameTh}`)
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: /ยืมใช้ชั่วคราว/ }).check()
  await page.locator("#checkout-condition").selectOption({ label: "ดี" })
  await page.locator("#checkout-date").fill(today)

  // A wrong entry first: the due date the day before the handover.
  await page.locator("#checkout-expected-return").fill(bangkokDate(-1))
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
  await expectFieldError(page, "checkout-expected-return", "วันกำหนดคืนต้องเป็นวันเดียวกับหรือหลังวันที่ส่งมอบ")
  await expect(page.locator("#checkout-expected-return")).toBeFocused()
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog")).toHaveCount(0)

  await page.locator("#checkout-expected-return").fill(dueDate)
  await expect(page.locator("#checkout-expected-return-error")).toHaveCount(0)
  await signOnPad(page, page.getByRole("group", { name: "ลายเซ็นผู้รับ" }), e2eDevice, browserName)
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
  const review = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ตรวจสอบก่อนส่งมอบ" })
  await expect(review).toContainText("ยืมใช้ชั่วคราว")
  await expect(review).toContainText("ถูกยืม")
  await expect(review).toContainText(dueDate)
  const saved = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith(`/api/assets/${asset.id}/checkout`) && response.request().method() === "POST")
  await review.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันส่งมอบ" }).click()
  const checkout = (await (await saved).json()) as { id: string; documentNo: string; expectedReturnDate: string | null; handoverMode: string }
  expect(checkout.handoverMode).toBe("temporary_loan")
  expect(checkout.expectedReturnDate?.slice(0, 10)).toBe(dueDate)

  await page.waitForURL(`**/th/asset-management/checkouts/${checkout.id}`)
  await expect(page.getByText(checkout.documentNo, { exact: true }).first()).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText("กำหนดคืน")
  await record(measure, "HO- document of the loan", testInfo)

  const after = await readAsset(page, asset.id)
  expect(after.status.name).toBe("Checked Out")
  expect(after.custodianId).toBe(receiver.id)
})

// The double checkout (spec §6.1.3) as it happens at the desk: two handover forms of the same asset are open (two IT
// staff, or one form left open); the first saves, the second is refused with the reason, and the asset stays with the
// first receiver. The refusal's reason a person reads is checked apart (finding TX-A1): it fails until the app names it.
const refusalToasts = new Map<string, string>()

test.describe("no double checkout", () => {
  test.describe.configure({ mode: "serial" })

  test("a second handover of an asset already handed over is refused, and the asset stays with the first receiver", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
    const data = readRunData()
    const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
    const asset = await createTxAsset(page, masters, testInfo.project.name, "HO3", "โน้ตบุ๊กส่งมอบซ้ำ")
    const fillHandover = async (form: typeof page, receiverCode: string, receiverLabel: string) => {
      await pickSearchable(form, "checkout-destination", receiverCode, receiverLabel)
      await form.getByRole("radio", { name: /มอบหมายใช้งานประจำ/ }).check()
      await form.locator("#checkout-condition").selectOption({ label: "ดี" })
      await form.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
    }
    const first = data.employees.receiver1
    const second = data.employees.receiver2

    // Both forms open before either saves.
    await visit(measure, `/th/asset-management/checkout?assetId=${asset.id}`, "the second desk's handover form", testInfo)
    const otherDesk = await page.context().newPage()
    await otherDesk.goto(`/th/asset-management/checkout?assetId=${asset.id}`)
    await fillHandover(otherDesk, first.code, `${first.code} - ${first.fullNameTh}`)
    const firstSaved = otherDesk.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith(`/api/assets/${asset.id}/checkout`) && response.request().method() === "POST")
    await otherDesk.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ตรวจสอบก่อนส่งมอบ" }).getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันส่งมอบ" }).click()
    expect((await firstSaved).status()).toBe(201)
    await otherDesk.waitForURL("**/th/asset-management/checkouts/**")
    await otherDesk.close()

    measure.expectRefusal({ method: "POST", url: new RegExp(`/api/assets/${asset.id}/checkout$`), status: 409, reason: "the asset was handed over from the other form first" })
    await fillHandover(page, second.code, `${second.code} - ${second.fullNameTh}`)
    const refused = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith(`/api/assets/${asset.id}/checkout`) && response.request().method() === "POST")
    await page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ตรวจสอบก่อนส่งมอบ" }).getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันส่งมอบ" }).click()
    expect((await refused).status()).toBe(409)
    const toast = page.locator("[data-sonner-toast][data-type=error]")
    await expect(toast).toBeVisible()
    const toastText = (await toast.innerText()).trim()
    refusalToasts.set(testInfo.project.name, toastText)
    testInfo.annotations.push({ type: "refusal shown", description: toastText })
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`/th/asset-management/checkout\\?assetId=${asset.id}`))

    const after = await readAsset(page, asset.id)
    expect(after.status.name).toBe("In Use")
    expect(after.custodianId).toBe(first.id)

    // A fresh form no longer offers the asset, and its page's handover action waits for the return.
    await visit(measure, `/th/asset-management/checkout?assetId=${asset.id}`, "a fresh handover form", testInfo)
    await page.locator("#checkout-asset").click()
    await page.getByRole("dialog").last().getByRole("combobox").fill(asset.assetTag)
    await expect(page.getByRole("dialog").last()).toContainText("ไม่พบรายการ")
    await page.keyboard.press("Escape")
    await visitAssetPage(measure, asset.id, "asset handed over", testInfo)
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "ส่งมอบ", exact: true }).first()).toBeDisabled()
  })

  test("the refusal says why: the asset is already handed over (finding TX-A1)", async ({ e2eTheme }, testInfo) => {
    test.fail(true, "TX-A1: a refused second handover shows only เกิดข้อผิดพลาด, the server's code is not in the error catalog")
    const text = refusalToasts.get(testInfo.project.name)
    expect(text, `the refusal test ran first (${e2eTheme})`).toBeTruthy()
    expect(text).toMatch(/ส่งมอบหรือยืมไม่ได้|ยังไม่รับคืน|ถูกส่งมอบแล้ว|ข้อมูลทรัพย์สินเปลี่ยนระหว่างบันทึก/)
  })
})
```

```ts
// App findings of the transaction rehearsal that every visit of a page would trip (details and evidence in the plan's
// findings file). Each one has its own expected-to-fail test in findings.spec.ts, which passes, and so is
// reported, once the app is fixed; until then a workflow test that visits the page drops exactly that violation (the
// rule on its known element), so the workflow's other measurements still count. Any other violation still fails.
import type { TestInfo } from "@playwright/test"
import type { PageRecord } from "../support/measure-core.ts"

export type KnownAxe = { finding: string; rule: string; target: RegExp; why: string }

// TX-A2: the asset page's QR code (qrcode.react's QRCodeSVG draws role="img" and the app gives it no title).
export const QR_WITHOUT_NAME: KnownAxe = {
  finding: "TX-A2",
  rule: "svg-img-alt",
  target: /^svg\[height="160"\]$/,
  why: "the asset page's QR code is an image with no text alternative",
}

// TX-A3: the A4 documents' fields (operation-document-print.tsx) are <dt>/<dd> pairs in a <div> grid with no <dl>. axe
// names a pair's cell by its tag or by its classes, inside the field's grid-cols-[38%_1fr] row.
export const PRINT_FIELDS_WITHOUT_DL: KnownAxe = {
  finding: "TX-A3",
  rule: "dlitem",
  target: /grid-cols-\\\[38\\%_1fr\\\]/,
  why: "the A4 document's field pairs are dt and dd outside a dl",
}

// TX-A8: on the phone, a region that scrolls has no keyboard access: the review dialog's body when its rows are taller
// than the screen (return, transfer), and the batch receipt's table.
export const SCROLL_REGION_WITHOUT_KEYS: KnownAxe = {
  finding: "TX-A8",
  rule: "scrollable-region-focusable",
  target: /data-slot="accessible-dialog-body"|data-slot="data-table-scroll"/,
  why: "a scrolling region on the phone takes no keyboard focus",
}

export const KNOWN_AXE: readonly KnownAxe[] = [QR_WITHOUT_NAME, PRINT_FIELDS_WITHOUT_DL, SCROLL_REGION_WITHOUT_KEYS]

// Drop the known violations from a recorded page (the counts stay as axe gave them, so the summary still shows them),
// and say so on the test.
export function dropKnownAxe(record: PageRecord, known: readonly KnownAxe[], testInfo: TestInfo): PageRecord {
  if (!record.axe) return record
  record.axe.failing = record.axe.failing.filter((violation) => {
    const match = known.find((entry) => entry.rule === violation.id && violation.targets.every((target) => entry.target.test(target)))
    if (!match) return true
    testInfo.annotations.push({ type: "known app finding", description: `${match.finding}: ${match.why} (axe ${violation.id} on ${record.label})` })
    return false
  })
  return record
}
```

```ts
// The transaction rehearsal's axe findings, one expected-to-fail test each (known-findings.ts): the workflow tests drop
// exactly these violations, and these tests keep them in view. Each passes once the app is fixed, and Playwright then
// reports it ("expected to fail, but passed"): the fix task removes the test.fail line and the known entry together.
```

```ts
// Spec §6.1.4: IT staff take an E2E asset back (รับคืน) from the receiver: the RT- document, then the asset's status
// and room after it; a return marked "send to repair" (ส่งซ่อม) opens a repair record. A wrong entry first shows its
// errors under the fields (spec §6.7). The handover before each return goes through the checkout API (handover.spec.ts
// walks the handover form itself).
import { asUser, expect, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { readRunData } from "../support/state.ts"
import {
  createTxAsset,
  ensureSecondLocation,
  expectFieldError,
  expectPrints,
  handoverByApi,
  PHOTO_FIXTURE,
  pickSearchable,
  readAsset,
  readAssetMasters,
  record,
  signOnPad,
  visit,
  visitAssetPage,
} from "./tx-support.ts"

asUser("itStaff")

test("a return: the RT- document prints, and the asset is Ready in the room it was taken to, with no custodian", async ({ page, measure, e2eDevice, browserName }, testInfo) => {
  const data = readRunData()
  const receiver = data.employees.receiver1
  const staff = data.employees.itStaff
  const room = await ensureSecondLocation()
  const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
  const asset = await createTxAsset(page, masters, testInfo.project.name, "RT1", "โน้ตบุ๊กรับคืน")
  const handover = await handoverByApi(page, asset.id, receiver.id, masters.goodConditionId)

  // From the asset page's quick action.
  await visitAssetPage(measure, asset.id, "asset handed over", testInfo)
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: "รับคืน", exact: true }).first().click()
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`/th/asset-management/checkin\\?checkoutId=${handover.id}`))
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "รับคืนทรัพย์สิน" })).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText(handover.documentNo)
  await record(measure, "return form", testInfo)

  // A wrong entry first: nothing chosen; each error shows under its field and the first takes focus.
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
  for (const field of ["checkin-return-by", "checkin-receive-by", "checkin-condition-after", "checkin-next-status", "checkin-next-location"]) {
    await expectFieldError(page, field, "กรุณาระบุข้อมูลนี้")
  }
  await expect(page.locator("#checkin-return-by")).toBeFocused()

  await pickSearchable(page, "checkin-return-by", receiver.code, `${receiver.code} - ${receiver.fullNameTh}`)
  await pickSearchable(page, "checkin-receive-by", staff.code, `${staff.code} - ${staff.fullNameTh}`)
  await page.locator("#checkin-condition-after").selectOption({ label: "ดี" })
  await page.locator("#checkin-next-status").selectOption({ label: "พร้อมใช้งาน" })
  await page.locator("#checkin-next-location").selectOption({ label: `${room.code} - ${room.name}` })
  await page.locator("form input[type=file]").setInputFiles(PHOTO_FIXTURE)
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText("photo.png")
  await signOnPad(page, page.getByRole("group", { name: "ลายเซ็นผู้คืน" }), e2eDevice, browserName)
  await signOnPad(page, page.getByRole("group", { name: "ลายเซ็นผู้รับคืน" }), e2eDevice, browserName)

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
  const review = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ตรวจสอบก่อนรับคืน" })
  await expect(review).toContainText(receiver.fullNameTh)
  await expect(review).toContainText(staff.fullNameTh)
  await expect(review).toContainText("พร้อมใช้งาน")
  await expect(review).toContainText(room.code)
  await expect(review).toContainText("รูปหรือ ลายเซ็น 3 รายการ")
  await record(measure, "return review", testInfo)
  const saved = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith(`/api/assets/${asset.id}/checkin`) && response.request().method() === "POST")
  await measure.timed("return save", async () => {
    await review.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันรับคืน" }).click()
    await saved
  })
  const checkin = (await (await saved).json()) as { id: string; documentNo: string }
  expect(checkin.documentNo).toMatch(/^RT-/)

  await page.waitForURL(`**/th/asset-management/checkins/${checkin.id}`)
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "ใบรับคืนทรัพย์สิน" })).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText(checkin.documentNo, { exact: true }).first()).toBeVisible()
  await record(measure, "RT- document", testInfo)
  await expectPrints(page, "พิมพ์ใบรับคืน")

  const after = await readAsset(page, asset.id)
  expect(after.status.name).toBe("Ready")
  expect(after.currentLocationId).toBe(room.id)
  expect(after.custodianId).toBeNull()
  await visitAssetPage(measure, asset.id, "asset after the return", testInfo)
  await expect(page.getByTestId("asset-identity-summary")).toContainText("พร้อมใช้งาน")
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText(room.name)
})

test("a return sent to repair opens an unfinished repair record, and the asset is Under Maintenance", async ({ page, measure, e2eDevice, browserName }, testInfo) => {
  const data = readRunData()
  const receiver = data.employees.receiver2
  const staff = data.employees.itStaff
  const room = await ensureSecondLocation()
  const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
  const asset = await createTxAsset(page, masters, testInfo.project.name, "RT2", "โน้ตบุ๊กคืนส่งซ่อม")
  const handover = await handoverByApi(page, asset.id, receiver.id, masters.goodConditionId)
  const problem = `${asset.assetTag} จอแตกตอนรับคืน`

  await visit(measure, `/th/asset-management/checkin?checkoutId=${handover.id}`, "return form", testInfo)
  await pickSearchable(page, "checkin-return-by", receiver.code, `${receiver.code} - ${receiver.fullNameTh}`)
  await pickSearchable(page, "checkin-receive-by", staff.code, `${staff.code} - ${staff.fullNameTh}`)
  await page.locator("#checkin-condition-after").selectOption({ label: "เสียหาย" })
  await page.locator("#checkin-next-status").selectOption({ label: "ส่งซ่อม" })
  await page.locator("#checkin-next-location").selectOption({ label: `${room.code} - ${room.name}` })
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText("ระบบจะสร้างบันทึกซ่อม (ยังซ่อมไม่เสร็จ) ให้อัตโนมัติ")
  await page.getByLabel("รายละเอียดปัญหาในใบซ่อม").fill(problem)
  await signOnPad(page, page.getByRole("group", { name: "ลายเซ็นผู้คืน" }), e2eDevice, browserName)

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
  const review = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ตรวจสอบก่อนรับคืน" })
  await expect(review).toContainText("อยู่ระหว่างซ่อม")
  const saved = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith(`/api/assets/${asset.id}/checkin`) && response.request().method() === "POST")
  await review.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันรับคืน" }).click()
  const checkin = (await (await saved).json()) as { id: string; documentNo: string }
  await page.waitForURL(`**/th/asset-management/checkins/${checkin.id}`)
  await record(measure, "RT- document (sent to repair)", testInfo)

  const after = await readAsset(page, asset.id)
  expect(after.status.name).toBe("Under Maintenance")
  const tickets = (await (await page.request.get(`/api/maintenance-tickets?assetId=${asset.id}`)).json()) as { data: { id: string; repairNo: string; repairStatus: string; problem: string }[] }
  expect(tickets.data, "one repair record for the asset").toHaveLength(1)
  const [ticket] = tickets.data
  expect(ticket.problem).toContain(problem)
  expect(["open", "in_progress"]).toContain(ticket.repairStatus)

  // The repair record is where the person goes next: it opens from the asset page.
  await visitAssetPage(measure, asset.id, "asset sent to repair", testInfo)
  await expect(page.getByTestId("asset-identity-summary")).toContainText("อยู่ระหว่างซ่อม")
  await visit(measure, `/th/maintenance/${ticket.id}`, "the repair record", testInfo)
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText(ticket.repairNo)
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText(asset.assetTag)
})
```

```ts
// Spec §6.1.5: IT staff transfer (โอนย้าย) an E2E asset to the run's second room and a new custodian: the TR- document,
// the new room and custodian. Transfer had no data in the dev database and its print page had never been previewed
// (wiki #22). A wrong entry first shows its errors under the fields (spec §6.7).
import { asUser, expect, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { readRunData } from "../support/state.ts"
import {
  createTxAsset,
  ensureSecondLocation,
  expectFieldError,
  expectPrints,
  pickSearchable,
  readAsset,
  readAssetMasters,
  record,
  visit,
  visitAssetPage,
} from "./tx-support.ts"

asUser("itStaff")

const transfers = new Map<string, { assetId: string; transferId: string; documentNo: string; custodian: string }>()

test.describe("transfer", () => {
  test.describe.configure({ mode: "serial" })

  test("a transfer to the second room and a new custodian: the asset's room and custodian change, and the TR- document opens and prints", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
    const receiver = readRunData().employees.receiver2
    const room = await ensureSecondLocation()
    const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
    const asset = await createTxAsset(page, masters, testInfo.project.name, "TR1", "โน้ตบุ๊กโอนย้าย")

    await visitAssetPage(measure, asset.id, "asset before the transfer", testInfo)
    await page.getByRole("link", { name: "โอนย้าย", exact: true }).first().click()
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`/th/asset-management/transfer\\?assetId=${asset.id}`))
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "โอนย้ายทรัพย์สิน" })).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.locator("#transfer-asset")).toContainText(asset.assetTag)
    await record(measure, "transfer form", testInfo)

    // A wrong entry first: no destination and no reason.
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
    await expectFieldError(page, "transfer-to-location", "เลือกปลายทางการโอนย้ายอย่างน้อย 1 รายการ")
    await expectFieldError(page, "transfer-reason", "กรุณาระบุข้อมูลนี้")
    await expect(page.locator("#transfer-to-location")).toBeFocused()

    await pickSearchable(page, "transfer-to-location", room.code, `${room.code} - ${room.name}`)
    await pickSearchable(page, page.getByRole("button", { name: /^ผู้ถือครองปลายทาง/ }), receiver.code, `${receiver.code} - ${receiver.fullNameTh}`)
    await page.locator("#transfer-reason").fill(`${asset.assetTag} ย้ายไปห้องปลายทาง`)
    await expect(page.locator("#transfer-to-location-error")).toHaveCount(0)

    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
    const review = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ตรวจสอบก่อนโอนย้าย" })
    await expect(review).toContainText(room.code)
    await expect(review).toContainText(receiver.fullNameTh)
    await expect(review).toContainText("ใช้งานอยู่")
    await record(measure, "transfer review", testInfo)
    const saved = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith(`/api/assets/${asset.id}/transfer`) && response.request().method() === "POST")
    await measure.timed("transfer save", async () => {
      await review.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันโอนย้าย" }).click()
      await saved
    })
    const transfer = (await (await saved).json()) as { id: string; documentNo: string }
    expect(transfer.documentNo).toMatch(/^TR-/)
    transfers.set(testInfo.project.name, { assetId: asset.id, transferId: transfer.id, documentNo: transfer.documentNo, custodian: receiver.fullNameTh })

    // The form goes back to the asset page: the new room and custodian.
    await page.waitForURL(`**/th/assets/${asset.id}`)
    const after = await readAsset(page, asset.id)
    expect(after.currentLocationId).toBe(room.id)
    expect(after.custodianId).toBe(receiver.id)
    expect(after.status.name).toBe("In Use")
    await visitAssetPage(measure, asset.id, "asset after the transfer", testInfo)
    await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText(room.name)
    await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText(receiver.fullNameTh)

    // The TR- document (wiki #22: never previewed before), opened at its address.
    await visit(measure, `/th/asset-management/transfers/${transfer.id}`, "TR- document", testInfo)
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "โอนย้ายทรัพย์สิน" })).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.getByText(transfer.documentNo, { exact: true }).first()).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText(room.name)
    await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText(`${asset.assetTag} ย้ายไปห้องปลายทาง`)
    await expectPrints(page, "พิมพ์เอกสารโอนย้าย")
  })

  // Finding TX-A4: no page links to /asset-management/transfers/<id> (the asset page's timeline links the HO- and RT-
  // documents only), so a person cannot open the TR- document they just made.
  test("the TR- document opens from the asset page (finding TX-A4)", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
    test.fail(true, "TX-A4: the TR- document has no way in from the UI")
    const transfer = transfers.get(testInfo.project.name)
    expect(transfer, "the transfer test ran first").toBeTruthy()
    if (!transfer) return
    // Any of the asset page's views would do: the overview and the operations timeline.
    let links = 0
    for (const view of [undefined, "operations"] as const) {
      await visitAssetPage(measure, transfer.assetId, `asset after the transfer (${view ?? "overview"})`, testInfo, view)
      links += await page.locator(`a[href*="/asset-management/transfers/${transfer.transferId}"]`).count()
    }
    expect(links, "links to the TR- document").toBeGreaterThan(0)
  })

  // Finding TX-A6: the TR- document prints the status, room and department before and after, but not the custodian, so
  // a transfer that hands the asset to a new holder has no paper naming them.
  test("the TR- document names the new custodian (finding TX-A6)", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
    test.fail(true, "TX-A6: the TR- document leaves out the custodian")
    const transfer = transfers.get(testInfo.project.name)
    expect(transfer, "the transfer test ran first").toBeTruthy()
    if (!transfer) return
    await visit(measure, `/th/asset-management/transfers/${transfer.transferId}`, "TR- document", testInfo)
    await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText(transfer.custodian, { timeout: 3_000 })
  })
})
```

**Interfaces:**

```ts
test("three assets added on the batch form move to the second room in one bulk move, a wrong entry first (e2e transactions task 5, TX5)", async () => {
```

```ts
// Spec §6.1.6: IT staff add three E2E assets at once on the batch form (spec §7: the batch form is exercised too), then
// move all three to the run's second room in one bulk move (ย้ายที่ตั้งหลายรายการ). A wrong entry first shows its errors
// under the fields (spec §6.7).
import { asUser, expect, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { readRunData, readRunState } from "../support/state.ts"
import { txAssetName, txAssetTag } from "./tx-plan.ts"
import {
  ensureSecondLocation,
  expectFieldError,
  pickSearchable,
  readAsset,
  readAssetMasters,
  record,
  visit,
  visitAssetPage,
} from "./tx-support.ts"

asUser("itStaff")

test("three assets added on the batch form move to the second room together", async ({ page, measure, e2eDevice }, testInfo) => {
  const { runId } = readRunState()
  const data = readRunData()
  const room = await ensureSecondLocation()
  const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
  const tags = [1, 2, 3].map((index) => txAssetTag(runId, testInfo.project.name, `BM${index}`))
  const name = txAssetName(runId, testInfo.project.name, "จอภาพย้ายที่ตั้ง")

  // The batch form: the shared values once, a tag per row (the table on the desktop, a card per row on the phone).
  await visit(measure, "/th/assets/new", "new asset form", testInfo)
  await page.getByRole("group", { name: "วิธีเพิ่มทรัพย์สิน" }).getByRole("button", { name: "เพิ่มเป็นชุด" }).click()
  await page.locator("#asset-batch-name").fill(name)
  await page.locator("#asset-batch-category").selectOption(masters.categoryId)
  await page.locator("#asset-batch-company").selectOption(data.companyId)
  await page.locator("#asset-batch-branch").selectOption(data.branchId)
  await page.locator("#asset-batch-department").selectOption(data.departmentId)
  await page.locator("#asset-batch-current-location").selectOption(data.locationId)
  await page.locator("#asset-batch-status").selectOption(masters.readyStatusId)
  await page.locator("#asset-batch-condition").selectOption(masters.goodConditionId)
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "เพิ่มแถว" }).click()
  await expect(page.locator("#asset-batch-row-count")).toContainText("รายการตอนนี้ 3 รายการ")
  const view = e2eDevice === "desktop" ? "row" : "card"
  for (const [index, tag] of tags.entries()) await page.locator(`#asset-batch-${view}-${index}-tag`).fill(tag)
  await record(measure, "batch form filled", testInfo)

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึกทั้งชุด" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText("ตรวจสอบก่อนบันทึก").first()).toBeVisible()
  for (const tag of tags) await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText(tag)
  const created = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith("/api/assets/batch") && response.request().method() === "POST")
  await measure.timed("batch save", async () => {
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันและบันทึกทั้งชุด" }).click()
    await created
  })
  const batch = (await (await created).json()) as { created: number; assetIds: string[]; assets: { id: string; assetTag: string }[] }
  expect(batch.created).toBe(3)
  expect(batch.assets.map((asset) => asset.assetTag).sort()).toEqual([...tags].sort())
  await expect(page.getByText("สร้างทรัพย์สินเป็นชุดสำเร็จ")).toBeVisible()
  await record(measure, "batch created", testInfo)

  // The bulk move: a wrong entry first (nothing chosen), then the three found by their tags.
  await visit(measure, "/th/asset-management/bulk-move", "bulk move form", testInfo)
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
  await expectFieldError(page, "bulk-move-assets", "กรุณาเลือกทรัพย์สินอย่างน้อย 1 รายการ")
  await expectFieldError(page, "bulk-move-to-location", "กรุณาระบุข้อมูลนี้")
  await expectFieldError(page, "bulk-move-reason", "กรุณาระบุข้อมูลนี้")
  await expect(page.locator("#bulk-move-assets input[type=checkbox]").first()).toBeFocused()

  await page.getByRole("searchbox", { name: "ทรัพย์สิน" }).fill(tags[0].slice(0, -1))
  const list = page.locator("#bulk-move-assets")
  await expect(list.getByRole("checkbox")).toHaveCount(3)
  for (const tag of tags) await list.getByRole("checkbox", { name: new RegExp(tag) }).check()
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText("เลือกแล้ว 3 รายการ")
  await expect(page.locator("#bulk-move-assets-error")).toHaveCount(0)
  await pickSearchable(page, "bulk-move-to-location", room.code, `${room.code} - ${room.name}`)
  await page.locator("#bulk-move-reason").fill(`${runId} ย้ายทั้งชุดไปห้องปลายทาง`)
  const moved = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith("/api/assets/bulk-move") && response.request().method() === "POST")
  await measure.timed("bulk move save", async () => {
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
    await moved
  })
  expect((await moved).status()).toBe(200)
  await page.waitForURL("**/th/assets")
  // The register loads its list and thumbnails; leaving before they arrive would cancel them.
  await page.waitForLoadState("networkidle")
  await expect(page.locator("[data-sonner-toast]").filter({ hasText: "ย้ายที่ตั้งทรัพย์สินสำเร็จ" })).toBeVisible()

  for (const asset of batch.assets) {
    const after = await readAsset(page, asset.id)
    expect(after.currentLocationId, asset.assetTag).toBe(room.id)
    expect(after.status.name, asset.assetTag).toBe("Ready")
  }
  await visitAssetPage(measure, batch.assets[0].id, "a moved asset", testInfo)
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toContainText(room.name)
})
```

Spec §6.1.7, §6.7; TX-R5, TX-R11; findings TX-A5, TX-A7.

**Files:**
- Create: `e2e/transactions/cancel.spec.ts` (new, LF)
- Test: `tests/e2e-transactions.test.ts` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: TX1's helpers; the asset page's ⋯ (การดำเนินการเพิ่มเติม) → ยกเลิกรายการ, `POST /api/asset-checkouts/<id>/cancel`, the VOID HO- document, `/th/assets/<id>?view=operations`.
- Produces: nothing for later tasks.

- [ ] **Step 1: The failing test.** Append to `tests/e2e-transactions.test.ts` exactly:

```ts

test("the latest transaction cancelled: the state before it, the VOID document with the reason, the cancel in the history; its two gaps are expected to fail (e2e transactions task 6, TX6)", async () => {
  const spec = readTxSpec("e2e/transactions/cancel.spec.ts")
  assert.match(spec, /await expectFieldError\(page, "transaction-cancel-reason", "กรุณาระบุเหตุผลอย่างน้อย 5 ตัวอักษร"\)/)
  assert.match(spec, /page\.getByRole\("status"\)\.filter\(\{ hasText: "VOID \/ ยกเลิกแล้ว" \}\)/)
  assert.match(spec, /expect\(after\.custodianId\)\.toBeNull\(\)/)
  assert.match(spec, /await visitAssetPage\(measure, asset\.id, "asset history after the cancel", testInfo, "operations"\)/)
  assert.deepEqual(expectedFailures(spec), ["TX-A5", "TX-A7"])
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Watch it fail.** `node --test tests/e2e-transactions.test.ts`. Expected: 6 tests, 5 pass, 1 fail: "the latest transaction cancelled: … (e2e transactions task 6, TX6)" (`ENOENT … e2e/transactions/cancel.spec.ts`). And (controller) `--list` of the spec prints `Error: No tests found.`
- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** (exact; file tool, LF). Create `e2e/transactions/cancel.spec.ts` with exactly:

```ts
// Spec §6.1.7: IT staff cancel the latest transaction (ยกเลิกรายการ) of an E2E asset, a handover made by mistake: the
// asset goes back to the state before it, the HO- document prints as VOID with the reason, and the asset's history keeps
// the compensating movement. A wrong entry first shows its error under the field (spec §6.7).
import { asUser, expect, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { readRunData } from "../support/state.ts"
import {
  createTxAsset,
  expectFieldError,
  expectPrints,
  handoverByApi,
  readAsset,
  readAssetMasters,
  record,
  visit,
  visitAssetPage,
} from "./tx-support.ts"

asUser("itStaff")

const cancelled = new Map<string, { history: string; operator: string; staffName: string }>()

test.describe("cancel the latest transaction", () => {
  test.describe.configure({ mode: "serial" })

  test("a mistaken handover is cancelled: the asset is Ready again with no custodian, the HO- document prints as VOID with the reason, and the history keeps the cancel", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
    const receiver = readRunData().employees.receiver1
    const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
    const asset = await createTxAsset(page, masters, testInfo.project.name, "CX1", "โน้ตบุ๊กยกเลิกรายการ")
    const handover = await handoverByApi(page, asset.id, receiver.id, masters.goodConditionId)
    const reason = `${asset.assetTag} ส่งมอบผิดคน`

    await visitAssetPage(measure, asset.id, "asset handed over by mistake", testInfo)
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "การดำเนินการเพิ่มเติม" }).click()
    await page.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "ยกเลิกรายการ" }).click()
    const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ยืนยันการยกเลิกรายการ" })
    await expect(dialog).toBeVisible()
    await expect(dialog.getByText("กำลังตรวจสอบว่าสามารถยกเลิกได้หรือไม่")).toHaveCount(0)
    // The dialog says what it will undo: In Use with the receiver now, Ready in the first room after.
    await expect(dialog).toContainText("สถานะปัจจุบัน")
    await expect(dialog).toContainText("ใช้งานอยู่")
    await expect(dialog).toContainText("สถานะที่จะคืนกลับ")
    await expect(dialog).toContainText("พร้อมใช้งาน")
    const operator = (await dialog.locator("dt", { hasText: "ผู้ทำรายการเดิม" }).locator("xpath=following-sibling::dd[1]").innerText()).trim()
    await record(measure, "cancel dialog", testInfo)

    // A wrong entry first: a reason of three letters.
    await dialog.getByLabel("เหตุผลที่ยกเลิก").fill("ผิด")
    await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันยกเลิกรายการ" }).click()
    await expectFieldError(page, "transaction-cancel-reason", "กรุณาระบุเหตุผลอย่างน้อย 5 ตัวอักษร")
    await expect(dialog.getByLabel("เหตุผลที่ยกเลิก")).toBeFocused()

    await dialog.getByLabel("เหตุผลที่ยกเลิก").fill(reason)
    await expect(page.locator("#transaction-cancel-reason-error")).toHaveCount(0)
    const saved = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith(`/api/asset-checkouts/${handover.id}/cancel`) && response.request().method() === "POST")
    await measure.timed("cancel save", async () => {
      await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันยกเลิกรายการ" }).click()
      await saved
    })
    expect((await saved).status()).toBe(200)
    await expect(dialog).toBeHidden()

    // Back to the state before the handover.
    const after = await readAsset(page, asset.id)
    expect(after.status.name).toBe("Ready")
    expect(after.custodianId).toBeNull()
    await expect(page.getByTestId("asset-identity-summary")).toContainText("พร้อมใช้งาน")

    // The HO- document is VOID, with the reason, and still prints.
    await visit(measure, `/th/asset-management/checkouts/${handover.id}`, "VOID HO- document", testInfo)
    const banner = page.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: "VOID / ยกเลิกแล้ว" })
    await expect(banner).toBeVisible()
    await expect(banner).toContainText(reason)
    await expect(page.getByText(handover.documentNo, { exact: true }).first()).toBeVisible()
    await expectPrints(page, "พิมพ์ใบส่งมอบ")

    // The history keeps the handover and the cancel that undid it.
    await visitAssetPage(measure, asset.id, "asset history after the cancel", testInfo, "operations")
    const history = page.getByRole("main")
    await expect(history).toContainText("ส่งมอบทรัพย์สิน")
    const cancelEntry = history.getByText(/Checkout Cancel|ยกเลิก.*ส่งมอบ/).first()
    await expect(cancelEntry).toBeVisible()
    const staffName = readRunData().users.itStaff?.displayName ?? ""
    cancelled.set(testInfo.project.name, { history: (await cancelEntry.innerText()).trim(), operator, staffName })
    testInfo.annotations.push({ type: "cancel dialog operator shown", description: operator })
  })

  // Finding TX-A5: the cancel's movement has no Thai label (movementTypes has no checkout_cancel), so the Thai page shows
  // the raw type, "Checkout Cancel".
  test("the cancel reads in Thai in the asset's history (finding TX-A5)", async ({ e2eTheme }, testInfo) => {
    test.fail(true, "TX-A5: the history shows the cancel as Checkout Cancel")
    const shown = cancelled.get(testInfo.project.name)
    expect(shown, `the cancel test ran first (${e2eTheme})`).toBeTruthy()
    expect(shown?.history).not.toMatch(/Checkout Cancel/)
  })

  // Finding TX-A7: the cancel dialog's "ผู้ทำรายการเดิม" (who made the transaction) shows the user's id, a UUID, not the
  // person's name.
  test("the cancel dialog names the person who made the transaction (finding TX-A7)", async ({ e2eTheme }, testInfo) => {
    test.fail(true, "TX-A7: the dialog shows the operator's user id")
    const shown = cancelled.get(testInfo.project.name)
    expect(shown, `the cancel test ran first (${e2eTheme})`).toBeTruthy()
    expect(shown?.operator).toBe(shown?.staffName)
  })
})
```

```ts
// Spec §6.1.8: the desk delivery. IT staff take the asset to the receiver's desk and do the whole handover on their
// phone: taps, the keyboard over the form while the remark is typed (the save button stays reachable), a photo, and
// the receiver's signature with a finger on the IT staff's phone; the HO- document opens on the phone. Phone projects
// only: on the desktop the same handover is handover.spec.ts. Chromium, not WebKit (ruling TX-R3): Playwright 1.61.1
// draws a finger stroke only through Chromium's touch input.
import { asUser, expect, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { readRunData } from "../support/state.ts"
import {
  closePhoneKeyboard,
  createTxAsset,
  expectPrints,
  expectReachable,
  openPhoneKeyboard,
  PHOTO_FIXTURE,
  pickSearchable,
  readAsset,
  readAssetMasters,
  record,
  signOnPad,
  visitAssetPage,
} from "./tx-support.ts"

asUser("itStaff")

test("the desk delivery on the phone: the handover with the keyboard open and a finger signature, and the HO- document on the phone", async ({ page, measure, e2eDevice, browserName }, testInfo) => {
  test.skip(e2eDevice !== "phone", "the desk delivery is done on the phone (spec §6.1.8)")
  const receiver = readRunData().employees.receiver1
  const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
  const asset = await createTxAsset(page, masters, testInfo.project.name, "DSK", "โน้ตบุ๊กส่งถึงโต๊ะ")

  await visitAssetPage(measure, asset.id, "asset on the phone", testInfo)
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: "ส่งมอบ", exact: true }).first().tap()
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`/th/asset-management/checkout\\?assetId=${asset.id}`))
  await record(measure, "handover form on the phone", testInfo)

  await pickSearchable(page, "checkout-destination", receiver.code, `${receiver.code} - ${receiver.fullNameTh}`)
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: /มอบหมายใช้งานประจำ/ }).check()
  await page.locator("#checkout-condition").selectOption({ label: "ดี" })

  // The keyboard opens over the form while the remark is typed; the save button can still be reached.
  const remark = page.getByLabel("หมายเหตุ")
  await remark.tap()
  await openPhoneKeyboard(page)
  await remark.fill(`${asset.assetTag} ส่งถึงโต๊ะผู้รับ`)
  const save = page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true })
  await expectReachable(save)
  await measure.screenshot("handover form with the keyboard open")
  await closePhoneKeyboard(page)

  await page.locator("form input[type=file]").setInputFiles(PHOTO_FIXTURE)
  await expect(page.getByText("เลือกรูปแล้ว พร้อมบันทึกพร้อมรายการส่งมอบ")).toBeVisible()
  await signOnPad(page, page.getByRole("group", { name: "ลายเซ็นผู้รับ" }), e2eDevice, browserName)
  await measure.screenshot("the receiver signed with a finger")

  await save.tap()
  const review = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ตรวจสอบก่อนส่งมอบ" })
  await expect(review).toContainText(receiver.fullNameTh)
  await expect(review).toContainText("มีรูปหรือ ลายเซ็นแนบแล้ว")
  const confirm = review.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันส่งมอบ" })
  await expectReachable(confirm)
  await record(measure, "handover review on the phone", testInfo)
  const saved = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith(`/api/assets/${asset.id}/checkout`) && response.request().method() === "POST")
  await measure.timed("handover save on the phone", async () => {
    await confirm.tap()
    await saved
  })
  const checkout = (await (await saved).json()) as { id: string; documentNo: string; receiverSignature: string | null }
  expect(checkout.receiverSignature, "the finger signature is stored").toBeTruthy()

  await page.waitForURL(`**/th/asset-management/checkouts/${checkout.id}`)
  await expect(page.getByText(checkout.documentNo, { exact: true }).first()).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByRole("img", { name: "ผู้รับทรัพย์สิน" })).toBeVisible()
  await record(measure, "HO- document on the phone", testInfo)
  await expectPrints(page, "พิมพ์ใบส่งมอบ")

  const after = await readAsset(page, asset.id)
  expect(after.status.name).toBe("In Use")
  expect(after.custodianId).toBe(receiver.id)
})
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import test from "node:test"

// The E2E suite's field audit walk and review (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-e2e-workflow-suite-design.md
// §6.2; the audit tasks EA1–EA5). One test per task, appended in order. The walk itself runs with `npm run test:e2e`
// against a running app; these pin what can be checked without one. The working tree is CRLF on Windows: every read
// strips \r.
const read = (file: string) => readFileSync(file, "utf8").replace(/\r/g, "")
const SPEC = "e2e/audit/field-audit.spec.ts"
// The Thai words of one namespace of messages/th.json, so the walk's words are the app's own.
const thai = (namespace: string) => JSON.parse(read("messages/th.json"))[namespace] as Record<string, string>

// The text of one block of the spec: from its describe line to the next top-level describe (or the end).
function block(source: string, title: string): string {
  const start = source.indexOf(`test.describe("${title}"`)
  assert.ok(start >= 0, `the block "${title}"`)
  const next = source.indexOf("\ntest.describe(", start + 1)
  return source.slice(start, next < 0 ? undefined : next)
}

test("the walk's records carry the run id and the project within the schemas' limits; IT staff open the round on the form after its errors under the fields (e2e audit task 1, EA1)", async () => {
  const { auditRecords, projectCode } = await import("../e2e/audit/audit-plan.ts")
  const { E2E_PROJECTS } = await import("../e2e/support/matrix.ts")
  // The longest run id the guard makes (after Z: T and the time).
  const runId = "E2E-1019T2359"
  assert.deepEqual(E2E_PROJECTS.map((project) => projectCode(project.name)), ["DL", "DD", "PL", "PD"])
  const tags = new Set<string>()
  for (const project of E2E_PROJECTS) {
    const records = auditRecords(runId, project.name)
    const prefix = `${runId}-${records.code}`
    // src/lib/validations/department.ts code max 20, location.ts code max 50, the asset tag column 50.
    assert.ok(records.department.code.startsWith(prefix) && records.department.code.length <= 20, records.department.code)
    for (const room of Object.values(records.rooms)) assert.ok(room.code.startsWith(prefix) && room.code.length <= 50 && room.name.startsWith(runId), room.code)
    for (const asset of records.assets) {
      assert.ok(asset.tag.startsWith(`${prefix}-`) && asset.tag.length <= 50, asset.tag)
      assert.ok(asset.name.startsWith(`${runId} ${records.code} `), asset.name)
      assert.ok(!tags.has(asset.tag), `${asset.tag} is the project's own`)
      tags.add(asset.tag)
    }
    // Room A: matched, mismatch, the parent and its two components; room B: not found and offline; the outsider is the
    // run's department in room A.
    const where = Object.fromEntries(records.assets.map((asset) => [asset.key, `${asset.room}/${asset.department}`]))
    assert.deepEqual(where, {
      matched: "roomA/round",
      mismatch: "roomA/round",
      parent: "roomA/round",
      component1: "roomA/round",
      component2: "roomA/round",
      notFound: "roomB/round",
      offline: "roomB/round",
      outOfScope: "roomA/run",
    })
    assert.deepEqual(records.components.map((component) => component.key), ["component1", "component2"])
    assert.ok(records.round.name.startsWith(runId) && records.ownRound.name.startsWith(runId) && records.unknownTag.startsWith(prefix))
  }
  assert.throws(() => projectCode("setup"), /no short code/)

  // Created through the app: the places as the seed admin, the assets and the component install as the person.
  const data = read("e2e/audit/audit-data.ts")
  for (const route of [`"/api/departments"`, `"/api/locations"`, `"/api/assets"`, "/components`"]) assert.ok(data.includes(route), route)
  assert.ok(!/prisma|mssql/i.test(data.replace(/^\/\/.*$/gm, "")), "no direct database access")

  // The spec: WebKit on the phone, one serial chain, IT staff open the round on the form; the errors under the fields.
  const spec = read(SPEC)
  assert.ok(!spec.includes(`from "@playwright/test"`), "the suite's own test")
  assert.ok(spec.split("\n").includes("runPhoneOnWebKit()"))
  assert.ok(spec.split("\n").includes(`test.describe.configure({ mode: "serial" })`))
  const open = block(spec, "IT staff open the round")
  assert.ok(open.includes(`asUser("itStaff")`))
  assert.ok(open.includes(`await measure.visit("/th/audit/rounds/new", "round form")`))
  assert.ok(open.includes(`toHaveText("${JSON.parse(read("messages/th.json")).common.validation.required}")`), "the required error under the name")
  assert.ok(open.includes(`toHaveText("${JSON.parse(read("messages/th.json")).common.validation.endBeforeStart}")`), "the date pair error under the end date")
  assert.ok(open.includes(thai("auditRound").createTitle) && open.includes(thai("auditRound").statusOpen))
})
```
