- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** (exact):

  - Create `e2e/audit/audit-plan.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// What the field audit walk (spec §6.2) creates for one project, as plain data: its own department (the round's scope),
// two rooms, the assets of the walk and of the IT head's own round, and the words the walk types. Every code, tag and
// name carries the run id and the project's short code, so the four projects never share a record (spec §11 "Run
// order") and a person sees the marker in every list. tests/e2e-audit.test.ts pins it; e2e/audit/audit-data.ts creates
// it through the app. Imports nothing, so the unit test can load it.

// The project's short code in its records (desktop-light → DL).
export function projectCode(projectName: string): string {
  const code = { "desktop-light": "DL", "desktop-dark": "DD", "phone-light": "PL", "phone-dark": "PD" }[projectName]
  if (!code) throw new Error(`no short code for the project ${projectName}`)
  return code
}

export type AuditAssetKey = "matched" | "mismatch" | "parent" | "component1" | "component2" | "notFound" | "offline" | "outOfScope"

export type AuditAssetPlan = {
  key: AuditAssetKey
  tag: string
  name: string
  room: "roomA" | "roomB"
  // In the round's department, or the run's own (the out-of-scope asset).
  department: "round" | "run"
  category: "desktop" | "component"
}

// The walk (spec §6.2.2–3): in room A one matched, one mismatch (another department and the condition, with the photo),
// a parent with two installed components; in room B one not found and one saved offline; and one asset of the run's
// department in room A, outside the round's scope.
export function auditRecords(runId: string, projectName: string) {
  const code = projectCode(projectName)
  const prefix = `${runId}-${code}`
  const asset = (key: AuditAssetKey, suffix: string, name: string, room: AuditAssetPlan["room"], extra: Partial<AuditAssetPlan> = {}): AuditAssetPlan => ({
    key,
    tag: `${prefix}-${suffix}`,
    name: `${runId} ${code} ${name}`,
    room,
    department: "round",
    category: "desktop",
    ...extra,
  })
  const assets: AuditAssetPlan[] = [
    asset("matched", "A1", "จอภาพ", "roomA"),
    asset("mismatch", "A2", "เครื่องพิมพ์", "roomA"),
    asset("parent", "P1", "คอมพิวเตอร์ตั้งโต๊ะ", "roomA"),
    asset("component1", "C1", "หน่วยความจำ", "roomA", { category: "component" }),
    asset("component2", "C2", "ฮาร์ดดิสก์", "roomA", { category: "component" }),
    asset("notFound", "A4", "โปรเจกเตอร์", "roomB"),
    asset("offline", "A5", "สแกนเนอร์", "roomB"),
    asset("outOfScope", "X1", "โทรศัพท์", "roomA", { department: "run" }),
  ]
  return {
    code,
    department: { code: `${prefix}-D`, name: `${runId} ${code} แผนกตรวจนับ` },
    rooms: {
      roomA: { code: `${prefix}-RA`, name: `${runId} ${code} ห้อง A`, locationType: "Room" as const },
      roomB: { code: `${prefix}-RB`, name: `${runId} ${code} ห้อง B`, locationType: "Room" as const },
    },
    assets,
    components: [
      { key: "component1" as const, role: "หน่วยความจำ", slotNo: "1" },
      { key: "component2" as const, role: "ฮาร์ดดิสก์", slotNo: "2" },
    ],
    round: { name: `${runId} ${code} ตรวจนับประจำปี` },
    ownRound: { name: `${runId} ${code} รอบของหัวหน้า IT` },
    // A tag no asset has: the typed search finds nothing in the round and nothing in the register.
    unknownTag: `${prefix}-ZZ9`,
  }
}

export type AuditRecords = ReturnType<typeof auditRecords>
```

  - Create `e2e/audit/audit-data.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// The records of one project's field audit walk (e2e/audit/audit-plan.ts), created through the app (spec §7: no direct
// SQL write, so the app's own rules apply):
// - the round's department and its two rooms through the master-data APIs, as the seed admin (IT staff hold no
//   department or location permission; the setup project creates the run's own the same way);
// - the assets through POST /api/assets and the two components through the component install
//   (POST /api/assets/<parent>/components), as the person who registers them (IT staff, or the IT head for their own
//   round), so the asset rules and the install checks run.
// The category, status and condition ids come from the round form's own scope selects, the lists a person picks from.
import type { APIRequestContext, Page } from "@playwright/test"
import { auditRecords, type AuditAssetKey, type AuditRecords } from "./audit-plan.ts"
import type { RunData } from "../support/state.ts"

export type AuditMasterIds = { categoryDesktop: string; categoryComponent: string; statusReady: string; conditionGood: string; conditionFair: string }

export type AuditWalkData = {
  records: AuditRecords
  departmentId: string
  rooms: { roomA: string; roomB: string }
  assets: Record<AuditAssetKey, { id: string; tag: string; name: string }>
  masters: AuditMasterIds
}

async function created<T>(request: APIRequestContext, url: string, data: unknown): Promise<T> {
  const response = await request.post(url, { data })
  if (response.status() !== 201) throw new Error(`POST ${url} answered ${response.status()}: ${(await response.text()).slice(0, 300)}`)
  return (await response.json()) as T
}

// The options of one scope select of the round form (/th/audit/rounds/new), by its label.
async function selectOptions(page: Page, label: string): Promise<{ value: string; label: string }[]> {
  return page
    .getByLabel(label, { exact: true })
    .locator("option")
    .evaluateAll((options) => options.map((option) => ({ value: (option as HTMLOptionElement).value, label: option.textContent?.trim() ?? "" })))
}

function optionId(options: { value: string; label: string }[], match: (label: string) => boolean, what: string): string {
  const option = options.find((candidate) => candidate.value && match(candidate.label))
  if (!option) throw new Error(`the round form lists no ${what}`)
  return option.value
}

// Read on the round form, which the person opening the round has on screen.
export async function readAuditMasters(page: Page): Promise<AuditMasterIds> {
  const categories = await selectOptions(page, "หมวดหมู่ทรัพย์สิน")
  const statuses = await selectOptions(page, "สถานะทรัพย์สิน")
  const conditions = await selectOptions(page, "สภาพทรัพย์สิน")
  return {
    categoryDesktop: optionId(categories, (label) => label.startsWith("Desktop Computer - "), "Desktop Computer category"),
    categoryComponent: optionId(categories, (label) => label.startsWith("Computer Component - "), "Computer Component category"),
    statusReady: optionId(statuses, (label) => label === "พร้อมใช้งาน", "Ready status"),
    conditionGood: optionId(conditions, (label) => label === "ดี", "Good condition"),
    conditionFair: optionId(conditions, (label) => label === "พอใช้", "Fair condition"),
  }
}

// The department and the two rooms, as the seed admin. Before the round form opens: its scope list is the server's.
export async function createAuditPlaces(admin: APIRequestContext, run: RunData, runId: string, projectName: string) {
  const records = auditRecords(runId, projectName)
  const department = await created<{ id: string }>(admin, "/api/departments", { ...records.department, companyId: run.companyId, isActive: true })
  const roomA = await created<{ id: string }>(admin, "/api/locations", { ...records.rooms.roomA, branchId: run.branchId, isActive: true })
  const roomB = await created<{ id: string }>(admin, "/api/locations", { ...records.rooms.roomB, branchId: run.branchId, isActive: true })
  return { records, departmentId: department.id, rooms: { roomA: roomA.id, roomB: roomB.id } }
}

// The walk's assets and the parent's two components, as the signed-in person of this page.
export async function createAuditAssets(
  page: Page,
  run: RunData,
  places: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof createAuditPlaces>>,
  masters: AuditMasterIds,
): Promise<AuditWalkData> {
  const { records } = places
  const assets = {} as AuditWalkData["assets"]
  for (const plan of records.assets) {
    const asset = await created<{ id: string; assetTag: string }>(page.request, "/api/assets", {
      assetTag: plan.tag,
      name: plan.name,
      categoryId: plan.category === "component" ? masters.categoryComponent : masters.categoryDesktop,
      companyId: run.companyId,
      branchId: run.branchId,
      ownershipType: "shared",
      departmentId: plan.department === "round" ? places.departmentId : run.departmentId,
      currentLocationId: places.rooms[plan.room],
      homeLocationId: places.rooms[plan.room],
      statusId: masters.statusReady,
      conditionId: masters.conditionGood,
      remark: `${records.round.name} (E2E)`,
      // The schema takes a missing optional date or number only when its key is there (the asset form sends every
      // key; foundation note N3): a body without them answers 400 "required".
      licenseTotalSeats: null,
      licenseUsedSeats: null,
      purchaseDate: null,
      purchasePrice: null,
      warrantyStartDate: null,
      warrantyEndDate: null,
    })
    if (asset.assetTag !== plan.tag) throw new Error(`the asset was saved as ${asset.assetTag}, not ${plan.tag}`)
    assets[plan.key] = { id: asset.id, tag: plan.tag, name: plan.name }
  }
  for (const component of records.components) {
    await created(page.request, `/api/assets/${assets.parent.id}/components`, {
      componentAssetId: assets[component.key].id,
      componentRole: component.role,
      slotNo: component.slotNo,
      installedAt: null,
      reason: `${records.round.name} (E2E)`,
    })
  }
  return { records, departmentId: places.departmentId, rooms: places.rooms, assets, masters }
}
```

  - Create `e2e/audit/settle.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// axe on the page as the person reads it. A toast still sliding in (sonner's 400 ms opacity transition) reads as low
// contrast: the offline toast measured 4.04:1 at 0 ms and passed at 1.5 s in the rehearsal (note N-AU2). So before axe
// runs, wait until the toast region has no running transition.
import type { Page } from "@playwright/test"
import type { Measure } from "../support/measure.ts"

export async function settleToasts(page: Page) {
  await page.waitForFunction(() =>
    Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("[data-sonner-toaster]")).every((region) => region.getAnimations({ subtree: true }).length === 0),
  )
}

// measure.recordPage once the toasts have settled.
export async function recordSettled(page: Page, measure: Measure, label: string) {
  await settleToasts(page)
  return measure.recordPage(label)
}
```

  - Create `e2e/audit/field-audit.spec.ts` (LF) with exactly (its header names all seven blocks; EA2–EA5 add one import line each and append the rest):

```ts
// The field audit walk and its review (spec §6.2, the user's second worry), in all four projects, the phone on WebKit
// (spec §8). One serial chain per project, on that project's own records (e2e/audit/audit-plan.ts); each block is one
// person's part:
// 1. IT staff open the round of the project's department on the round form, after it shows its errors under the
//    fields (spec §6.2.1, §6.7);
// 2. IT staff walk room A by typing names and tags: one matched, one mismatch (another department and the condition,
//    with the photo the condition asks for), a parent whose components are not all checked, an unknown and an
//    out-of-scope search (spec §6.2.2);
// 3. in room B the phone goes offline, saves a check, comes back and sends it; one asset is marked not found from the
//    pending list (spec §6.2.2–3);
// 4. IT staff are refused the review of their own findings and the close of the round they opened (spec §6.2.4);
// 5. the IT head reviews every finding and closes the round (spec §6.2.4);
// 6. the IT head's own round: their own finding and their own close are refused, and the round is cancelled;
// 7. the audit exports by permission (spec §6.2.5, §6.5), expected to fail until finding AU1 and decision U1.
import { existsSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs"
import { asUser, expect, runPhoneOnWebKit, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { ADMIN_STATE_FILE, fromRoot, runDir } from "../support/paths.ts"
import { readRunData, readRunState } from "../support/state.ts"
import { createAuditAssets, createAuditPlaces, readAuditMasters, type AuditWalkData } from "./audit-data.ts"
import { recordSettled } from "./settle.ts"

runPhoneOnWebKit()
test.describe.configure({ mode: "serial" })

// The chain's state, one file per project in the run's folder (git-ignored): the records, the round, and the finding
// id the not-found save answers.
type AuditChain = AuditWalkData & { roundId: string; auditNo: string; notFoundFindingId?: string }
const chainFile = (project: string) => fromRoot(`${runDir(readRunState().runId)}/audit-${project}.json`)
function saveChain(project: string, chain: AuditChain) {
  writeFileSync(chainFile(project), `${JSON.stringify(chain, null, 2)}\n`)
}
function readChain(project: string): AuditChain {
  const file = chainFile(project)
  if (!existsSync(file)) throw new Error(`no audit chain for ${project}: the round was not opened in this run`)
  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(file, "utf8")) as AuditChain
}

// Bangkok's date as the date input holds it (YYYY-MM-DD), offset by whole days.
function bangkokDate(offsetDays = 0): string {
  return new Date(Date.now() + offsetDays * 86_400_000).toLocaleDateString("sv-SE", { timeZone: "Asia/Bangkok" })
}

test.describe("IT staff open the round", () => {
  asUser("itStaff")

  test("IT staff open the round of the project's department on the round form, after its errors under the fields", async ({ page, measure, playwright, baseURL }, testInfo) => {
    const run = readRunData()
    const { runId } = readRunState()
    const project = testInfo.project.name
    const admin = await playwright.request.newContext({ baseURL, storageState: fromRoot(ADMIN_STATE_FILE) })
    const places = await measure.timed("the department and the two rooms (seed admin)", () => createAuditPlaces(admin, run, runId, project))
    await admin.dispose()
    const { records } = places

    await measure.visit("/th/audit/rounds/new", "round form")
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "สร้างรอบตรวจนับ" })).toBeVisible()
    const masters = await readAuditMasters(page)
    const walk = await measure.timed("the walk's assets and components (IT staff)", () => createAuditAssets(page, run, places, masters))

    const save = page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true })
    const name = page.locator("#audit-round-name")
    // Spec §6.7: an empty required field first; the error shows under it and focus goes there.
    await save.click()
    await expect(page.locator("#audit-round-name-error")).toHaveText("กรุณาระบุข้อมูลนี้")
    await expect(name).toBeFocused()
    await expect(name).toHaveAttribute("aria-invalid", "true")
    await name.fill(records.round.name)
    await expect(page.locator("#audit-round-name-error")).toHaveCount(0)
    // Then the date pair reversed.
    await page.locator("#audit-round-start-date").fill(bangkokDate())
    await page.locator("#audit-round-end-date").fill(bangkokDate(-1))
    await save.click()
    await expect(page.locator("#audit-round-end-date-error")).toHaveText("วันสิ้นสุดต้องไม่ก่อนวันเริ่มต้น")
    await expect(page.locator("#audit-round-end-date")).toBeFocused()
    await page.locator("#audit-round-end-date").fill(bangkokDate(30))
    await expect(page.locator("#audit-round-end-date-error")).toHaveCount(0)

    // Open now, scoped to the project's department; the preview lists every asset of it and not the outsider.
    await page.locator("#audit-round-status").selectOption({ label: "เปิดตรวจ" })
    await page.getByLabel("แผนก", { exact: true }).selectOption({ label: `${records.department.code} - ${records.department.name}` })
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "ดูตัวอย่าง" }).click()
    await expect(page.getByText("ตัวอย่างรายการที่จะเข้ารอบตรวจนับ")).toBeVisible()
    await measure.recordPage("round form with its preview")
    const inRound = records.assets.filter((asset) => asset.department === "round")
    for (const asset of inRound) await expect(page.locator("form").getByText(asset.tag, { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.locator("form").getByText(walk.assets.outOfScope.tag, { exact: true })).toHaveCount(0)

    const created = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith("/api/audit-rounds") && response.request().method() === "POST")
    const round = await measure.timed("save the round", async () => {
      await save.click()
      const response = await created
      expect(response.status(), "the round is saved").toBe(201)
      await page.waitForURL(/\/th\/audit\/rounds\/[0-9a-f-]{36}$/)
      return (await response.json()) as { id: string; auditNo: string; generatedItems: number }
    })
    expect(round.generatedItems, "one item per asset of the department, the components included").toBe(inRound.length)
    await expect(page.getByText(`สร้างรอบตรวจนับสำเร็จ และสร้างรายการตรวจ ${inRound.length} รายการ`)).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`/th/audit/rounds/${round.id}$`))
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: records.round.name })).toBeVisible()
    await recordSettled(page, measure, "round page after the save")
    saveChain(project, { ...walk, roundId: round.id, auditNo: round.auditNo })
    expect(readChain(project).roundId, "the chain's state is saved for the next blocks").toBe(round.id)
  })
})
```

  - Create `e2e/audit/scan-steps.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// The steps a person takes on the audit scan page (/th/audit/rounds/<id>/scan), with the words on screen
// (messages/th.json auditScan): pick the room, type a name or a tag, open the check, change a field, add a photo, save,
// and read the banner. On a phone the check is a bottom sheet, on the desktop a panel beside the list; both hold the
// same form ([data-audit-check-form]).
import type { Locator, Page } from "@playwright/test"
import { expect } from "../support/fixtures.ts"

export const checkForm = (page: Page): Locator => page.locator("[data-audit-check-form]")
export const savedBanner = (page: Page): Locator => page.locator("[data-audit-scan-saved]")
export const offlineBar = (page: Page): Locator => page.locator("[data-audit-scan-offline]")
export const searchBox = (page: Page): Locator => page.getByLabel("ค้นหาทรัพย์สินในรอบนี้", { exact: true })

// The room sheet: opened by itself on the first visit of a round with several rooms, or from the room button. A room
// reads "<code> - <name>" (the round options of src/lib/audit-options.ts).
export async function pickRoom(page: Page, room: { code: string; name: string }) {
  const roomName = `${room.code} - ${room.name}`
  const sheet = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ห้องที่กำลังตรวจ" })
  if (!(await sheet.isVisible())) await page.locator("[data-audit-scan-room]").click()
  await expect(sheet).toBeVisible()
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: new RegExp(`^${escapeRegExp(roomName)}`) }).click()
  await expect(sheet).toBeHidden()
  await expect(page.locator("[data-audit-scan-room]")).toContainText(roomName)
}

// Type into the round's search once the last check has closed. On the phone the check is a modal sheet whose focus trap
// holds focus until its exit animation ends: text typed before that went nowhere in the rehearsal (phone-dark, note
// N-AU7), so the sheet must be gone first, and the box must hold the text.
export async function typeSearch(page: Page, text: string) {
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog")).toHaveCount(0)
  await searchBox(page).fill(text)
  await expect(searchBox(page)).toHaveValue(text)
}

// Type a name or a tag as a person does, then open the one result for the tag.
export async function findAndOpen(page: Page, typed: string, tag: string) {
  await typeSearch(page, typed)
  const results = page.locator("[data-audit-scan-results]")
  // The round holds one asset for each word the walk types (its own records).
  await expect(results.getByRole("status")).toHaveText("พบ 1 รายการในรอบนี้ · Enter = เปิดรายการแรก")
  await results.getByRole("button", { name: tagPattern(tag) }).click()
  await expect(checkForm(page)).toBeVisible()
  await expect(checkHeading(page, tag)).toBeVisible()
}

// The check's heading: the sheet's title on a phone, the panel's heading on the desktop.
export function checkHeading(page: Page, tag: string): Locator {
  return page.getByRole("heading", { name: tag, exact: true })
}

// One of the check's four fields (ที่ตั้ง, ผู้ถือครอง, แผนก, สภาพ): a searchable select. Types the search and picks the
// option whose label starts with the given text.
export async function chooseInCheckField(page: Page, label: "ที่ตั้ง" | "ผู้ถือครอง" | "แผนก" | "สภาพ", search: string, optionStart: string) {
  const field = checkForm(page).locator("[data-audit-check-field]").filter({ has: page.locator("label", { hasText: new RegExp(`^${label}`) }) })
  await field.getByRole("button").first().click()
  const searchInput = page.getByRole("combobox", { name: "ค้นหา" })
  await searchInput.fill(search)
  await page.getByRole("option", { name: new RegExp(`^${escapeRegExp(optionStart)}`) }).click()
  await expect(field).toHaveAttribute("data-mismatch", "true")
}

// The save button of the check names what it saves (saveAllMatch, saveMismatch, saveOutOfScope).
export const saveButton = (page: Page): Locator => checkForm(page).locator("[data-audit-check-actions]").getByRole("button", { name: /^บันทึก/ })

export async function addPhoto(page: Page, file: string) {
  await checkForm(page).locator('input[type="file"]').setInputFiles(file)
  await expect(checkForm(page).getByRole("list", { name: "รูปที่รออัปโหลด 1 รูป" })).toBeVisible()
}

// Spec §8: slow 4G on the phone's walk. WebKit has no network or CPU emulation, so each request of the page for a
// document, an RSC payload, a script, a style, a font or an image waits the round trip of a slow 4G link before it goes
// (latency only; the bandwidth is the machine's). The app's own API calls are left alone: WebKit cannot resend an
// intercepted request body when it retries on a fresh connection ("Send failed since rewinding of the data stream
// failed", rehearsal note N-AU8), and the app then queues the save as if offline. The CPU throttle of spec §8 is
// Chromium's (CDP) and is not applied on WebKit (ruling EA-R4).
export const SLOW_4G_ROUND_TRIP_MS = 150
export const isSlowedOnThePhone = (url: URL): boolean => !url.pathname.startsWith("/api/")
export async function slowNetwork(page: Page) {
  await page.route(isSlowedOnThePhone, async (route) => {
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, SLOW_4G_ROUND_TRIP_MS))
    await route.fallback()
  })
}

// The phone keyboard covers the lower half of the screen (spec §8 "the keyboard-open height"): 390×360 is what is left
// above it. The check's pinned save bar must stay on screen with the focused field above it.
export const KEYBOARD_OPEN_VIEWPORT = { width: 390, height: 360 }

export function escapeRegExp(text: string): string {
  return text.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&")
}

// A row or a result that names this tag.
export const tagPattern = (tag: string): RegExp => new RegExp(escapeRegExp(tag))
```

  - `e2e/support/measure-core.ts`, edit 1 of 1. Old:

```ts
// The attachment that carries a test's summary row from its fixture to the summary reporter.
```

  New:

```ts
// The browser's own error for a request made while the test has set it offline (context.setOffline, the audit walk's
// offline queue, spec §6.2.3): Chromium's net::ERR_INTERNET_DISCONNECTED; WebKit names every offline failure "WebKit
// encountered an internal error". Only the records made inside the test's offline window are the test's own (as an
// expected refusal is); the same text at any other moment still fails the test.
export const OFFLINE_ERROR_TEXT = /^(?:net::ERR_INTERNET_DISCONNECTED|WebKit encountered an internal error)$/
const OFFLINE_CONSOLE_TEXT = /^Failed to load resource: (?:net::ERR_INTERNET_DISCONNECTED|WebKit encountered an internal error)$/

// Splits the records from the window's first index on: the offline failures and their console lines become allowed
// entries ("offline-by-the-test"), everything else is kept as it was.
export function splitOfflineRecords(
  records: { console: readonly ConsoleRecord[]; failures: readonly FailureRecord[] },
  from: { console: number; failures: number },
): { console: ConsoleRecord[]; failures: FailureRecord[]; allowed: Classified["allowed"] } {
  const result: { console: ConsoleRecord[]; failures: FailureRecord[]; allowed: Classified["allowed"] } = { console: [], failures: [], allowed: [] }
  records.console.forEach((message, index) => {
    if (index >= from.console && OFFLINE_CONSOLE_TEXT.test(message.text)) result.allowed.push({ id: "offline-by-the-test", kind: "console", text: message.text })
    else result.console.push(message)
  })
  records.failures.forEach((failure, index) => {
    if (index >= from.failures && OFFLINE_ERROR_TEXT.test(failure.errorText)) result.allowed.push({ id: "offline-by-the-test", kind: "requestfailed", url: failure.url })
    else result.failures.push(failure)
  })
  return result
}

// The attachment that carries a test's summary row from its fixture to the summary reporter.
```

```ts
  // Open a page as a person does: navigate, wait for it to load, record its load time, and run axe on it.
```

  New:

```ts
  // The test takes the browser offline itself for the steps of step (spec §6.2.3, the audit walk's offline queue), and
  // the browser is back online when step ends. A request that fails meanwhile with the browser's offline error, and its
  // console line, are the test's own, as an expected refusal is: listed as allowed ("offline-by-the-test"). Any other
  // record, and any record after the window, still counts.
  async whileOffline<T>(step: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    const from = { console: this.consoleRecords.length, failures: this.failures.length }
    await this.page.context().setOffline(true)
    try {
      return await step()
    } finally {
      await this.page.context().setOffline(false)
      const split = splitOfflineRecords({ console: this.consoleRecords, failures: this.failures }, from)
      this.consoleRecords.splice(0, this.consoleRecords.length, ...split.console)
      this.failures.splice(0, this.failures.length, ...split.failures)
      this.offlineAllowed.push(...split.allowed)
    }
  }

  // Open a page as a person does: navigate, wait for it to load, record its load time, and run axe on it.
```


  - Create `e2e/audit/pending-steps.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// The round's pending list (/th/audit/rounds/<id>/pending, messages/th.json auditPending): find an item and mark it
// not found with the in-app dialog. The list is cards on the phone and a table on the desktop; both are in the page and
// one of them is hidden.
import type { Locator, Page } from "@playwright/test"
import { expect } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import type { E2eDevice } from "../support/matrix.ts"

// An item on the pending list: a card on the phone, a table row (labelled "ดู: <tag>") on the desktop.
export function pendingEntry(page: Page, device: E2eDevice, tag: string): Locator {
  return device === "phone" ? page.locator("article").filter({ hasText: tag }) : page.getByLabel(`ดู: ${tag}`, { exact: true })
}

// "บันทึกไม่พบ": a button on the phone card, an item of ⋯ in the desktop row. Confirms the dialog with no remark (none is
// needed) and answers the finding id the save returns. onDialog runs while the dialog is open (axe on it).
export async function markNotFound(page: Page, device: E2eDevice, tag: string, onDialog?: () => Promise<unknown>): Promise<string> {
  if (device === "phone") {
    await pendingEntry(page, device, tag).getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึกไม่พบ" }).click()
  } else {
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: `การดำเนินการของ ${tag}` }).click()
    await page.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "บันทึกไม่พบ" }).click()
  }
  const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "บันทึกทรัพย์สินนี้ว่าไม่พบ" })
  await expect(dialog).toBeVisible()
  await onDialog?.()
  const marked = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().includes("/api/audit-items/") && response.url().endsWith("/mark-not-found"))
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันว่าไม่พบ" }).click()
  const response = await marked
  expect(response.status(), "the not-found save").toBe(200)
  await expect(page.getByText("บันทึกว่าไม่พบ และสร้างรายการไม่ตรงรอพิจารณาแล้ว")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(dialog).toBeHidden()
  return ((await response.json()) as { findingId: string }).findingId
}
```

```ts
test.describe("IT staff in room B, offline, and not found", () => {
  asUser("itStaff")

  test("IT staff in room B: the phone goes offline, saves a check and sends it when it is back; one asset is marked not found", async ({ page, measure, e2eDevice }, testInfo) => {
    test.setTimeout(240_000)
    const project = testInfo.project.name
    const chain = readChain(project)
    const { records, assets } = chain
    if (e2eDevice === "phone") await scan.slowNetwork(page)

    await measure.visit(`/th/audit/rounds/${chain.roundId}/scan`, "scan page")
    await scan.pickRoom(page, records.rooms.roomB)
    const list = page.locator("[data-audit-scan-room-list]")
    const offlineRow = list.getByRole("button", { name: scan.tagPattern(assets.offline.tag) })
    await expect(offlineRow).toBeVisible()

    // Offline (spec §6.2.3): the save fails to reach the server, so the check is kept on the device and marked to send.
    // The first answer of the scan route is the queued check's, sent once the phone is back: waited for over the whole
    // offline window (20 s on the slow WebKit phone in the rehearsal, more than the 15 s action timeout).
    const sent = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith(`/api/audit-rounds/${chain.roundId}/scan`) && response.request().method() === "POST", { timeout: 90_000 })
    await measure.whileOffline(async () => {
      await expect(scan.offlineBar(page)).toContainText("ออฟไลน์ · รอส่ง 0 รายการ")
      await offlineRow.click()
      await expect(scan.checkHeading(page, assets.offline.tag)).toBeVisible()
      await scan.saveButton(page).click()
      await expect(scan.savedBanner(page)).toContainText(`${assets.offline.tag} เก็บไว้ในเครื่อง · จะส่งเมื่อมีสัญญาณ`)
      await expect(scan.offlineBar(page)).toContainText("ออฟไลน์ · รอส่ง 1 รายการ")
      await expect(offlineRow).toContainText("รอส่ง")
      await recordSettled(page, measure, "offline with one check in the queue")
    })

    // Back online: the queue is sent by itself.
    await measure.timed("back online: the queue is sent", async () => {
      expect((await sent).status(), "the queued check is sent").toBe(200)
      await expect(scan.offlineBar(page)).toHaveCount(0)
    })
    await list.getByRole("button", { name: /^ตรวจแล้ว/ }).click()
    await expect(list.getByRole("button", { name: scan.tagPattern(assets.offline.tag) })).toContainText("ตรวจแล้ว")

    // Not found: from the room's pending list, the in-app dialog, no remark needed.
    await list.getByRole("button", { name: /^ยังไม่ตรวจ/ }).click()
    await page.getByRole("link", { name: "ของที่หาไม่เจอ → รายการค้าง" }).click()
    await page.waitForURL(`**/th/audit/rounds/${chain.roundId}/pending**`)
    await expect(pending.pendingEntry(page, e2eDevice, assets.notFound.tag)).toBeVisible()
    await measure.recordPage("pending list of room B")
    const findingId = await measure.timed("mark not found", () =>
      pending.markNotFound(page, e2eDevice, assets.notFound.tag, () => recordSettled(page, measure, "not found dialog")),
    )
    await expect(pending.pendingEntry(page, e2eDevice, assets.notFound.tag)).toHaveCount(0)
    saveChain(project, { ...chain, notFoundFindingId: findingId })

    // The round has no item left to check (a GET of the round).
    const round = (await (await page.request.get(`/api/audit-rounds/${chain.roundId}`)).json()) as { items: { auditStatus: string; asset: { assetTag: string } }[] }
    expect(round.items.filter((item) => item.auditStatus === "pending").map((item) => item.asset.assetTag), "no item left to check").toEqual([])
  })
})
```

```ts
// The findings page (/th/audit/findings, messages/th.json auditFinding) and the refusals the UI never sends: find one
// finding, and send a request from the page as the person's browser would.
import type { Locator, Page } from "@playwright/test"
import type { E2eDevice } from "../support/matrix.ts"

// A finding on the findings page: a card on the phone, a table row on the desktop (both are in the page; one is hidden).
export function findingEntry(page: Page, device: E2eDevice, tag: string, typeLabel: string): Locator {
  const entries = device === "phone" ? page.locator("div.grid.md\\:hidden > div") : page.locator("tbody tr")
  return entries.filter({ hasText: tag }).filter({ hasText: typeLabel })
}

// A request from the page itself (the same session and cookies), for a refusal the UI never sends: the button is not
// offered, so the route's own answer is read this way.
export async function browserFetch(page: Page, url: string, method: string, body: unknown): Promise<{ status: number; body: unknown }> {
  return page.evaluate(
    async ({ url, method, body }) => {
      const response = await fetch(url, { method, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(body) })
      return { status: response.status, body: (await response.json().catch(() => null)) as unknown }
    },
    { url, method, body },
  )
}
```

```ts
test.describe("IT staff are refused the review and the close", () => {
  asUser("itStaff")

  test("IT staff are refused the review of their own findings and the close of the round they opened", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
    const chain = readChain(testInfo.project.name)
    if (!chain.notFoundFindingId) throw new Error("the not-found save left no finding id")
    await measure.visit(`/th/audit/findings?status=pending&roundId=${chain.roundId}`, "findings of the round (IT staff)")
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "รายการไม่ตรงจากการตรวจนับ" })).toBeVisible()
    // Every finding of the round is theirs: the review buttons give way to the segregation note.
    await expect(page.getByText("ไม่สามารถพิจารณารายการไม่ตรงที่คุณเป็นผู้รายงานเองได้").filter({ visible: true }).first()).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "อนุมัติ", exact: true })).toHaveCount(0)
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "ปฏิเสธ", exact: true })).toHaveCount(0)

    // The review route refuses them: IT staff hold no audit approve.
    measure.expectRefusal({ method: "POST", url: /\/api\/audit-findings\/[^/]+\/review$/, status: 403, reason: "IT staff hold no audit approve (spec §6.2.4)" })
    const reviewed = await review.browserFetch(page, `/api/audit-findings/${chain.notFoundFindingId}/review`, "POST", { action: "approve" })
    expect(reviewed).toEqual({ status: 403, body: { error: "Forbidden: insufficient permissions" } })

    // The round page offers them no close, and the close route refuses them: no approve, and they opened it.
    await measure.visit(`/th/audit/rounds/${chain.roundId}`, "round page (IT staff)")
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: chain.records.round.name })).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "ปิดรอบตรวจ" })).toHaveCount(0)
    measure.expectRefusal({ method: "PATCH", url: new RegExp(`/api/audit-rounds/${chain.roundId}$`), status: 403, reason: "IT staff, who opened the round, may not close it (spec §6.2.4)" })
    const closed = await review.browserFetch(page, `/api/audit-rounds/${chain.roundId}`, "PATCH", { action: "close" })
    expect(closed).toEqual({ status: 403, body: { error: "Forbidden: insufficient permissions" } })
  })
})

test.describe("The IT head reviews and closes", () => {
  asUser("itHead")

  test("the IT head reviews every finding of the round and closes it", async ({ page, measure, e2eDevice }, testInfo) => {
    test.setTimeout(240_000)
    const chain = readChain(testInfo.project.name)
    const { assets } = chain
    const runDepartment = runRecords(readRunState().runId).department.code
    await measure.visit(`/th/audit/findings?status=pending&roundId=${chain.roundId}`, "findings of the round (IT head)")

    // Each finding of the walk, approved or rejected with a remark in its dialog.
    const decisions: { tag: string; type: string; action: "approve" | "reject"; remark: string }[] = [
      { tag: assets.mismatch.tag, type: "แผนกไม่ตรง", action: "approve", remark: `ย้ายไปแผนก ${runDepartment} ตามที่พบ` },
      { tag: assets.mismatch.tag, type: "สภาพไม่ตรง", action: "reject", remark: "ตรวจซ้ำแล้ว สภาพยังดี" },
      { tag: assets.component2.tag, type: "ไม่พบทรัพย์สิน", action: "approve", remark: "ยืนยันไม่พบส่วนควบ" },
      { tag: assets.notFound.tag, type: "ไม่พบทรัพย์สิน", action: "approve", remark: "ยืนยันไม่พบ" },
      { tag: assets.outOfScope.tag, type: "ทรัพย์สินนอกขอบเขต", action: "approve", remark: "อยู่นอกขอบเขตรอบนี้จริง" },
    ]
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "อนุมัติ", exact: true })).toHaveCount(decisions.length)
    for (const [index, decision] of decisions.entries()) {
      const approve = decision.action === "approve"
      const entry = review.findingEntry(page, e2eDevice, decision.tag, decision.type)
      await expect(entry).toHaveCount(1)
      await entry.getByRole("button", { name: approve ? "อนุมัติ" : "ปฏิเสธ", exact: true }).click()
      const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: approve ? "ยืนยันการอนุมัติรายการไม่ตรง" : "ยืนยันการปฏิเสธรายการไม่ตรง" })
      await expect(dialog).toBeVisible()
      await dialog.getByLabel(approve ? "หมายเหตุการอนุมัติ" : "หมายเหตุการปฏิเสธ").fill(decision.remark)
      if (index === 0) await recordSettled(page, measure, "review dialog")
      await measure.timed(`${decision.action} ${decision.type} ${decision.tag}`, async () => {
        await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: approve ? "อนุมัติรายการไม่ตรง" : "ปฏิเสธรายการไม่ตรง" }).click()
        await expect(dialog).toBeHidden()
        await expect(entry).toHaveCount(0)
      })
      await expect(page.getByText(approve ? "อนุมัติรายการไม่ตรงและปรับข้อมูลทรัพย์สินสำเร็จ" : "ปฏิเสธรายการไม่ตรงสำเร็จ").first()).toBeVisible()
    }
    await recordSettled(page, measure, "findings of the round, all reviewed")

    // The approved department is the asset's now; the rejected condition is not (a GET of the asset).
    const mismatch = (await (await page.request.get(`/api/assets/${assets.mismatch.id}`)).json()) as { department: { code: string } | null; condition: { name: string } }
    expect(mismatch.department?.code, "the approved department").toBe(runDepartment)
    expect(mismatch.condition.name, "the rejected condition stays").toBe("Good")

    // The checklist is clear and the IT head did not open the round: the close goes through.
    await measure.visit(`/th/audit/rounds/${chain.roundId}`, "round page (IT head)")
    const close = page.getByRole("button", { name: "ปิดรอบตรวจ" })
    await expect(close).toBeEnabled()
    await expect(page.getByText("ผู้ปิดรอบต้องไม่ใช่ผู้สร้างรอบ")).toHaveCount(0)
    await close.click()
    const confirm = page.getByRole("alertdialog", { name: "ยืนยันปิดรอบตรวจนับนี้?" })
    await expect(confirm).toBeVisible()
    await recordSettled(page, measure, "close confirmation")
    await measure.timed("close the round", async () => {
      await confirm.getByRole("button", { name: "ปิดรอบตรวจ" }).click()
      await expect(page.getByText("ปิดรอบตรวจนับสำเร็จ")).toBeVisible()
      await expect(close).toHaveCount(0)
    })
    await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText("ปิดรอบ", { exact: true }).first()).toBeVisible()
    await recordSettled(page, measure, "round page, closed")
  })
})
```

```ts
test("the IT head's own finding and own close are refused by the segregation rules and the round is cancelled; the audit exports by permission (e2e audit task 5, EA5)", async () => {
  const { auditSegregationErrors } = await import("../src/lib/audit-segregation.ts")
  const spec = read(SPEC)
  const own = block(spec, "The IT head's own round")
  assert.ok(own.includes(`asUser("itHead")`))
  // The rules as the code has them (spec §6.2.4, the user's "เลือก 3"): the reporter may not review, the opener may not close.
  assert.ok(own.includes(`body: { error: "${auditSegregationErrors.reviewOwnFinding}" }`))
  assert.ok(own.includes(`body: { error: "${auditSegregationErrors.closeOwnRound}" }`))
  assert.ok(own.includes(thai("auditRound").closeSeparateApprover))
  assert.ok(own.includes(thai("auditRound").cancelReasonRequired) && own.includes(thai("auditRound").cancelSuccess))

  // The exports: expected to fail until finding AU1 (offered without audit:export) and decision U1 (no role holds it).
  const exports = block(spec, "The audit exports by permission")
  assert.ok(exports.includes(`test.fail(true, "AU1: `) && exports.includes(`test.fail(true, "U1: `))
  assert.ok(exports.includes(`page.getByRole("link", { name: "${thai("auditFinding").exportFindings}" })`))
  const { E2E_ROLE_PERMISSIONS } = await import("../e2e/support/run-plan.ts")
  for (const permissions of Object.values(E2E_ROLE_PERMISSIONS)) assert.ok(!permissions.includes("audit:export"), "no E2E role holds audit:export (U1)")
})
```

```ts
        name: records.ownRound.name,
        auditYear: Number(bangkokDate().slice(0, 4)),
        scopeDepartmentId: chain.departmentId,
        scopeLocationId: chain.rooms.roomB,
        includeClosedAssets: false,

        startDate: bangkokDate(),
        endDate: bangkokDate(7),
        status: "open",
      },
    })
    expect(opened.status(), "the IT head's round is saved").toBe(201)
    const own = (await opened.json()) as { id: string; auditNo: string; generatedItems: number }
    expect(own.generatedItems, "room B's two assets").toBe(2)

    // They mark one asset not found: the finding is theirs.
    await measure.visit(`/th/audit/rounds/${own.id}/pending`, "pending list of the IT head's round")
    const findingId = await pending.markNotFound(page, e2eDevice, assets.notFound.tag)

    // Their own finding: the note instead of the buttons, and the review route refuses it (segregation of duties).

    const entry = review.findingEntry(page, e2eDevice, assets.notFound.tag, "ไม่พบทรัพย์สิน")
    await expect(entry.getByText("ไม่สามารถพิจารณารายการไม่ตรงที่คุณเป็นผู้รายงานเองได้")).toBeVisible()
    await expect(entry.getByRole("button", { name: "อนุมัติ", exact: true })).toHaveCount(0)
    measure.expectRefusal({ method: "POST", url: new RegExp(`/api/audit-findings/${findingId}/review$`), status: 403, reason: "the reporter may not review their own finding (segregation of duties)" })
    const reviewed = await review.browserFetch(page, `/api/audit-findings/${findingId}/review`, "POST", { action: "approve" })
    expect(reviewed).toEqual({ status: 403, body: { error: "Audit finding must be reviewed by a different user than the reporter" } })

    // Their own round: the close waits with the rule beside it, and the close route refuses the opener (spec §6.2.4:
    // the rule as the code has it, src/app/api/audit-rounds/[id]/route.ts closeOwnRound).
    await measure.visit(`/th/audit/rounds/${own.id}`, "the IT head's round")

    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "ปิดรอบตรวจ" })).toBeDisabled()
    measure.expectRefusal({ method: "PATCH", url: new RegExp(`/api/audit-rounds/${own.id}$`), status: 403, reason: "the opener may not close their own round (closeOwnRound)" })
    const closed = await review.browserFetch(page, `/api/audit-rounds/${own.id}`, "PATCH", { action: "close" })
    expect(closed).toEqual({ status: 403, body: { error: "Audit round must be closed by a different approver than the creator" } })

    // The round is cancelled with a reason; an empty reason first shows its error under the field.
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "ยกเลิกรอบตรวจ" }).click()
    const cancel = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ยืนยันยกเลิกรอบตรวจ" })
    await expect(cancel).toBeVisible()
    await cancel.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันยกเลิก" }).click()
    await expect(page.locator("#audit-round-cancel-reason-error")).toHaveText("กรุณาระบุเหตุผลการยกเลิก")
    await expect(page.locator("#audit-round-cancel-reason")).toBeFocused()
    await page.locator("#audit-round-cancel-reason").fill("รอบทดสอบการแยกหน้าที่ของ E2E")
    await recordSettled(page, measure, "cancel dialog")
    await measure.timed("cancel the round", async () => {
      await cancel.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันยกเลิก" }).click()
      await expect(page.getByText("ยกเลิกรอบตรวจสำเร็จ")).toBeVisible()
    })
    await expect(page.getByText("รอบนี้ถูกยกเลิกและอ่านได้อย่างเดียว ไม่สามารถสแกน แก้ผลตรวจ พิจารณา หรือปิดรอบต่อได้")).toBeVisible()
    await recordSettled(page, measure, "the IT head's round, cancelled")
  })
})

// Spec §6.5: a role without the export permission does not see it; spec §6.2.5: the findings export downloads. No E2E
// role holds audit export (spec §7's table), yet the findings page offers the export to every audit viewer and the
// download is the route's 403 body: WebKit saves it as export.json, Chromium cancels it (finding AU1; decision U1 for
// the user). Expected to fail until then; Playwright reports it when it passes.
test.describe("The audit exports by permission", () => {
  test.describe("IT staff", () => {
    asUser("itStaff")

    test("IT staff, who hold no audit export, are not offered the findings export (finding AU1)", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
      test.fail(true, "AU1: the findings page offers the audit exports without audit:export")

      await measure.visit(`/th/audit/findings?status=all&roundId=${chain.roundId}`, "findings of the round, all (IT staff)")
      await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "รายการไม่ตรงจากการตรวจนับ" })).toBeVisible()
      await expect(page.getByRole("link", { name: "ส่งออกรายการไม่ตรง" })).toHaveCount(0)
      await expect(page.getByRole("link", { name: "ส่งออก PDF" })).toHaveCount(0)
    })
  })

  test.describe("IT head", () => {
    asUser("itHead")

    test("the findings export downloads the round's findings (decision U1)", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
      test.fail(true, "U1: no E2E role holds audit:export (spec §7)")
      const chain = readChain(testInfo.project.name)
      await measure.visit(`/th/audit/findings?status=all&roundId=${chain.roundId}`, "findings of the round, all (IT head)")
      const downloading = page.waitForEvent("download")
      await page.getByRole("link", { name: "ส่งออกรายการไม่ตรง" }).click()
      const download = await downloading
      // The file as downloaded, kept in the report (until U1: the route's 403 body, export.json, which Chromium cancels).
      const file = await download.path().catch(() => null)
      if (file) await testInfo.attach(`download ${download.suggestedFilename()}`, { path: file, contentType: "application/octet-stream" })
      expect(download.suggestedFilename(), "an Excel file").toMatch(/\.xlsx$/)
      expect(file, "the download completed").not.toBeNull()
      const workbook = new ExcelJS.Workbook()
      await workbook.xlsx.readFile(file ?? "")
      // The "Asset Tag" column (src/lib/audit-excel.ts auditFindingColumns), found by its header.
      const sheet = workbook.getWorksheet("Audit Findings")
      const header = (sheet?.getRow(1).values ?? []) as unknown[]
      const tagColumn = header.indexOf("Asset Tag")
      expect(tagColumn, "the Asset Tag column").toBeGreaterThan(0)
      const tags = new Set<string>()
      sheet?.eachRow((row) => tags.add(String(row.getCell(tagColumn).value ?? "")))
      for (const asset of [chain.assets.mismatch, chain.assets.component2, chain.assets.notFound, chain.assets.outOfScope]) {
        expect(tags.has(asset.tag), `${asset.tag} is in the export`).toBe(true)
      }
    })
  })
})
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import test from "node:test"

// The E2E suite's repair and PM area (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-e2e-workflow-suite-design.md §6.3, §6.7;
// tasks ER1–ER4). One test per task, appended in order. The specs run with `npm run test:e2e` against a running app;
// these pin what can be checked without one: who walks, the run marker on every asset, the wrong entry first, the
// expected-to-fail findings, the Thai copy against messages/th.json, and the outcomes against the app's own rules.
// The working tree is CRLF on Windows: every read strips \r.
const read = (file: string) => readFileSync(file, "utf8").replace(/\r/g, "")
const th = JSON.parse(read("messages/th.json"))
const titles = (source: string) => [...source.matchAll(/^test\("([^"]+)"/gm)].map((match) => match[1])
// Every write a file sends: request.post/put/patch/delete and the URL it names.
const writes = (source: string) => [...source.matchAll(/request\.(post|put|patch|delete)\(\s*[`"]([^`"]+)[`"]/g)].map((match) => `${match[1]} ${match[2]}`)

test("repair records: an unfinished record sends an asset in use to Under Maintenance and repaired brings it back In Use, IT staff, a wrong entry first (e2e repair task 1, ER1)", async () => {
  const spec = read("e2e/repair/repair-record.spec.ts")
  const data = read("e2e/repair/repair-data.ts")
  // Spec §2: IT staff open and close repair records; the phone runs on Chromium (WebKit only for the audit walk and the
  // desk delivery, spec §8).
  assert.ok(spec.split("\n").includes(`asUser("itStaff")`))
  assert.ok(!spec.includes("runPhoneOnWebKit()"))
  assert.ok(titles(spec).some((title) => title.endsWith("(ER1)")), "the ER1 test")
  // Every asset carries the run id and the project (spec §5, §11 "Run order"); the arranging writes are the asset and
  // its handover, through the app; nothing touches the settings, LDAP or the import.
  assert.ok(data.includes("const tag = `${readRunState().runId}-${testInfo.project.name}-${suffix}`"))
  assert.deepEqual(writes(data), ["post /api/assets", "post /api/assets/${assetId}/checkout"])
  assert.deepEqual(writes(spec), [])
  for (const banned of ["/api/admin/settings", "ldap", "import-confirm", "import-preview"]) assert.ok(!`${spec}${data}`.toLowerCase().includes(banned), banned)
  // Spec §6.7: an empty required field, then a date pair reversed, each shown under its field.
  assert.ok(spec.includes(`await expect(page.locator("#repair-record-problem-error")).toHaveText("${th.common.validation.required}")`))
  assert.ok(spec.includes(`await expect(dialog.locator("#repair-complete-return-date-error")).toHaveText("${th.repairRecord.returnDateBeforeReported}")`))
  // The Thai copy the walk reads is the app's own (messages/th.json).
  for (const text of [th.repairRecord.createTitle, th.repairRecord.doneNo, th.repairRecord.complete, th.repairRecord.completeTitle, th.repairRecord.status.in_progress, th.repairRecord.status.closed, th.repairRecord.outcome.usable, th.repairRecord.assetStatusEffect.toMaintenance]) {
    assert.ok(spec.includes(text), text)
  }
  // The outcomes the test asserts are the app's rules (src/lib/repair-record-policy.ts): an asset in use with an active
  // handover goes Under Maintenance, and repaired brings it back In Use.
  const policy = await import("../src/lib/repair-record-policy.ts")
  const inUse = { statusName: "In Use", ownershipType: "personal", custodianId: "employee", hasActiveCheckout: true, hasOpenRecord: false }
  assert.equal(policy.getRepairRecordCreateEffect(inUse, { done: false, outcome: "usable" }).nextStatusName, "Under Maintenance")
  assert.equal(policy.getRepairRecordCompleteEffect({ ...inUse, statusName: "Under Maintenance" }, "usable").nextStatusName, "In Use")
  assert.ok(spec.includes(`toBe("Under Maintenance")`) && spec.includes(`expect(after.status.name, "repaired, with an active handover: In Use").toBe("In Use")`))
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** (exact):

  - Create `e2e/repair/repair-data.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// The repair and PM area's data and shared steps (spec §6.3). Each test creates its own E2E assets through the app as
// IT staff (POST /api/assets with the asset form's own choices), hands one over for good when it needs an asset in use,
// and reads an asset back with a GET. Every asset tag carries the run id and the project, so the four projects never
// share an asset and a person sees the marker in every list. The arranging requests go through page.request, so they
// are not the steps under test; the walk itself is on the page.
import type { APIRequestContext, Locator, Page, TestInfo } from "@playwright/test"
import { expect } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import type { Measure } from "../support/measure.ts"
import { readRunData, readRunState } from "../support/state.ts"

export type AssetMasters = { categoryId: string; readyStatusId: string; goodConditionId: string }
export type AssetRow = { id: string; assetTag: string; name: string; status: { name: string; nameTh: string }; custodian: { code: string; fullNameTh: string } | null }

// Today in Bangkok as the date inputs hold it (yyyy-mm-dd), the day the server counts records by.
export function bangkokToday(now = new Date()): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { timeZone: "Asia/Bangkok", year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit" }).format(now)
}

// The day before a yyyy-mm-dd date.
export function dayBefore(date: string): string {
  const day = new Date(`${date}T00:00:00Z`)
  day.setUTCDate(day.getUTCDate() - 1)
  return day.toISOString().slice(0, 10)
}

// E2E-1019N-desktop-light-R1: the run, the project and the asset's role in the test (at most 50 characters).
export function areaTag(testInfo: TestInfo, suffix: string): string {
  const tag = `${readRunState().runId}-${testInfo.project.name}-${suffix}`
  if (tag.length > 50) throw new Error(`asset tag ${tag} is longer than 50 characters`)
  return tag
}

// The asset form's choices: the IT staff role has no category or status API, so they are read from the selects of
// /th/assets/new, on a page of their own (not the test's measured page): Notebook, Ready (พร้อมใช้งาน), Good (ดี).
export async function readAssetMasters(page: Page): Promise<AssetMasters> {
  const formPage = await page.context().newPage()
  try {
    await formPage.goto("/th/assets/new")
    const valueOf = async (select: string, label: RegExp) => {
      const value = await formPage.locator(`${select} option`).filter({ hasText: label }).first().getAttribute("value")
      if (!value) throw new Error(`the asset form has no ${label} in ${select}`)
      return value
    }
    return {
      categoryId: await valueOf("#asset-category", /^Notebook - /),
      readyStatusId: await valueOf("#asset-status", /^พร้อมใช้งาน$/),
      goodConditionId: await valueOf("#asset-condition", /^ดี$/),
    }
  } finally {
    await formPage.close()
  }
}

// One E2E asset in the run's department and room, Ready and Good.
export async function createAsset(
  request: APIRequestContext,
  masters: AssetMasters,
  asset: { tag: string; name: string; ownershipType: "personal" | "shared" | "stock" }
): Promise<AssetRow> {
  const run = readRunData()
  const response = await request.post("/api/assets", {
    data: {
      assetTag: asset.tag,
      name: asset.name,
      categoryId: masters.categoryId,
      companyId: run.companyId,
      branchId: run.branchId,
      ownershipType: asset.ownershipType,
      departmentId: run.departmentId,
      homeLocationId: run.locationId,
      currentLocationId: run.locationId,
      statusId: masters.readyStatusId,
      conditionId: masters.goodConditionId,
      // The schema takes a missing date, price or seat count only when its key is there (the asset form sends them).
      purchaseDate: null,
      purchasePrice: null,
      warrantyStartDate: null,
      warrantyEndDate: null,
      licenseTotalSeats: null,
      licenseUsedSeats: null,
      remark: `${readRunState().runId} ซ่อมบำรุง E2E`,
      isActive: true,
    },
  })
  if (response.status() !== 201) throw new Error(`POST /api/assets ${asset.tag} answered ${response.status()}: ${(await response.text()).slice(0, 300)}`)
  return (await response.json()) as AssetRow
}

// A permanent handover to an employee (no due date): the asset is In Use with an active handover, as a staff member's
// own notebook is when it goes for repair.
export async function handOverPermanently(request: APIRequestContext, masters: AssetMasters, assetId: string, employeeId: string) {
  const response = await request.post(`/api/assets/${assetId}/checkout`, {
    data: {
      handoverMode: "permanent_assignment",
      checkoutType: "user",
      custodianId: employeeId,
      checkoutDate: bangkokToday(),
      expectedReturnDate: null,
      conditionBefore: masters.goodConditionId,
      remark: `${readRunState().runId} ส่งมอบก่อนส่งซ่อม`,
    },
  })
  if (response.status() !== 201) throw new Error(`the handover of ${assetId} answered ${response.status()}: ${(await response.text()).slice(0, 300)}`)
}

export async function readAsset(request: APIRequestContext, assetId: string): Promise<AssetRow> {
  const response = await request.get(`/api/assets/${assetId}`)
  if (!response.ok()) throw new Error(`GET /api/assets/${assetId} answered ${response.status()}`)
  return (await response.json()) as AssetRow
}

// The searchable select of the repair and PM forms: open it, type the asset tag, pick the asset.
export async function chooseAsset(page: Page, triggerId: string, tag: string) {
  await page.locator(`#${triggerId}`).click()
  await page.getByPlaceholder("พิมพ์เพื่อค้นหา...").fill(tag)
  await page.getByRole("option", { name: new RegExp(`^${escapeRegExp(tag)} - `) }).click()
  await expect(page.locator(`#${triggerId}-value`)).toContainText(tag)
}

// The value beside a label of a key-value list (dl > dt + dd), for example สถานะปัจจุบัน on the repair record page.
export function valueBeside(scope: Page | Locator, label: string): Locator {
  return scope.locator("dt", { hasText: new RegExp(`^${escapeRegExp(label)}$`) }).first().locator("xpath=following-sibling::dd[1]")
}

// The record's state badge in the page header (ยังซ่อมไม่เสร็จ, ซ่อมเสร็จแล้ว, ยกเลิก).
export function headerBadge(page: Page): Locator {
  return page.locator('[data-slot="page-header-meta"] [data-slot="status-badge"]')
}

// The repair form's save: waits for the POST, checks it, and lands on the new record's page.
export async function saveRepairForm(page: Page, measure: Measure, label: string) {
  const created = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith("/api/maintenance-tickets") && response.request().method() === "POST")
  await measure.timed(label, async () => {
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
    await created
  })
  const response = await created
  expect(response.status(), label).toBe(201)
  const record = (await response.json()) as { id: string; repairNo: string; repairStatus: string }
  await page.waitForURL(new RegExp(`/th/maintenance/${record.id}(?:\\?|$)`))
  return record
}

function escapeRegExp(value: string): string {
  return value.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&")
}
```

```ts
// Repair records (spec §6.3 1–3), as IT staff on the computer (and the same walk on the phone): an unfinished record
// sends the asset to Under Maintenance, "repaired" brings it back by custody (In Use with its custodian, or Ready),
// "cannot repair, propose disposal" sends it to Pending Disposal, a mistaken record is cancelled, a second open record
// is refused, and the repair print opens. Each workflow starts with one wrong entry that shows under its field (§6.7).
import { asUser, expect, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { readRunData } from "../support/state.ts"
import {
  areaTag,
  bangkokToday,
  chooseAsset,
  createAsset,
  dayBefore,
  handOverPermanently,
  headerBadge,
  readAsset,
  readAssetMasters,
  saveRepairForm,
  valueBeside,
} from "./repair-data.ts"

asUser("itStaff")

test("an unfinished repair sends an asset in use to Under Maintenance, and repaired brings it back In Use with its custodian (ER1)", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
  const run = readRunData()
  const receiver = run.employees.receiver1
  const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
  const tag = areaTag(testInfo, "R1")
  const asset = await createAsset(page.request, masters, { tag, name: `${tag} โน้ตบุ๊กส่งซ่อม`, ownershipType: "personal" })
  await handOverPermanently(page.request, masters, asset.id, receiver.id)
  expect((await readAsset(page.request, asset.id)).status.name, "the asset is In Use before the repair").toBe("In Use")

  // The repair list, then its "บันทึกซ่อม".
  await measure.visit("/th/maintenance", "repair list")
  await page.getByRole("main").getByRole("link", { name: "บันทึกซ่อม", exact: true }).first().click()
  await page.waitForURL(/\/th\/maintenance\/new(?:\?|$)/)
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "บันทึกซ่อม" })).toBeVisible()
  await measure.recordPage("repair form")

  await chooseAsset(page, "repair-record-asset", tag)
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: "ยังซ่อมไม่เสร็จ (ส่งซ่อม / รออะไหล่)" }).check()
  await expect(page.getByText('ทรัพย์สินจะเปลี่ยนเป็น "อยู่ระหว่างซ่อม" จนกว่าจะกดซ่อมเสร็จ')).toBeVisible()

  // A wrong entry first (spec §6.7): no problem written. The error shows under the field and focus goes to it.
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
  await expect(page.locator("#repair-record-problem-error")).toHaveText("กรุณาระบุข้อมูลนี้")
  await expect(page.locator("#repair-record-problem")).toBeFocused()
  await measure.recordPage("repair form with its error")

  await page.locator("#repair-record-problem").fill(`${tag} จอไม่ติด ส่งร้านเปลี่ยนจอ`)
  await expect(page.locator("#repair-record-problem-error")).toHaveCount(0)
  const record = await saveRepairForm(page, measure, "save the unfinished repair")
  expect(record.repairStatus).toBe("in_progress")
  expect(record.repairNo).toMatch(/^MT-\d{8}-\d{4}$/)

  // The record page: unfinished, the asset Under Maintenance, the custodian kept.
  await expect(headerBadge(page)).toHaveText("ยังซ่อมไม่เสร็จ")
  await expect(valueBeside(page, "สถานะปัจจุบัน")).toHaveText("อยู่ระหว่างซ่อม")
  await expect(valueBeside(page, "ผู้ถือครอง")).toContainText(receiver.code)
  await measure.recordPage("unfinished repair record")
  expect((await readAsset(page.request, asset.id)).status.name, "an unfinished repair: Under Maintenance").toBe("Under Maintenance")

  // Repaired. A wrong entry first: the return date before the day the repair was recorded (a date pair reversed).
  const today = bangkokToday()
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "ซ่อมเสร็จ", exact: true }).click()
  const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "บันทึกว่าซ่อมเสร็จ" })
  await expect(dialog).toBeVisible()
  await dialog.locator("#repair-complete-return-date").fill(dayBefore(today))
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "ซ่อมเสร็จ", exact: true }).click()
  await expect(dialog.locator("#repair-complete-return-date-error")).toHaveText("วันที่ซ่อมเสร็จต้องไม่ก่อนวันที่แจ้งซ่อม")
  await expect(dialog.locator("#repair-complete-return-date")).toBeFocused()
  await measure.recordPage("repaired dialog with its error")

  await dialog.locator("#repair-complete-return-date").fill(today)
  await expect(dialog.locator("#repair-complete-return-date-error")).toHaveCount(0)
  await dialog.getByRole("radio", { name: "ใช้งานได้" }).check()
  const completed = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith(`/api/maintenance-tickets/${record.id}`) && response.request().method() === "PATCH")
  await measure.timed("save repaired", async () => {
    await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "ซ่อมเสร็จ", exact: true }).click()
    expect((await completed).status()).toBe(200)
  })
  await expect(dialog).toBeHidden()
  await expect(headerBadge(page)).toHaveText("ซ่อมเสร็จแล้ว")
  await expect(valueBeside(page, "ผลการซ่อม")).toHaveText("ใช้งานได้")
  await expect(valueBeside(page, "สถานะปัจจุบัน")).toHaveText("ใช้งานอยู่")
  await expect(valueBeside(page, "ผู้ถือครอง")).toContainText(receiver.code)
  await measure.recordPage("repaired record")
  const after = await readAsset(page.request, asset.id)
  expect(after.status.name, "repaired, with an active handover: In Use").toBe("In Use")
  expect(after.custodian?.code, "the custodian is kept").toBe(receiver.code)
})
```

```ts
  await expect(headerBadge(page)).toHaveText("ซ่อมเสร็จแล้ว")

  await expect(valueBeside(page, "สถานะปัจจุบัน")).toHaveText("รอตัดจำหน่าย")
```

```ts
test("a second record for an asset under repair is refused, and a mistaken record is cancelled with the asset back to Ready (ER3)", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
  const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
  const tag = areaTag(testInfo, "R3")
  const asset = await createAsset(page.request, masters, { tag, name: `${tag} เครื่องบันทึกผิด`, ownershipType: "stock" })

  await measure.visit(`/th/maintenance/new?assetId=${asset.id}`, "repair form")
  await page.locator("#repair-record-problem").fill(`${tag} บันทึกผิดเครื่อง`)
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: "ยังซ่อมไม่เสร็จ (ส่งซ่อม / รออะไหล่)" }).check()
  const record = await saveRepairForm(page, measure, "save the mistaken repair")
  await expect(valueBeside(page, "สถานะปัจจุบัน")).toHaveText("อยู่ระหว่างซ่อม")

  // No second open record: the asset search shows it, but it cannot be chosen, with the reason beside it.
  await measure.visit("/th/maintenance/new", "repair form, a second record")
  await page.locator("#repair-record-asset").click()
  await page.getByPlaceholder("พิมพ์เพื่อค้นหา...").fill(tag)
  const option = page.getByRole("option", { name: new RegExp(`^${tag} - `) })
  await expect(option).toContainText('ทรัพย์สินนี้มีงานซ่อมที่ยังไม่เสร็จ กด "ซ่อมเสร็จ" ที่บันทึกนั้นแทน')
  await expect(option).toHaveAttribute("aria-disabled", "true")
  await page.keyboard.press("Escape")
  await expect(page.getByPlaceholder("พิมพ์เพื่อค้นหา...")).toBeHidden()
  await expect(page.locator("#repair-record-asset")).toBeFocused()
  await expect(page.locator("#repair-record-asset-value")).not.toContainText(tag)
  // axe after the search closes (finding AR3 has its own test below: the open search hides the page with aria-hidden).
  await measure.recordPage("repair form after the refused search")

  // Cancel the mistaken record: the reason stays in its history, the asset goes back to Ready.
  await measure.visit(`/th/maintenance/${record.id}`, "mistaken record")
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "ยกเลิกบันทึก", exact: true }).click()
  const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ยกเลิกบันทึกนี้?" })
  await expect(dialog).toBeVisible()
  await dialog.locator("#repair-cancel-reason").fill(`${tag} เลือกเครื่องผิด`)
  await measure.recordPage("cancel dialog")
  const cancelled = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith(`/api/maintenance-tickets/${record.id}`) && response.request().method() === "PATCH")
  await measure.timed("cancel the mistaken record", async () => {
    await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันยกเลิก", exact: true }).click()
    expect((await cancelled).status()).toBe(200)
  })
  await expect(dialog).toBeHidden()
  await expect(headerBadge(page)).toHaveText("ยกเลิก")
  await expect(valueBeside(page, "สถานะปัจจุบัน")).toHaveText("พร้อมใช้งาน")
  await expect(page.locator("#history")).toContainText("ยกเลิกบันทึก")
  await expect(page.locator("#history")).toContainText(`${tag} เลือกเครื่องผิด`)
  // A cancelled record cannot be finished or cancelled again.
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "ซ่อมเสร็จ", exact: true })).toHaveCount(0)
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "ยกเลิกบันทึก", exact: true })).toHaveCount(0)
  await measure.recordPage("cancelled record")
  expect((await readAsset(page.request, asset.id)).status.name, "cancelled: back to Ready").toBe("Ready")
})

// Finding AR1 (expected to fail until it is fixed): the print sheet's fields are <dt>/<dd> pairs in a <div> grid with no
// <dl> (src/components/asset-operations/operation-document-print.tsx), an axe serious "dlitem" violation on every print
// (handover, return, transfer, disposal, repair). When AR1 is fixed this test passes and Playwright reports it.
test("the repair print opens with the record's number, state and outcome (ER3, finding AR1)", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
  test.fail(true, "AR1: the print sheet's dt/dd pairs sit outside a dl (axe dlitem, serious)")
  const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
  const tag = areaTag(testInfo, "R4")
  const asset = await createAsset(page.request, masters, { tag, name: `${tag} เครื่องพิมพ์บันทึกซ่อม`, ownershipType: "stock" })

  await measure.visit(`/th/maintenance/new?assetId=${asset.id}`, "repair form")
  await page.locator("#repair-record-problem").fill(`${tag} เปลี่ยนแบตเตอรี่`)
  const record = await saveRepairForm(page, measure, "save a finished repair")
  await expect(headerBadge(page)).toHaveText("ซ่อมเสร็จแล้ว")
  await page.getByRole("main").getByRole("link", { name: "พิมพ์" }).first().click()
  await page.waitForURL(new RegExp(`/th/maintenance/${record.id}/print`))
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "บันทึกการซ่อม" })).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText(record.repairNo).first()).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText(tag).first()).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText("ซ่อมเสร็จแล้ว").first()).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText("ใช้งานได้").first()).toBeVisible()
  await measure.recordPage("repair print")
})

// Finding AR3 (expected to fail until it is decided): while a searchable select is open, its modal popover hides the
// rest of the page with aria-hidden, and the page's links and buttons under it stay focusable: an axe serious
// "aria-hidden-focus" violation on every searchable select (src/components/ui/searchable-select.tsx, Radix Popover
// modal). Focus is trapped in the popover, so the controller decides: inert instead of aria-hidden, or a reviewed
// allow-list entry.
test("the open asset search passes axe (ER3, finding AR3)", async ({ page, measure }) => {
  test.fail(true, "AR3: the open searchable select leaves focusable content under aria-hidden (axe aria-hidden-focus, serious)")
  await measure.visit("/th/maintenance/new", "repair form")
  await page.locator("#repair-record-asset").click()
  await expect(page.getByPlaceholder("พิมพ์เพื่อค้นหา...")).toBeFocused()
  await measure.recordPage("repair form with the asset search open")
})
```

```ts
// One state change on a PM list row: its button, the confirm dialog and its button, the PATCH, the toast, the dialog
// closes.
export async function planAction(page: Page, row: Locator, action: string, dialogTitle: string, toast: string) {
  await row.getByRole("button", { name: action, exact: true }).click()
  const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: dialogTitle })
  await expect(dialog).toBeVisible()
  const changed = page.waitForResponse((response) => /\/api\/maintenance-plans\/[^/]+$/.test(response.url()) && response.request().method() === "PATCH")
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: action, exact: true }).click()
  expect((await changed).status(), action).toBe(200)
  await expect(page.getByText(toast)).toBeVisible()
  await expect(dialog).toBeHidden()
}

function escapeRegExp(value: string): string {
```

  - Create `e2e/repair/pm-plan.spec.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// The preventive maintenance (PM) plan (spec §6.3 4), as IT staff on the computer (and the same walk on the phone):
// create a monthly plan, find it in the "PM ถึงกำหนด" box, record it done, and see the next due date advance with the
// month-end clamp (31 Jan + 1 month = 28 Feb), then pause, resume and end it. The asset's status does not change. The
// create starts with one wrong entry that shows under its field (§6.7).
import { asUser, expect, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { areaTag, chooseAsset, createAsset, headerBadge, planAction, readAsset, readAssetMasters, saveRepairForm, valueBeside } from "./repair-data.ts"

asUser("itStaff")

// The PM list holds every plan of the database, active first; a page of 100 keeps the run's plan on the first page.
const PM_LIST = "/th/maintenance/pm?pageSize=100"

test("a monthly PM plan is created, recorded done on 31 Jan with its next due date clamped to 28 Feb, then paused, resumed and ended (ER4)", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
  const masters = await readAssetMasters(page)
  const tag = areaTag(testInfo, "P1")
  const asset = await createAsset(page.request, masters, { tag, name: `${tag} เครื่องสำรองไฟ`, ownershipType: "shared" })
  const title = `${tag} ตรวจเช็กประจำเดือน`

  // The PM page, then "เพิ่มแผน PM".
  await measure.visit(PM_LIST, "PM plans")
  await page.getByRole("main").getByRole("link", { name: "เพิ่มแผน PM", exact: true }).click()
  await page.waitForURL(/\/th\/maintenance\/pm\/new(?:\?|$)/)
  await measure.recordPage("new PM plan form")

  // A wrong entry first (spec §6.7): the asset chosen, no plan name. The error shows under the field.
  await chooseAsset(page, "maintenance-plan-asset", tag)
  await page.locator("#maintenance-plan-next-due-date").fill("2026-01-31")
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
  await expect(page.locator("#maintenance-plan-title-error")).toHaveText("กรุณาระบุข้อมูลนี้")
  await expect(page.locator("#maintenance-plan-title")).toBeFocused()
  await measure.recordPage("new PM plan form with its error")

  await page.locator("#maintenance-plan-title").fill(title)
  await expect(page.locator("#maintenance-plan-frequency")).toHaveValue("monthly")
  const createdResponse = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith("/api/maintenance-plans") && response.request().method() === "POST")
  await measure.timed("save the PM plan", async () => {
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
    await createdResponse
  })
  const created = await createdResponse
  expect(created.status()).toBe(201)
  const plan = (await created.json()) as { id: string; planNo: string; nextDueDate: string; planState: string }
  expect(plan.planNo).toMatch(/^PM-\d{8}-\d{4}$/)
  expect(plan.planState).toBe("active")
  await page.waitForURL(/\/th\/maintenance\/pm(?:\?|$)/)

  // Due on 31 Jan, so it is overdue and shows in the repair page's "PM ถึงกำหนด" box.
  await measure.visit("/th/maintenance", "repair list with PM due")
  const dueBox = page.locator("#pm-due")
  const dueRow = dueBox.locator("li").filter({ hasText: title })
  await expect(dueRow).toContainText(plan.planNo)
  await expect(dueRow).toContainText("เลยกำหนด")

  // Recorded done from the box: the form names the plan, the asset is fixed, and the date is the day the PM was done.
  await dueRow.getByRole("link", { name: "บันทึกว่าทำแล้ว" }).click()
  await page.waitForURL(new RegExp(`/th/maintenance/new\\?planId=${plan.id}`))
  await expect(page.getByText(`${plan.planNo} - ${title}`)).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.locator("#repair-record-asset")).toBeDisabled()
  await expect(page.locator("#repair-record-asset-value")).toContainText(tag)
  await expect(page.locator("#repair-record-problem")).toHaveValue(title)
  await expect(page.getByRole("radio", { name: "ซ่อมเสร็จแล้ว" })).toBeChecked()
  await measure.recordPage("PM done form")
  await page.locator("#repair-record-date").fill("2026-01-31")
  const record = await saveRepairForm(page, measure, "save the PM done")
  await expect(headerBadge(page)).toHaveText("ซ่อมเสร็จแล้ว")
  await expect(valueBeside(page, "แผน PM")).toContainText(plan.planNo)
  await expect(valueBeside(page, "สถานะปัจจุบัน")).toHaveText("พร้อมใช้งาน")
  await measure.recordPage("PM done record")
  expect(record.repairStatus).toBe("closed")
  expect((await readAsset(page.request, asset.id)).status.name, "a PM done leaves the asset's status as it was").toBe("Ready")

  // The next due date: 31 Jan + 1 month = 28 Feb 2026 (not 3 Mar). The edit page holds it as the date input's value.
  await measure.visit(`/th/maintenance/pm/${plan.id}/edit`, "PM plan edit page")
  await expect(page.locator("#maintenance-plan-next-due-date")).toHaveValue("2026-02-28")
  await measure.visit(PM_LIST, "PM plans after done")
  const row = () => page.getByRole("main").locator("li").filter({ hasText: title })
  await expect(row()).toContainText("กำหนด PM ครั้งถัดไป: 28/02/2569")
  await expect(row()).toContainText("ใช้งาน")

  // Pause: the plan leaves the due box and has no "บันทึกว่าทำแล้ว".
  await planAction(page, row(), "พักแผน", "พักแผน PM นี้หรือไม่", "พักแผน PM แล้ว")
  await expect(row().locator('[data-slot="status-badge"]').last()).toHaveText("พักแผน")
  await expect(row().getByRole("link", { name: "บันทึกว่าทำแล้ว" })).toHaveCount(0)
  await measure.recordPage("PM plans, plan paused")
  await measure.visit("/th/maintenance", "repair list, plan paused")
  await expect(page.locator("#pm-due")).not.toContainText(title)

  // Resume: active again, back in the due box.
  await measure.visit(PM_LIST, "PM plans to resume")
  await planAction(page, row(), "เปิดใช้งานต่อ", "เปิดใช้งานแผน PM นี้ต่อหรือไม่", "เปิดใช้งานแผน PM ต่อแล้ว")
  await expect(row().locator('[data-slot="status-badge"]').last()).toHaveText("ใช้งาน")
  await expect(row().getByRole("link", { name: "บันทึกว่าทำแล้ว" })).toBeVisible()
  await measure.visit("/th/maintenance", "repair list, plan resumed")
  await expect(page.locator("#pm-due").locator("li").filter({ hasText: title })).toContainText(plan.planNo)

  // End: for good. No record, no edit, no pause or resume.
  await measure.visit(PM_LIST, "PM plans to end")
  await planAction(page, row(), "จบแผน", "จบแผน PM นี้ถาวรหรือไม่", "จบแผน PM แล้ว")
  await expect(row().locator('[data-slot="status-badge"]').last()).toHaveText("จบแผน")
  for (const name of ["บันทึกว่าทำแล้ว", "แก้ไข"]) await expect(row().getByRole("link", { name })).toHaveCount(0)
  for (const name of ["พักแผน", "เปิดใช้งานต่อ", "จบแผน"]) await expect(row().getByRole("button", { name })).toHaveCount(0)
  await measure.recordPage("PM plans, plan ended")
  expect((await readAsset(page.request, asset.id)).status.name, "the plan's states leave the asset's status as it was").toBe("Ready")
})
```
