```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import { existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import test from "node:test"
import { E2E_PROJECTS } from "../e2e/support/matrix.ts"

// The E2E suite's disposal workflows (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-e2e-workflow-suite-design.md §6.4; tasks
// ED1–ED3). One test per task, appended in order. The specs run with `npm run test:e2e` against a running app; these
// pin what can be checked without one: who walks each step, the refusals asserted, each test's own records, and the
// findings kept as expected failures. The working tree is CRLF on Windows: every read strips \r.
const read = (file: string) => readFileSync(file, "utf8").replace(/\r/g, "")
const disposalSpecs = () => (existsSync("e2e/disposal") ? readdirSync("e2e/disposal").filter((file) => file.endsWith(".spec.ts")).sort() : [])

test("one disposal is requested, approved alone and executed with evidence by the right people, and the print opens (e2e disposal task 1, ED1)", () => {
  const support = read("e2e/disposal/disposal-support.ts")
  // The four projects' codes in record tags are the projects of the matrix, each its own.
  const codes = Object.fromEntries([...support.matchAll(/"((?:desktop|phone)-(?:light|dark))": "([A-Z]{2})"/g)].map((match) => [match[1], match[2]]))
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(codes), E2E_PROJECTS.map((project) => project.name))
  assert.equal(new Set(Object.values(codes)).size, 4)
  // The person changes inside a test by the run's saved sessions, never a sign-in; the theme cookie stays.
  assert.ok(support.includes("await context.clearCookies()"), "actAs drops the session it had")
  assert.ok(support.includes(`{ name: "theme", value: theme, url: baseURL }`), "actAs keeps the project's theme")
  assert.ok(!/signInOnce|\/login/.test(support), "no sign-in in the disposal helpers")
  // A room of the test's own, made by the seed admin as the setup makes the run's room; assets through the asset API.
  assert.ok(support.includes(`storageState: fromRoot(ADMIN_STATE_FILE)`), "the seed admin's saved session")
  assert.ok(support.includes(`locationType: "Room"`), "a room")
  assert.ok(support.includes(`page.request.post("/api/assets"`), "assets through the asset API")
  for (const key of ["licenseTotalSeats", "licenseUsedSeats", "purchaseDate", "purchasePrice", "warrantyStartDate", "warrantyEndDate"]) {
    assert.ok(support.includes(`${key}: null,`), `${key} is sent`)
  }
  // A toast is read by axe only once its fade-in has ended.
  assert.ok(support.includes("getAnimations({ subtree: true }).length === 0"), "expectToast waits for the fade")

  assert.deepEqual(disposalSpecs().filter((file) => file === "single-request.spec.ts"), ["single-request.spec.ts"])
  const spec = read("e2e/disposal/single-request.spec.ts")
  assert.ok(spec.includes(`from "../support/fixtures.ts"`) && !spec.includes(`from "@playwright/test"`), "the suite's test")
  assert.ok(spec.split("\n").includes(`asUser("itStaff")`), "IT staff walk it")
  assert.ok(spec.includes(`recordPrefix(testInfo, "DSP1")`), "its own records")
  for (const step of [
    `await measure.visit("/th/disposal/new"`,
    `"เหตุผลการตัดจำหน่ายต้องมีอย่างน้อย 12 ตัวอักษร"`,
    `expect(staffDecision.status(), "IT staff may not approve").toBe(403)`,
    `admin.post("/api/disposal-requests/bulk-decision"`,
    `await actAs(page, "itHead", e2eTheme)`,
    `"กรุณาระบุเหตุผลที่ไม่อนุมัติ"`,
    `expect(headExecuteBody.code).toBe("DISPOSAL_SOD_CONFLICT")`,
    `await actAs(page, "itStaff", e2eTheme)`,
    `"กรุณาแนบหลักฐานก่อนบันทึกการตัดจำหน่ายจริง"`,
    `setInputFiles(EVIDENCE_PDF)`,
    `"กรุณากรอกเลขที่เอกสารอ้างอิง"`,
    `.toBe("Disposed")`,
    `"ใบอนุมัติตัดจำหน่ายทรัพย์สิน"`,
  ]) assert.ok(spec.includes(step), step)
  // Finding D1 stays an expected failure until the print layout is fixed.
  assert.ok(spec.includes(`test.fail(true, "finding D1:`), "D1 expected to fail")
  assert.ok(spec.includes(`test.describe.configure({ mode: "serial" })`), "the print test follows the workflow")
})
```

```ts
// The disposal workflows' helpers (spec §6.4). Each test makes its own records: a room of its own (the seed admin
// creates it, as the setup does), assets in it, then the requests the people walk. Every code, tag and reason carries
// the run id and the project, so the four projects never share a record and a person sees the marker in every list.
// The person at the device changes inside one test (IT staff request, the IT head approves, IT staff execute): actAs
// swaps the page's session for that user's saved one, so the one page, and its measurements, follow the whole workflow.
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import { request as playwrightRequest, type APIRequestContext, type Locator, type Page, type TestInfo } from "@playwright/test"
import { expect } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import type { E2eTheme } from "../support/matrix.ts"
import type { Measure } from "../support/measure.ts"
import { ADMIN_STATE_FILE, authStateFile, fromRoot } from "../support/paths.ts"
import type { E2eUserKey } from "../support/run-plan.ts"
import { readRunData, readRunState, type RunData } from "../support/state.ts"

// The four projects' short codes, for tags and codes that have a length limit (location code 50, asset tag 50).
const PROJECT_CODES: Record<string, string> = { "desktop-light": "DL", "desktop-dark": "DD", "phone-light": "PL", "phone-dark": "PD" }

export function projectCode(testInfo: TestInfo): string {
  const code = PROJECT_CODES[testInfo.project.name]
  if (!code) throw new Error(`no project code for ${testInfo.project.name}`)
  return code
}

// The marker of one test's records: E2E-1019N-DL-DSP1 (run, project, workflow).
export function recordPrefix(testInfo: TestInfo, workflow: string): string {
  return `${readRunState().runId}-${projectCode(testInfo)}-${workflow}`
}

// The person at the device changes: the page drops the session it has and takes the user's saved one (the run's
// storage state, from the setup's single sign-in each), keeping the project's theme. No sign-in happens.
export async function actAs(page: Page, user: E2eUserKey, theme: E2eTheme) {
  const state = JSON.parse(readFileSync(fromRoot(authStateFile(readRunState().runId, user)), "utf8")) as {
    cookies: Parameters<ReturnType<Page["context"]>["addCookies"]>[0]
  }
  const context = page.context()
  const baseURL = readRunState().baseURL
  await context.clearCookies()
  await context.addCookies([...state.cookies, { name: "theme", value: theme, url: baseURL }])
}

// The seed admin's API, for what only an admin may create (a room): the same storage state the setup uses.
export async function seedAdminApi(): Promise<APIRequestContext> {
  return playwrightRequest.newContext({ baseURL: readRunState().baseURL, storageState: fromRoot(ADMIN_STATE_FILE) })
}

async function created<T>(response: Awaited<ReturnType<APIRequestContext["post"]>>, what: string): Promise<T> {
  if (response.status() !== 201) throw new Error(`${what} answered ${response.status()}: ${(await response.text()).slice(0, 300)}`)
  return (await response.json()) as T
}

// A room of the test's own (spec §5: the suite creates its own data), so no other workflow's audit round scoped to the
// run's room reaches these assets, and this test's open round reaches nothing else.
export async function createRoom(code: string, name: string): Promise<string> {
  const admin = await seedAdminApi()
  try {
    const { branchId } = readRunData()
    const room = await created<{ id: string }>(
      await admin.post("/api/locations", { data: { code, name, locationType: "Room", branchId, isActive: true } }),
      `POST /api/locations ${code}`,
    )
    return room.id
  } finally {
    await admin.dispose()
  }
}

export type AssetChoices = { categoryId: string; statusId: string; conditionId: string }

// IT staff open the asset form (a measured page) and the choices a new asset needs are read off its selects: the first
// category, the Ready status ("พร้อมใช้งาน") and the first condition. These are the ids the form itself would send.
export async function readAssetChoices(page: Page, measure: Measure): Promise<AssetChoices> {
  await measure.visit("/th/assets/new", "asset form (choices for the disposal assets)")
  const firstValue = async (select: Locator) =>
    select.locator("option").evaluateAll((options) => (options as HTMLOptionElement[]).map((option) => option.value).find(Boolean) ?? "")
  const categoryId = await firstValue(page.locator("#asset-category"))
  const conditionId = await firstValue(page.locator("#asset-condition"))
  const statusId = await page.locator("#asset-status option", { hasText: /^พร้อมใช้งาน$/ }).getAttribute("value")
  if (!categoryId || !conditionId || !statusId) throw new Error("the asset form has no category, condition or Ready status to choose")
  return { categoryId, statusId, conditionId }
}

export type DisposalAsset = { id: string; assetTag: string; name: string }

// Assets in the test's room, Ready with no holder (a disposal can start from there), created by the user signed in on
// the page through the asset API the form posts to.
export async function createAssets(page: Page, choices: AssetChoices, roomId: string, tags: readonly string[]): Promise<DisposalAsset[]> {
  const data: RunData = readRunData()
  const assets: DisposalAsset[] = []
  for (const assetTag of tags) {
    const name = `${assetTag} เครื่องทดสอบตัดจำหน่าย`
    const asset = await created<{ id: string; assetTag: string }>(
      await page.request.post("/api/assets", {
        data: {
          assetTag,
          name,
          categoryId: choices.categoryId,
          companyId: data.companyId,
          branchId: data.branchId,
          currentLocationId: roomId,
          homeLocationId: roomId,
          statusId: choices.statusId,
          conditionId: choices.conditionId,
          remark: `${assetTag} สร้างโดยชุดทดสอบ E2E`,
          // The schema's preprocessed optional numbers and dates need their keys present (the form always sends
          // them; foundation note N3 is the same zod behaviour).
          licenseTotalSeats: null,
          licenseUsedSeats: null,
          purchaseDate: null,
          purchasePrice: null,
          warrantyStartDate: null,
          warrantyEndDate: null,
        },
      }),
      `POST /api/assets ${assetTag}`,
    )
    assets.push({ id: asset.id, assetTag: asset.assetTag, name })
  }
  return assets
}

// The run's employee a person picks (the requester, the executor): the option reads "<code> - <name>".
export function employeeLabel(key: string): string {
  const employee = readRunData().employees[key]
  if (!employee) throw new Error(`the run has no ${key} employee`)
  return `${employee.code} - ${employee.fullNameTh}`
}

// A SearchableSelect (a button that opens a searchable list): open it, type the code, choose the option.
export async function chooseSearchable(page: Page, triggerId: string, label: string) {
  await page.locator(`#${triggerId}`).click()
  await page.getByRole("combobox").last().fill(label.split(" - ")[0])
  await page.getByRole("option", { name: label }).click()
  await expect(page.locator(`#${triggerId}`)).toContainText(label)
}

// The disposal asset picker: type the tag, wait for the search, choose the asset's option.
export async function pickDisposalAsset(page: Page, search: Locator, asset: DisposalAsset) {
  await search.fill(asset.assetTag)
  await page.getByRole("option", { name: new RegExp(escapeRegExp(asset.assetTag)) }).click()
}

// A sonner toast with this text.
export function toast(page: Page, text: string | RegExp): Locator {
  return page.locator("[data-sonner-toast]").filter({ hasText: text })
}

// The toast shows, and its fade-in has ended: sonner fades a toast in, and axe reads a fading toast's colors at the
// opacity of that moment (a color-contrast failure the person never sees once it has settled).
export async function expectToast(page: Page, text: string | RegExp): Promise<Locator> {
  const item = toast(page, text)
  await expect(item).toBeVisible()
  await expect
    .poll(() => item.evaluate((element) => element.getAnimations({ subtree: true }).length === 0 && getComputedStyle(element).opacity === "1"))
    .toBe(true)
  return item
}

// The button on screen: the detail pages render the next action twice (the header on the desktop, the action bar on
// the phone), and only one of them shows.
export function visibleButton(page: Page, name: string | RegExp): Locator {
  return page.getByRole("button", { name }).filter({ visible: true })
}

export function visibleLink(page: Page, name: string | RegExp): Locator {
  return page.getByRole("link", { name }).filter({ visible: true })
}

// A bulk execution of one approved request, up to its preview, from the approved tab of the queue: select the row, fill
// the shared values (executor, final status Disposed, shared recipient) and ask for the preview. Returns the dialog once
// the preview has answered (its counts show).
export async function openBulkExecutionPreview(page: Page, asset: DisposalAsset, executor: string, recipient: string): Promise<Locator> {
  const toggle = page.getByRole("button", { name: "เลือกหลายรายการ" }).filter({ visible: true })
  if (await toggle.count()) await toggle.click()
  // The bulk execution names each row by the asset tag and name ("ทรัพย์สิน <tag> - <name>").
  await page.getByRole("checkbox", { name: new RegExp(`ทรัพย์สิน ${escapeRegExp(asset.assetTag)} - `) }).filter({ visible: true }).check()
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "ตรวจสอบการดำเนินการ" }).click()
  const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ตรวจสอบการดำเนินการตัดจำหน่ายแบบกลุ่ม" })
  await dialog.locator("#disposal-bulk-execution-executor").selectOption({ label: executor })
  await dialog.locator("#disposal-bulk-execution-status").selectOption({ label: "ตัดจำหน่ายแล้ว" })
  await dialog.locator("#disposal-bulk-execution-recipient").fill(recipient)
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันดำเนินการ 1 รายการ" }).click()
  await expect(dialog.getByText("ดำเนินการได้", { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
  return dialog
}

export function escapeRegExp(value: string): string {
  return value.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&")
}

export const EVIDENCE_PDF = "e2e/fixtures/evidence.pdf"
```

  - Create `e2e/disposal/single-request.spec.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// Disposal of one asset (spec §6.4.1–3, §6.7): IT staff request it, the IT head approves it alone, IT staff execute it
// with evidence, and the disposal print opens. On the way: a wrong entry first in each form (the error shows under the
// field, then the corrected entry saves); IT staff have no approve, nor has the unlinked seed admin; the approver cannot
// execute; execution waits for evidence.
import { asUser, expect, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { readRunData } from "../support/state.ts"
import {
  actAs,
  chooseSearchable,
  createAssets,
  createRoom,
  employeeLabel,
  EVIDENCE_PDF,
  expectToast,
  pickDisposalAsset,
  readAssetChoices,
  recordPrefix,
  seedAdminApi,
  visibleButton,
  visibleLink,
} from "./disposal-support.ts"

asUser("itStaff")
// The second test reads the print of the first one's request: one after the other, and the second skips if the first
// fails.
test.describe.configure({ mode: "serial" })

let printPath = ""

test("IT staff request one disposal, the IT head approves it, IT staff execute it with evidence, and the print opens", async ({ page, measure, e2eTheme }, testInfo) => {
  const prefix = recordPrefix(testInfo, "DSP1")
  const roomId = await createRoom(`${prefix}-R`, `${prefix} ห้องทดสอบตัดจำหน่าย`)
  const choices = await readAssetChoices(page, measure)
  const [asset] = await createAssets(page, choices, roomId, [`${prefix}-A1`])
  const reason = `${prefix} ชำรุดเกินซ่อม เสนอตัดจำหน่าย`

  // 1. IT staff request it. A reason under 12 characters first: the error shows under the field and nothing is sent.
  await measure.visit("/th/disposal/new", "IT staff: new disposal request")
  await pickDisposalAsset(page, page.locator("#disposal-request-asset"), asset)
  await expect(page.getByText(`${asset.assetTag} - ${asset.name}`)).toBeVisible()
  await chooseSearchable(page, "disposal-request-requested-by", employeeLabel("itStaff"))
  const reasonField = page.locator("#disposal-request-reason")
  await reasonField.fill("สั้นไป")
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
  await expect(page.locator("#disposal-request-reason-error")).toHaveText("เหตุผลการตัดจำหน่ายต้องมีอย่างน้อย 12 ตัวอักษร")
  await expect(reasonField).toHaveAttribute("aria-invalid", "true")
  await expect(reasonField).toBeFocused()
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: the short reason's error under the field")
  await reasonField.fill(reason)
  await expect(page.locator("#disposal-request-reason-error")).toHaveCount(0)
  await measure.timed("IT staff: save the request", async () => {
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
    await page.waitForURL(/\/th\/disposal\/[0-9a-f-]{36}$/)
  })
  await expectToast(page, "สร้างคำขอตัดจำหน่ายสำเร็จ")
  const requestUrl = new URL(page.url()).pathname
  const requestId = requestUrl.split("/").pop() as string
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(asset.name)
  const disposalNo = (await page.locator("header .tag").first().textContent())?.trim() ?? ""
  expect(disposalNo).toMatch(/^DP-\d{8}-\d{4}$/)
  await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText("รออนุมัติ").first()).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText("รอตัดจำหน่าย").first()).toBeVisible()
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: the request, pending approval")

  // IT staff have no approve: no review on the page, and the decision API refuses them.
  await expect(visibleButton(page, "พิจารณาคำขอ")).toHaveCount(0)
  const staffDecision = await page.request.patch(`/api/disposal-requests/${requestId}`, { data: { decision: "approve", nextStatusId: choices.statusId } })
  expect(staffDecision.status(), "IT staff may not approve").toBe(403)

  // The seed admin holds every permission but no employee link, so segregation of duties keeps them from approving
  // (spec §6.4.2: the approver must be linked to an employee). A preview writes nothing.
  const admin = await seedAdminApi()
  try {
    const adminPreview = await admin.post("/api/disposal-requests/bulk-decision", { data: { mode: "preview", requestIds: [requestId] } })
    expect(adminPreview.status()).toBe(200)
    const adminItems = ((await adminPreview.json()) as { items: { outcome: string; code: string | null }[] }).items
    expect(adminItems).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ outcome: "blocked", code: "DISPOSAL_SOD_CONFLICT" })])
  } finally {
    await admin.dispose()
  }

  // 2. The IT head approves it alone. A rejection with no reason first: the error shows under the remark.
  await actAs(page, "itHead", e2eTheme)
  await measure.visit(requestUrl, "IT head: the pending request")
  await visibleButton(page, "พิจารณาคำขอ").click()
  const decision = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "พิจารณาคำขอตัดจำหน่าย" })
  await expect(decision).toBeVisible()
  await decision.locator("#disposal-decision-decision").selectOption("reject")
  await decision.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึกผล" }).click()
  await expect(decision.locator("#disposal-decision-remark-error")).toHaveText("กรุณาระบุเหตุผลที่ไม่อนุมัติ")
  await measure.recordPage("IT head: the rejection's missing reason under the field")
  await decision.locator("#disposal-decision-decision").selectOption("approve")
  await decision.locator("#disposal-decision-remark").fill(`${prefix} อนุมัติให้ตัดจำหน่าย`)
  await measure.timed("IT head: save the approval", async () => {
    await decision.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึกผล" }).click()
    await expect(decision).toHaveCount(0)
  })
  await expectToast(page, "บันทึกผลการพิจารณาสำเร็จ")
  await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText("อนุมัติแล้ว").first()).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText(employeeLabel("itHead").split(" - ")[0]).first()).toBeVisible()

  // The approver cannot execute: no execute on the page, and the execute API refuses them.
  await expect(visibleButton(page, "บันทึกตัดจำหน่ายจริง")).toHaveCount(0)
  const headExecute = await page.request.patch(`/api/disposal-requests/${requestId}`, {
    data: {
      action: "execute",
      disposalType: "dispose",
      executionDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
      executedById: readRunData().employees.itHead.id,
      nextStatusId: choices.statusId,
      recipientName: `${prefix} ผู้รับซาก`,
      documentNo: `${prefix}-DOC`,
      // The body the execution dialog sends: every key present (the schema's preprocessed numbers need them).
      actualSaleValue: null,
      actualSalvageValue: null,
      executionRemark: null,
      useHistoricalEvidenceException: false,
      evidenceExceptionReason: null,
      evidenceExceptionAcknowledged: false,
    },
  })
  const headExecuteBody = (await headExecute.json()) as { code?: string }
  expect(headExecute.status(), `the approver may not execute: ${JSON.stringify(headExecuteBody)}`).toBe(403)
  expect(headExecuteBody.code).toBe("DISPOSAL_SOD_CONFLICT")
  await measure.recordPage("IT head: the approved request")

  // 3. IT staff execute it. With no evidence yet the dialog says so and does not save.
  await actAs(page, "itStaff", e2eTheme)
  await measure.visit(requestUrl, "IT staff: the approved request")
  await visibleButton(page, "บันทึกตัดจำหน่ายจริง").click()
  let execution = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "บันทึกผลการตัดจำหน่ายจริง" })
  await expect(execution.getByRole("alert")).toHaveText("กรุณาแนบหลักฐานก่อนบันทึกการตัดจำหน่ายจริง")
  await expect(execution.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึกผล" })).toHaveAttribute("aria-disabled", "true")
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: execution waits for evidence")
  await execution.getByRole("button", { name: "ยกเลิก" }).click()
  await expect(execution).toHaveCount(0)

  // The evidence (a fixture PDF) on the request.
  await measure.timed("IT staff: upload the evidence", async () => {
    await page.locator('input[type="file"]').first().setInputFiles(EVIDENCE_PDF)
    await expectToast(page, "อัปโหลดหลักฐานตัดจำหน่ายสำเร็จ")
  })
  await expect(page.getByText("evidence.pdf")).toBeVisible()

  // The execution: no document number first, the error shows under it; then the whole entry saves.
  await visibleButton(page, "บันทึกตัดจำหน่ายจริง").click()
  execution = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "บันทึกผลการตัดจำหน่ายจริง" })
  await chooseSearchable(page, "disposal-execution-executed-by", employeeLabel("itStaff"))
  await execution.locator("#disposal-execution-recipient").fill(`${prefix} ผู้รับซาก`)
  await execution.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึกผล" }).click()
  await expect(execution.locator("#disposal-execution-document-no-error")).toHaveText("กรุณากรอกเลขที่เอกสารอ้างอิง")
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: the missing document number under the field")
  await execution.locator("#disposal-execution-document-no").fill(`${prefix}-DOC`)
  await measure.timed("IT staff: save the execution", async () => {
    await execution.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึกผล" }).click()
    await expect(execution).toHaveCount(0)
  })
  await expectToast(page, "บันทึกผลการตัดจำหน่ายจริงสำเร็จ")
  await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText("ตัดจำหน่ายแล้ว").first()).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText(`${prefix}-DOC`)).toBeVisible()
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: the disposed request")

  // The asset left the books: its status is Disposed.
  const assetRead = await page.request.get(`/api/assets/${asset.id}`)
  expect(assetRead.status()).toBe(200)
  expect(((await assetRead.json()) as { status?: { name?: string } }).status?.name).toBe("Disposed")

  // 4. The disposal print opens and shows the number. Its axe check is the next test (finding D1).
  await visibleLink(page, "พิมพ์เอกสารตัดจำหน่ายฉบับสมบูรณ์").first().click()
  await page.waitForURL(/\/print(?:\?|$)/)
  await expect(page.getByText("ใบอนุมัติตัดจำหน่ายทรัพย์สิน").first()).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText(disposalNo).first()).toBeVisible()
  await measure.screenshot("disposal-print")
  printPath = new URL(page.url()).pathname
})

// Finding D1: the shared print layout (src/components/asset-operations/operation-document-print.tsx) puts each field's
// dt and dd in a div grid with no dl, an axe serious violation (dlitem) on every print page. Expected to fail until it
// is fixed; Playwright then reports "expected to fail but passed".
test("the disposal print has no serious or critical axe violation (finding D1)", async ({ page, measure }) => {
  test.fail(true, "finding D1: dt and dd outside a dl on the print layout (axe dlitem, serious)")
  expect(printPath, "the workflow test opened the print").not.toBe("")
  await measure.visit(printPath, "IT staff: the disposal print")
  await expect(page.getByText("ใบอนุมัติตัดจำหน่ายทรัพย์สิน").first()).toBeVisible()
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** (exact):

  - Create `e2e/disposal/batch-bulk-approval.spec.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// Disposal of a batch of two (spec §6.4.1–3, §6.7): IT staff request both in one batch with shared evidence, the IT
// head approves them in one bulk approval, and IT staff execute them. On the way: the batch form's wrong entry first;
// the IT head's own request is refused approval by the same IT head (the requester cannot approve their own request);
// the approver's bulk execution is blocked (the approver cannot execute).
import { asUser, expect, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { readRunData } from "../support/state.ts"
import {
  actAs,
  chooseSearchable,
  createAssets,
  createRoom,
  employeeLabel,
  escapeRegExp,
  EVIDENCE_PDF,
  expectToast,
  openBulkExecutionPreview,
  readAssetChoices,
  recordPrefix,
  visibleButton,
  type DisposalAsset,
} from "./disposal-support.ts"

asUser("itStaff")
// One batch walks through four tests: the bulk approval, one execution on its page, the approver's blocked bulk
// execution (finding D3) and IT staff's bulk execution (finding D2). Each needs the one before (an expected failure
// lets the next run); an unexpected failure skips the rest.
test.describe.configure({ mode: "serial" })

let batchAssets: DisposalAsset[] = []
let requestPaths: Record<string, string> = {}
let queueSearch = ""

test("IT staff request two disposals in one batch with shared evidence, and the IT head approves them in one go but not their own", async ({ page, measure, e2eTheme, e2eDevice }, testInfo) => {
  const prefix = recordPrefix(testInfo, "DSP2")
  queueSearch = prefix
  const roomId = await createRoom(`${prefix}-R`, `${prefix} ห้องทดสอบตัดจำหน่าย`)
  const choices = await readAssetChoices(page, measure)
  const assets = await createAssets(page, choices, roomId, [`${prefix}-A1`, `${prefix}-A2`, `${prefix}-A3`])
  batchAssets = assets.slice(0, 2)
  const own = assets[2]

  // 1. IT staff make the batch: both assets, the shared evidence, and a reason. Review without a requester first: the
  // error shows under the requester and the review waits.
  await measure.visit("/th/disposal/batch/new", "IT staff: new disposal batch")
  const search = page.getByPlaceholder("ค้นหารหัสหรือชื่อทรัพย์สิน")
  await search.fill(`${prefix}-A`)
  for (const asset of batchAssets) await page.getByRole("option", { name: new RegExp(escapeRegExp(asset.assetTag)) }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText("เลือกแล้ว 2 รายการ")).toBeVisible()
  await page.locator("#disposal-batch-reason").fill(`${prefix} หมดอายุการใช้งาน เสนอตัดจำหน่ายทั้งชุด`)
  await page.locator('input[type="file"]').first().setInputFiles(EVIDENCE_PDF)
  await expect(page.getByText("evidence.pdf")).toBeVisible()
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "ตรวจสอบชุดคำขอ" }).click()
  await expect(page.locator("#disposal-batch-requested-by-error")).toHaveText("กรุณาระบุข้อมูลนี้")
  await expect(page.locator("#disposal-batch-requested-by")).toBeFocused()
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: the missing requester under the field")
  await chooseSearchable(page, "disposal-batch-requested-by", employeeLabel("itStaff"))
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "ตรวจสอบชุดคำขอ" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "ตรวจสอบก่อนสร้างชุดคำขอ" })).toBeVisible()
  for (const asset of batchAssets) await expect(page.getByText(`${asset.assetTag} - ${asset.name}`)).toBeVisible()
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: the batch review")
  await measure.timed("IT staff: create the batch", async () => {
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันสร้างชุดคำขอ" }).click()
    await page.waitForURL(/\/th\/disposal\/batches\/[0-9a-f-]{36}$/)
  })
  await expectToast(page, "สร้างชุดคำขอ 2 รายการสำเร็จ")
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(/^DPB-\d{8}-\d{4}$/)
  await expect(page.getByText("evidence.pdf")).toBeVisible()
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: the batch, two requests pending")
  // Each request's page, read off the batch's list.
  requestPaths = {}
  for (const asset of batchAssets) {
    const href = await page
      .getByRole("main")
      .locator(e2eDevice === "phone" ? "li" : "tr")
      .filter({ hasText: asset.assetTag })
      .locator("a[href*='/th/disposal/']")
      .first()
      .getAttribute("href")
    requestPaths[asset.assetTag] = new URL(href ?? "", page.url()).pathname
  }

  // 2. The IT head's own request (made through the API they would post it with), for the refusal below.
  await actAs(page, "itHead", e2eTheme)
  const ownRequest = await page.request.post("/api/disposal-requests", {
    data: {
      assetId: own.id,
      disposalType: "dispose",
      reason: `${prefix} คำขอของหัวหน้า IT เอง`,
      requestedById: readRunData().employees.itHead.id,
      approverId: null,
      saleValue: null,
      salvageValue: null,
      sourceType: null,
      sourceId: null,
    },
  })
  expect(ownRequest.status(), await ownRequest.text()).toBe(201)
  const ownRequestId = ((await ownRequest.json()) as { id: string }).id

  // 3. The IT head's pending queue: the batch's two are selectable, their own request is not, with the reason shown.
  await measure.visit(`/th/disposal?status=pending&search=${encodeURIComponent(prefix)}`, "IT head: pending disposals")
  if (e2eDevice === "phone") await page.getByRole("button", { name: "เลือกหลายรายการ" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole("note", { name: "ไม่สามารถอนุมัติได้เนื่องจากขัดกับการแยกหน้าที่" }).filter({ visible: true })).toHaveCount(1)
  for (const asset of batchAssets) {
    await page.getByRole("checkbox", { name: new RegExp(`ทรัพย์สิน ${escapeRegExp(asset.assetTag)}$`) }).filter({ visible: true }).check()
  }
  // The toolbar's count (its live region says the same to a screen reader).
  await expect(page.locator("div").filter({ hasText: /^เลือกแล้ว 2 รายการ$/ }).filter({ visible: true })).toHaveCount(1)
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "ตรวจสอบและอนุมัติ" }).click()
  const bulk = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ตรวจสอบก่อนอนุมัติแบบกลุ่ม" })
  await expect(bulk.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันอนุมัติ 2 รายการ" })).toBeVisible()
  await bulk.locator("#disposal-bulk-approval-remark").fill(`${prefix} อนุมัติทั้งชุด`)
  await measure.recordPage("IT head: the bulk approval's preview")
  await measure.timed("IT head: approve the two", async () => {
    await bulk.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันอนุมัติ 2 รายการ" }).click()
    await expect(page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ผลการอนุมัติแบบกลุ่ม" })).toBeVisible()
  })
  const result = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ผลการอนุมัติแบบกลุ่ม" })
  await expect(result.getByRole("heading", { name: "อนุมัติแล้ว (2)" })).toBeVisible()
  for (const asset of batchAssets) await expect(result.getByText(asset.assetTag)).toBeVisible()
  await measure.recordPage("IT head: the bulk approval's result")
  await result.getByRole("button", { name: "ปิด" }).first().click()
  await expect(result).toHaveCount(0)

  // Their own request stays pending: no review on its page, and the bulk preview blocks it for segregation of duties.
  await measure.visit(`/th/disposal/${ownRequestId}`, "IT head: their own request")
  await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText("รออนุมัติ").first()).toBeVisible()
  await expect(visibleButton(page, "พิจารณาคำขอ")).toHaveCount(0)
  const ownPreview = await page.request.post("/api/disposal-requests/bulk-decision", { data: { mode: "preview", requestIds: [ownRequestId] } })
  expect(ownPreview.status()).toBe(200)
  const ownItems = ((await ownPreview.json()) as { items: { outcome: string; code: string | null }[] }).items
  expect(ownItems).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ outcome: "blocked", code: "DISPOSAL_SOD_CONFLICT" })])

  // The batch's two are approved.
  await measure.visit(`/th/disposal?status=approved&search=${encodeURIComponent(prefix)}`, "IT head: approved disposals")
  for (const asset of batchAssets) await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText(asset.assetTag).filter({ visible: true }).first()).toBeVisible()
})

test("IT staff execute one batch request on its page with the batch's shared evidence", async ({ page, measure }) => {
  const [asset] = batchAssets
  const path = requestPaths[asset.assetTag]
  expect(path, "the batch test read the request's page").toBeTruthy()
  await measure.visit(path, "IT staff: an approved batch request")
  await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText("อนุมัติแล้ว").first()).toBeVisible()
  // The batch's evidence counts for the request: the execution has no evidence notice.
  await visibleButton(page, "บันทึกตัดจำหน่ายจริง").click()
  const execution = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "บันทึกผลการตัดจำหน่ายจริง" })
  await expect(execution.getByText("กรุณาแนบหลักฐานก่อนบันทึกการตัดจำหน่ายจริง")).toHaveCount(0)
  await chooseSearchable(page, "disposal-execution-executed-by", employeeLabel("itStaff"))
  await execution.locator("#disposal-execution-recipient").fill(`${queueSearch} ผู้รับซาก`)
  await execution.locator("#disposal-execution-document-no").fill(`${asset.assetTag}-DOC`)
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: the execution of a batch request")
  await measure.timed("IT staff: save the execution", async () => {
    await execution.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึกผล" }).click()
    await expect(execution).toHaveCount(0)
  })
  await expectToast(page, "บันทึกผลการตัดจำหน่ายจริงสำเร็จ")
  await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText("ตัดจำหน่ายแล้ว").first()).toBeVisible()
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: the executed batch request")
})

// Finding D3: a dialog body that scrolls with nothing focusable in it cannot be scrolled from the keyboard (axe
// scrollable-region-focusable, serious; AccessibleDialogBody, src/components/ui/accessible-dialog.tsx). The bulk
// execution preview with nothing to execute is one: here the approver's own preview, which segregation of duties blocks
// (the approver cannot execute, in bulk too). Expected to fail until fixed.
test("the approver's bulk execution preview is blocked with the reason, and its body scrolls from the keyboard (finding D3)", async ({ page, measure, e2eTheme }) => {
  test.fail(true, "finding D3: the dialog body scrolls with nothing focusable (axe scrollable-region-focusable, serious)")
  const asset = batchAssets[1]
  expect(asset, "the batch test made the request").toBeTruthy()
  await actAs(page, "itHead", e2eTheme)
  await measure.visit(`/th/disposal?status=approved&search=${encodeURIComponent(asset.assetTag)}`, "IT head: approved disposals")
  const dialog = await openBulkExecutionPreview(page, asset, employeeLabel("itStaff"), `${queueSearch} ผู้รับซาก`)
  await expect(dialog.getByText("ไม่สามารถดำเนินการได้เนื่องจากขัดกับการแยกหน้าที่").first()).toBeVisible()
  await expect(dialog.getByRole("alert")).toHaveText("ไม่มีคำขอตัดจำหน่ายที่อนุมัติแล้วซึ่งดำเนินการได้ด้วยข้อมูลร่วมนี้")
  await measure.recordPage("IT head: the bulk execution's preview, blocked")
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "ยกเลิก" }).click()
  await expect(dialog).toHaveCount(0)
})

// Finding D2: a bulk execution can never execute for IT staff. It takes each request's own document number
// (buildDisposalExecutionInput, src/lib/disposal-execution-service.ts), which only an execution writes, and its
// dialog has no document number field; so every row is blocked "กรุณาระบุเลขที่เอกสารอ้างอิงในคำขอตัดจำหน่ายนี้ก่อน
// ดำเนินการ" (DISPOSAL_DOCUMENT_REQUIRED) unless a system admin uses the historical exception. Expected to fail until fixed.
test("IT staff execute the other batch request in a bulk execution (finding D2)", async ({ page, measure }) => {
  test.fail(true, "finding D2: bulk execution blocks every request for its missing document number")
  const asset = batchAssets[1]
  expect(asset, "the batch test made the request").toBeTruthy()
  await measure.visit(`/th/disposal?status=approved&search=${encodeURIComponent(asset.assetTag)}`, "IT staff: approved disposals")
  const dialog = await openBulkExecutionPreview(page, asset, employeeLabel("itStaff"), `${queueSearch} ผู้รับซาก`)
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: the bulk execution's preview")
  await expect(dialog.locator("#disposal-bulk-execution-permanent")).toBeVisible()
  await dialog.locator("#disposal-bulk-execution-permanent").check()
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "ยืนยันดำเนินการ 1 รายการ" }).click()
  const result = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "ผลการดำเนินการตัดจำหน่ายแบบกลุ่ม" })
  await expect(result.getByText("ดำเนินการแล้ว", { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
  await expect(result.getByText(asset.assetTag).first()).toBeVisible()
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: the bulk execution's result")
  await result.getByRole("button", { name: "ปิด" }).first().click()
  await measure.visit(`/th/disposal?status=disposed&search=${encodeURIComponent(asset.assetTag)}`, "IT staff: disposed disposals")
  await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText(asset.assetTag).filter({ visible: true }).first()).toBeVisible()
})
```

  After Step 3, `node --test tests/e2e-disposal.test.ts` passes (2 tests).

- [ ] **Step 5: Verify.** `node --test tests/e2e-disposal.test.ts` (2 tests, 2 pass); `npm test` (+1; **2336 tests, 2335 pass, 0 fail, 1 skipped** (area rehearsal: 2320)); `npx tsc --noEmit` (clean); `npm run lint` (0 errors, 146 warnings). `node <tools>/eol.mjs e2e/disposal/batch-bulk-approval.spec.ts tests/e2e-disposal.test.ts` prints `0 201`, `0 83`.
```ts
// An asset inside an open audit round cannot be disposed, with the reason shown (spec §6.4.4, wiki rule 12). The test
// opens its own round on its own room (spec §11: no workflow depends on another's state); the IT head then cancels the
// round, and the same request saves.
import { asUser, expect, test } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import {
  actAs,
  chooseSearchable,
  createAssets,
  createRoom,
  employeeLabel,
  escapeRegExp,
  readAssetChoices,
  recordPrefix,
  expectToast,
} from "./disposal-support.ts"

asUser("itStaff")

test("IT staff cannot request disposal of an asset in an open audit round, and can once the round is cancelled", async ({ page, measure, e2eTheme }, testInfo) => {
  const prefix = recordPrefix(testInfo, "DSP3")
  const roomId = await createRoom(`${prefix}-R`, `${prefix} ห้องทดสอบตัดจำหน่าย`)
  const choices = await readAssetChoices(page, measure)
  const [asset] = await createAssets(page, choices, roomId, [`${prefix}-A1`])

  // IT staff open a round on the room (the round's own workflow is spec §6.2's; here it is the condition).
  const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)
  const roundResponse = await page.request.post("/api/audit-rounds", {
    data: {
      name: `${prefix} รอบตรวจนับห้องตัดจำหน่าย`,
      auditYear: new Date().getFullYear(),
      scopeCompanyId: null,
      scopeBranchId: null,
      scopeDepartmentId: null,
      scopeLocationId: roomId,
      scopeCategoryId: null,
      scopeCustodianId: null,
      scopeStatusId: null,
      scopeConditionId: null,
      includeClosedAssets: false,
      riskPreset: "all",
      sampleRate: 100,
      startDate: today,
      endDate: today,
      status: "open",
    },
  })
  expect(roundResponse.status(), await roundResponse.text()).toBe(201)
  const round = (await roundResponse.json()) as { id: string; auditNo: string; generatedItems: number }
  expect(round.generatedItems, "the round holds the room's one asset").toBe(1)

  // 1. The request form's asset search shows the asset, not choosable, with the reason.
  await measure.visit("/th/disposal/new", "IT staff: new disposal request")
  await page.locator("#disposal-request-asset").fill(asset.assetTag)
  const option = page.getByRole("option", { name: new RegExp(escapeRegExp(asset.assetTag)) })
  await expect(option).toBeDisabled()
  await expect(option).toContainText("อยู่ในรอบตรวจนับที่ยังไม่ปิด")
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: the asset in an open round, with its reason")

  // 2. The request opened for the asset (as the audit finding's "open a disposal request" link does) is refused on save,
  // with the reason, and the asset keeps its status.
  await measure.visit(`/th/disposal/new?assetId=${asset.id}`, "IT staff: the request opened for the asset")
  await expect(page.getByText(`${asset.assetTag} - ${asset.name}`)).toBeVisible()
  await chooseSearchable(page, "disposal-request-requested-by", employeeLabel("itStaff"))
  await page.locator("#disposal-request-reason").fill(`${prefix} ชำรุดเกินซ่อม เสนอตัดจำหน่าย`)
  measure.expectRefusal({ method: "POST", url: /\/api\/disposal-requests$/, status: 400, reason: "an asset in an open audit round cannot be disposed (DISPOSAL_ASSET_BLOCKED)" })
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
  await expectToast(page, "ทรัพย์สินยังมีงานค้างที่ต้องปิดก่อนสร้างคำขอตัดจำหน่าย")
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/th\/disposal\/new\?assetId=/)
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: the refusal's reason")
  const before = (await (await page.request.get(`/api/assets/${asset.id}`)).json()) as { status?: { name?: string } }
  expect(before.status?.name).toBe("Ready")

  // 3. The IT head cancels the round (closing it needs every item checked; cancelling is the IT head's other way out).
  await actAs(page, "itHead", e2eTheme)
  const cancel = await page.request.patch(`/api/audit-rounds/${round.id}`, { data: { action: "cancel", reason: `${prefix} ยกเลิกรอบเพื่อทดสอบการตัดจำหน่าย` } })
  expect(cancel.status(), await cancel.text()).toBe(200)

  // 4. The same request now saves.
  await actAs(page, "itStaff", e2eTheme)
  await measure.visit(`/th/disposal/new?assetId=${asset.id}`, "IT staff: the request after the round is cancelled")
  await chooseSearchable(page, "disposal-request-requested-by", employeeLabel("itStaff"))
  await page.locator("#disposal-request-reason").fill(`${prefix} ชำรุดเกินซ่อม เสนอตัดจำหน่าย`)
  await measure.timed("IT staff: save the request", async () => {
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
    await page.waitForURL(/\/th\/disposal\/[0-9a-f-]{36}$/)
  })
  await expectToast(page, "สร้างคำขอตัดจำหน่ายสำเร็จ")
  await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText("รออนุมัติ").first()).toBeVisible()
  await measure.recordPage("IT staff: the request, pending approval")
})
```

  After Step 3, `node --test tests/e2e-disposal.test.ts` passes (3 tests).

### What a run writes (one run of `e2e/disposal`, four projects; through the app only)

Per project: 3 rooms; 5 assets; 5 disposal requests (`DP-yyyyMMdd-NNNN`): ED1's one (disposed), ED2's batch of two (one disposed, one left approved while D2 stands) and the IT head's own (pending), ED3's one (pending); 1 batch (`DPB-…`); 2 evidence PDFs under `./uploads` (one on a request, one on the batch); 1 audit round (`AUD-yyyy-NNNN`) opened and cancelled. ED3's refused save writes nothing. So four projects: 12 rooms, 20 assets, 20 requests (8 disposed, 4 approved, 8 pending), 4 batches, 8 evidence files, 4 cancelled rounds; plus the setup's records (foundation). Nothing is deleted. A `SELECT` after the whole-suite run `E2E-1019T2357` counted exactly these (`<temp>/tools-disposal/select-run-writes.cjs E2E-1019T2357`): 12 rooms, 20 assets, 20 requests (8 disposed, 4 approved, 8 pending), 4 batches, 4 request and 4 batch evidence files, 4 rounds all cancelled, 8 assets Disposed.

- [ ] **Step 1: The failing test.** Create `tests/e2e-cross-cutting.test.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import test from "node:test"

// The E2E suite's cross-cutting workflows (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-e2e-workflow-suite-design.md §6.5
// reports and exports, §6.6 roles and permissions, §6.7 errors under the field; tasks EM1–EM3). One test per task,
// appended in order. The workflows run with `npm run test:e2e`; these pin the data they stand on against the code, so a
// route or a page that changes its permission, its field id or its sentence fails here first. The working tree is CRLF
// on Windows: every read strips \r.
const read = (file: string) => readFileSync(file, "utf8").replace(/\r/g, "")
const thai = JSON.parse(read("messages/th.json")) as Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>

// The body of one exported handler (export async function GET/POST/PUT/PATCH) of a route file.
function handlerBody(source: string, method: string): string {
  const start = source.indexOf(`export async function ${method}(`)
  assert.ok(start >= 0, `${method} handler`)
  const next = source.indexOf("\nexport ", start + 1)
  return source.slice(start, next < 0 ? undefined : next)
}

// `requirePermission(user, "asset", "edit")` for "asset:edit".
function requireCall(permission: string): string {
  const [moduleName, action] = permission.split(":")
  return `requirePermission(user, "${moduleName}", "${action}")`
}

test("reports and exports: each export's route permission, the reports page's gates and labels, the run's assets (e2e cross-cutting task 1, EM1)", async () => {
  const { EXPORTS, mayExport, permissionBadge, ALLOWED, NOT_ALLOWED } = await import("../e2e/cross-cutting/support/export-rights.ts")
  const { E2E_ROLE_PERMISSIONS } = await import("../e2e/support/run-plan.ts")
  const reportsPage = read("src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/reports/page.tsx")
  const labels = thai.reportsPage as Record<string, string>
  // Each export's route requires the permission export-rights.ts names, first thing in its GET.
  const routeFiles: Record<string, string> = {
    overview: "src/app/api/reports/assets-overview/export/route.ts",
    register: "src/app/api/assets/export/route.ts",
    maintenance: "src/app/api/maintenance-tickets/export/route.ts",
    audit: "src/app/api/audit-findings/export/route.ts",
    disposal: "src/app/api/disposal-requests/export/route.ts",
    role: "src/app/api/admin/roles/export/route.ts",
  }
  const gates: Record<string, string> = { overview: "canReportExport", register: "canAssetExport", maintenance: "canMaintenanceExport", audit: "canAuditExport", disposal: "canDisposalExport", role: "canRoleExport" }
  const labelKeys: Record<string, string> = { overview: "exportAssetOverview", register: "exportAssetRegister", maintenance: "exportMaintenance", audit: "exportAuditFindings", disposal: "exportDisposal", role: "exportRoleAudit" }
  assert.deepEqual(EXPORTS.map((plan) => plan.key), ["overview", "register", "maintenance", "audit", "disposal", "role"])
  for (const plan of EXPORTS) {
    const get = handlerBody(read(routeFiles[plan.key]), "GET")
    assert.ok(get.includes(requireCall(plan.permission)), `${plan.key}: ${plan.permission}`)
    assert.ok(get.indexOf(requireCall(plan.permission)) < get.indexOf("prisma."), `${plan.key}: the permission before any read`)
    assert.equal(routeFiles[plan.key], `src/app${plan.route}/route.ts`, `${plan.key}: its route`)
    const [moduleName, action] = plan.permission.split(":")
    assert.ok(reportsPage.includes(`const ${gates[plan.key]} = hasPermission(user, "${moduleName}", "${action}")`), `${plan.key}: the reports page's gate`)
    assert.equal(plan.label, labels[labelKeys[plan.key]], `${plan.key}: its label`)
  }
  assert.deepEqual([ALLOWED, NOT_ALLOWED], [labels.allowed, labels.notAllowed])
  // Spec §7's roles: IT staff and the IT head may use the overview and the register, the viewer the register only, and
  // none of them a module export (no <module>:export in spec §7; finding M1's test shows the audit pages offer it).
  assert.deepEqual(EXPORTS.filter((plan) => mayExport("itStaff", plan.key)).map((plan) => plan.key), ["overview", "register"])
  assert.deepEqual(EXPORTS.filter((plan) => mayExport("itHead", plan.key)).map((plan) => plan.key), ["overview", "register"])
  assert.deepEqual(EXPORTS.filter((plan) => mayExport("viewer", plan.key)).map((plan) => plan.key), ["register"])
  assert.ok(!E2E_ROLE_PERMISSIONS.itHead.some((permission) => permission.endsWith(":export") && permission !== "report:export"))
  assert.equal(permissionBadge("viewer", EXPORTS[0]), `${labels.exportAssetOverview}: ${labels.notAllowed}`)
  // The report views' tab names and the overview's export filenames the spec reads.
  assert.deepEqual([labels.viewAccounting, labels.viewOperations, labels.viewCatalog, labels.viewNavigation], ["บัญชี", "การปฏิบัติงาน", "คลังรายงาน", "มุมมองรายงาน"])
  assert.match(read("src/app/api/assets/export/route.ts"), /workbookResponse\(buffer, `assets-export-\$\{new Date\(\)\.toISOString\(\)\.slice\(0, 10\)\}\.xlsx`\)/)
  assert.match(read("src/app/api/reports/assets-overview/export/route.ts"), /\{ metric: "Total Assets", value: totalAssets \},\n\s+\{ metric: "Total Purchase Value", value: Number\(totalValue\._sum\.purchasePrice \?\? 0\) \},/)
  // The assets are IT staff's, through the asset API with every preprocessed optional key present (foundation N3).
  const records = read("e2e/cross-cutting/support/records.ts")
  for (const key of ["licenseTotalSeats: null", "licenseUsedSeats: null", "warrantyStartDate: null", "warrantyEndDate: null"]) assert.ok(records.includes(key), key)
  assert.ok(records.includes(`handoverMode: "permanent_assignment"`), "the handover mode the checkout schema takes")
  // The spec: every test imports the suite's fixtures; finding M1 and M2 stay expected to fail until fixed.
  const spec = read("e2e/cross-cutting/reports.spec.ts")
  assert.ok(spec.includes(`from "../support/fixtures.ts"`) && !spec.includes(`from "@playwright/test"`))
  assert.ok(spec.includes(`test.fail(true, "M1: `) && spec.includes(`test.fail(true, "M2: `))
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** (exact):

  - Create `e2e/cross-cutting/support/records.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// The records the cross-cutting workflows stand on (spec §6.5–§6.7): E2E assets that IT staff register through the
// app's own asset API with their session, in the run's company, branch, department and room, and a handover to an E2E
// employee through the checkout API. The asset form's own choices (category, status, condition) are read from the form
// IT staff open, so no lookup needs a permission they do not have. Every tag and name carries the run id and the
// project, so the four projects never share a record (spec §11 "Run order").
import type { APIRequestContext, Browser, Page, TestInfo } from "@playwright/test"
import type { Measure } from "../../support/measure.ts"
import { authStateFile, fromRoot } from "../../support/paths.ts"
import { readRunData, readRunState, type RunData } from "../../support/state.ts"

export type AssetChoices = { categoryId: string; readyStatusId: string; draftStatusId: string; goodConditionId: string }

export type E2eAsset = { id: string; assetTag: string; name: string }

export type AssetSpec = {
  // The tag's last part, unique in the test: RPT1, OWN, ...
  slug: string
  name: string
  ownershipType?: "personal" | "shared" | "stock"
  purchasePrice?: number
  // Ready unless the test needs a Draft asset (the audit round's scope, field-errors.spec.ts).
  status?: "ready" | "draft"
}

// The run's marker for this project: E2E-1019N-desktop-light. Every tag and name of a test starts with it.
export function projectMarker(testInfo: TestInfo): string {
  return `${readRunState().runId}-${testInfo.project.name}`
}

// The asset form's choices: its "Monitor" category (else its first), and the Ready and Draft statuses and the Good
// condition by their Thai labels (the dev data's nameTh). With a measure, the visit is measured like any page of the
// test.
export async function readAssetChoices(page: Page, measure?: Measure): Promise<AssetChoices> {
  if (measure) await measure.visit("/th/assets/new", "asset form (its choices)")
  else await page.goto("/th/assets/new")
  const options = async (id: string) =>
    page.locator(`#${id} option`).evaluateAll((nodes) => nodes.map((node) => ({ value: (node as HTMLOptionElement).value, label: (node.textContent ?? "").trim() })))
  const categories = (await options("asset-category")).filter((option) => option.value)
  const statuses = await options("asset-status")
  const conditions = await options("asset-condition")
  const category = categories.find((option) => option.label.startsWith("Monitor ")) ?? categories[0]
  const ready = statuses.find((option) => option.label === "พร้อมใช้งาน")
  const draft = statuses.find((option) => option.label === "ร่าง")
  const good = conditions.find((option) => option.label === "ดี")
  if (!category || !ready || !draft || !good) throw new Error("the asset form has no category, no Ready or Draft status, or no Good condition")
  return { categoryId: category.value, readyStatusId: ready.value, draftStatusId: draft.value, goodConditionId: good.value }
}

async function created<T>(request: APIRequestContext, url: string, data: unknown): Promise<T> {
  const response = await request.post(url, { data })
  if (response.status() !== 201 && response.status() !== 200) {
    throw new Error(`POST ${url} answered ${response.status()}: ${(await response.text()).slice(0, 300)}`)
  }
  return (await response.json()) as T
}

// One E2E asset, Ready (or Draft), in the run's room and department (a shared or stock asset needs the department).
export async function createAsset(request: APIRequestContext, testInfo: TestInfo, choices: AssetChoices, spec: AssetSpec, run: RunData = readRunData()): Promise<E2eAsset> {
  const assetTag = `${projectMarker(testInfo)}-${spec.slug}`
  const name = `${projectMarker(testInfo)} ${spec.name}`
  const asset = await created<{ id: string; assetTag: string; name: string }>(request, "/api/assets", {
    assetTag,
    name,
    categoryId: choices.categoryId,
    companyId: run.companyId,
    branchId: run.branchId,
    ownershipType: spec.ownershipType ?? "shared",
    departmentId: run.departmentId,
    homeLocationId: run.locationId,
    currentLocationId: run.locationId,
    statusId: spec.status === "draft" ? choices.draftStatusId : choices.readyStatusId,
    conditionId: choices.goodConditionId,
    purchaseDate: "2026-01-15",
    purchasePrice: spec.purchasePrice ?? 12500,
    // The schema's preprocessed optional fields take a missing value only when the key is there (the form sends
    // every key; foundation note N3).
    licenseTotalSeats: null,
    licenseUsedSeats: null,
    warrantyStartDate: null,
    warrantyEndDate: null,
    remark: `${readRunState().runId} cross-cutting`,
    isActive: true,
  })
  if (asset.assetTag !== assetTag) throw new Error(`the asset was saved with the tag ${asset.assetTag}, not ${assetTag}`)
  return { id: asset.id, assetTag: asset.assetTag, name: asset.name }
}

// A permanent handover to an employee through the checkout API: the asset becomes In Use with that custodian. It
// returns the handover (its id is the return form's checkoutId).
export async function handOver(request: APIRequestContext, asset: E2eAsset, custodianId: string, choices: AssetChoices): Promise<{ id: string }> {
  return created<{ id: string }>(request, `/api/assets/${asset.id}/checkout`, {
    handoverMode: "permanent_assignment",
    checkoutType: "user",
    custodianId,
    checkoutDate: new Date().toISOString(),
    expectedReturnDate: null,
    conditionBefore: choices.goodConditionId,
    remark: `${readRunState().runId} handover`,
  })
}

// The return of a handover through the checkin API, back to Ready in the run's room; it returns the return (its id is
// the RT- document's).
export async function returnAsset(request: APIRequestContext, asset: E2eAsset, checkoutId: string, choices: AssetChoices, run: RunData = readRunData()): Promise<{ id: string }> {
  return created<{ id: string }>(request, `/api/assets/${asset.id}/checkin`, {
    checkoutId,
    returnDate: new Date().toISOString(),
    returnBy: run.employees.receiver1.fullNameTh,
    returnByEmployeeId: run.employees.receiver1.id,
    receiveBy: run.employees.itStaff.fullNameTh,
    receiveByEmployeeId: run.employees.itStaff.id,
    conditionAfter: choices.goodConditionId,
    nextStatusId: choices.readyStatusId,
    nextLocationId: run.locationId,
    remark: `${readRunState().runId} return`,
  })
}

// The asset as the API reads it back (GET /api/assets/<id>): its status, custodian and room after a step.
export async function readAsset(request: APIRequestContext, assetId: string) {
  const response = await request.get(`/api/assets/${assetId}`)
  if (!response.ok()) throw new Error(`GET /api/assets/${assetId} answered ${response.status()}`)
  return (await response.json()) as { id: string; assetTag: string; custodianId: string | null; currentLocationId: string; status: { name: string; nameTh: string } }
}

export type ItStaffDesk ={ assets: E2eAsset[]; choices: AssetChoices; request: APIRequestContext; close: () => Promise<void> }

// IT staff register the assets another person's test looks at, in a browser context of their own (the asset form for
// its choices, then the asset API): the test's own page stays that person's. The caller closes it.
export async function registerAssetsAsItStaff(browser: Browser, testInfo: TestInfo, specs: readonly AssetSpec[]): Promise<ItStaffDesk> {
  const { baseURL, runId } = readRunState()
  const context = await browser.newContext({ baseURL, locale: "th-TH", timezoneId: "Asia/Bangkok", storageState: fromRoot(authStateFile(runId, "itStaff")) })
  const page = await context.newPage()
  const choices = await readAssetChoices(page)
  const assets: E2eAsset[] = []
  for (const spec of specs) assets.push(await createAsset(context.request, testInfo, choices, spec))
  return { assets, choices, request: context.request, close: () => context.close() }
}
```

  - Create `e2e/cross-cutting/support/files.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// The files a person downloads (spec §6.5 "downloads a file that opens"): the download a click starts, and the
// workbook or PDF it holds, opened the way a spreadsheet program or a PDF reader would start (ExcelJS reads the
// workbook; a PDF starts with %PDF- and ends with %%EOF).
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import type { Page } from "@playwright/test"
import ExcelJS from "exceljs"

export type Download = { file: string; filename: string; bytes: Buffer }

// Click the control and keep the file it downloads, under the test's own output folder.
export async function downloadBy(page: Page, click: () => Promise<void>, name: string, outputPath: (name: string) => string): Promise<Download> {
  const [download] = await Promise.all([page.waitForEvent("download"), click()])
  const failure = await download.failure()
  if (failure) throw new Error(`the download of ${name} failed: ${failure}`)
  const file = outputPath(`${name}-${download.suggestedFilename()}`)
  await download.saveAs(file)
  return { file, filename: download.suggestedFilename(), bytes: readFileSync(file) }
}

// Every sheet of the workbook as rows of plain cell values (row 1 is the header).
export async function openWorkbook(bytes: Buffer): Promise<Record<string, unknown[][]>> {
  const workbook = new ExcelJS.Workbook()
  await workbook.xlsx.load(bytes as unknown as ArrayBuffer)
  const sheets: Record<string, unknown[][]> = {}
  workbook.eachSheet((sheet) => {
    const rows: unknown[][] = []
    sheet.eachRow((row) => {
      rows.push((row.values as unknown[]).slice(1).map((value) => (value && typeof value === "object" && "result" in value ? (value as { result: unknown }).result : value)))
    })
    sheets[sheet.name] = rows
  })
  return sheets
}

// A PDF a reader opens: the header and the end-of-file marker.
export function isPdf(bytes: Buffer): boolean {
  return bytes.subarray(0, 5).toString("latin1") === "%PDF-" && bytes.subarray(-1024).toString("latin1").includes("%%EOF")
}
```

  - Create `e2e/cross-cutting/support/ui.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// What a person reads on screen in the cross-cutting workflows: a total in a stat strip, the access-denied and
// not-found screens, the searchable pickers, and an error under a field (the V track's contract: the control is
// marked invalid, its error line is <id>-error and is named by aria-describedby, and the first wrong field has focus
// after a save).
import type { Locator, Page } from "@playwright/test"
import { expect } from "../../support/fixtures.ts"
import type { Measure } from "../../support/measure.ts"

// The value of a stat strip cell, by its label ("ทรัพย์สินทั้งหมด" → "3").
export function statValue(page: Page, label: string, scope = "main") {
  return page
    .locator(`${scope} [data-slot="stat"]`)
    .filter({ has: page.locator("dt", { hasText: new RegExp(`^${label}$`) }) })
    .locator(".text-metric")
}

// requirePagePermission sends a person without the permission to the access-denied page (src/lib/access-denied.ts),
// which names the permission they need.
export async function expectAccessDenied(page: Page, permission: string) {
  const [moduleName, action] = permission.split(":")
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`/th/access-denied\\?(?=.*module=${moduleName})(?=.*action=${action})`))
  // The screen's title is plain text, not a heading (findings, note N13).
  await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText("ไม่มีสิทธิ์เข้าหน้านี้", { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText(`สิทธิ์ที่ต้องใช้: ${permission}`)).toBeVisible()
}

// Open a page the person may not use. The dashboard's loading.tsx streams every page, so requirePagePermission's
// redirect reaches the browser after the first load, as a client navigation: wait for the access-denied page and its
// load, then measure it (axe on the page the person is left on).
export async function visitRefused(page: Page, measure: Measure, path: string, permission: string, label: string) {
  await page.goto(path, { waitUntil: "load" })
  await expectAccessDenied(page, permission)
  await page.waitForLoadState("load")
  await measure.recordPage(label)
}

// notFound() under the streamed dashboard renders the not-found screen in place (the document answers 200).
export async function expectNotFound(page: Page) {
  await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText("ไม่พบหน้าที่ต้องการ", { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
}

// A SearchableSelect (src/components/ui/searchable-select.tsx): open it (its button, by id or locator), type the
// search, choose the first option that matches, and see it chosen.
export async function choose(page: Page, trigger: string | Locator, search: string, option: string | RegExp = search) {
  const button = typeof trigger === "string" ? page.locator(`#${trigger}`) : trigger
  await button.click()
  await page.getByRole("dialog").getByRole("combobox").fill(search)
  await page.getByRole("option", { name: option }).first().click()
  await expect(button).toContainText(search)
}

// The error under one field after a save: its text under the field, the control marked invalid (aria-invalid on an
// input, data-invalid on a SearchableSelect's button; a radio group's fieldset carries neither, FieldGroup) and naming
// the error, and focus on the first wrong field (the control itself, or for a group the element given).
export async function expectFieldError(page: Page, controlId: string, message: string, options: { focus?: string | false; group?: boolean } = {}) {
  const control = page.locator(`#${controlId}`)
  const error = page.locator(`#${controlId}-error`)
  await expect(error).toHaveText(message)
  await expect(error).toBeVisible()
  if (!options.group) await expect(page.locator(`#${controlId}:is([aria-invalid="true"], [data-invalid="true"])`)).toHaveCount(1)
  await expect(control).toHaveAttribute("aria-describedby", new RegExp(`(?:^|\\s)${controlId}-error(?:\\s|$)`))
  if (options.focus !== false) await expect(options.focus ? page.locator(options.focus) : control).toBeFocused()
}

// The error is gone as soon as the field changes (V track spec §4.3), before any new save.
export async function expectNoFieldError(page: Page, controlId: string) {
  await expect(page.locator(`#${controlId}-error`)).toHaveCount(0)
}
```

  - Create `e2e/cross-cutting/support/export-rights.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// Which exports each E2E user may use (spec §6.5 "a role without the export permission does not see it"), as plain
// data the unit test pins against the code: the permission each export route requires (requirePermission in its
// route) and the role sets of spec §7 (run-plan.ts). The reports page shows each export only with that permission
// (its canReportExport, canAssetExport, ... in src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/reports/page.tsx), and its catalog lists
// the same six in "สิทธิ์รายงานของคุณ" with ใช้ได้ or ไม่มีสิทธิ์.
import { E2E_ROLE_PERMISSIONS, type E2eUserKey } from "../../support/run-plan.ts"

export type ExportKey = "overview" | "register" | "maintenance" | "audit" | "disposal" | "role"

export type ExportPlan = {
  key: ExportKey
  // The permission its route requires.
  permission: string
  // Its label in the catalog's permission list and on its buttons (messages/th.json reportsPage).
  label: string
  // The route a person's click downloads from.
  route: string
}

export const EXPORTS: readonly ExportPlan[] = [
  { key: "overview", permission: "report:export", label: "ส่งออกรายงานภาพรวม", route: "/api/reports/assets-overview/export" },
  { key: "register", permission: "asset:view", label: "ส่งออกทะเบียนทรัพย์สิน", route: "/api/assets/export" },
  { key: "maintenance", permission: "maintenance:export", label: "ส่งออกใบซ่อม", route: "/api/maintenance-tickets/export" },
  { key: "audit", permission: "audit:export", label: "ส่งออกรายการไม่ตรง", route: "/api/audit-findings/export" },
  { key: "disposal", permission: "disposal:export", label: "ส่งออกตัดจำหน่าย", route: "/api/disposal-requests/export" },
  { key: "role", permission: "role:export", label: "ส่งออกผลตรวจสิทธิ์ตามบทบาท", route: "/api/admin/roles/export" },
]

// The catalog's two words (reportsPage.allowed, reportsPage.notAllowed).
export const ALLOWED = "ใช้ได้"
export const NOT_ALLOWED = "ไม่มีสิทธิ์"

export function mayExport(user: E2eUserKey, key: ExportKey): boolean {
  const plan = EXPORTS.find((candidate) => candidate.key === key)
  if (!plan) throw new Error(`no export ${key}`)
  return E2E_ROLE_PERMISSIONS[user].includes(plan.permission)
}

// The catalog's badge for one export: "ส่งออกรายงานภาพรวม: ใช้ได้".
export function permissionBadge(user: E2eUserKey, plan: ExportPlan): string {
  return `${plan.label}: ${mayExport(user, plan.key) ? ALLOWED : NOT_ALLOWED}`
}
```

```ts
// The reports page as a person reads it (src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/reports/page.tsx): a text on screen in the
// view's content, a view tab, and the catalog's list of the person's export rights.
import type { Page } from "@playwright/test"
import { expect } from "../../support/fixtures.ts"
import type { E2eUserKey } from "../../support/run-plan.ts"
import { EXPORTS, permissionBadge } from "./export-rights.ts"

// reportsPage.viewAccounting, viewOperations, viewCatalog (messages/th.json).
const VIEWS = { accounting: "บัญชี", operations: "การปฏิบัติงาน", catalog: "คลังรายงาน" } as const
export type ReportView = keyof typeof VIEWS

// A text the view shows on screen (the phone shows cards where the desktop shows a table: the visible one counts).
export function inReport(page: Page, text: string) {
  return page.locator("[data-report-view-content]").getByText(text, { exact: true }).filter({ visible: true }).first()
}

// A view tab is a link in the "มุมมองรายงาน" navigation (reportsPage.viewNavigation); the view loads on the client.
export async function openView(page: Page, view: ReportView) {
  await page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "มุมมองรายงาน" }).getByRole("link", { name: VIEWS[view], exact: true }).click()
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`[?&]view=${view}(?:&|$)`))
}

// The catalog's "สิทธิ์รายงานของคุณ" list (reportsPage.permissionTitle), opened: one badge per export, ใช้ได้ or
// ไม่มีสิทธิ์ for this user.
export async function expectCatalogRights(page: Page, user: E2eUserKey) {
  await page.locator("summary", { hasText: "สิทธิ์รายงานของคุณ" }).click()
  for (const plan of EXPORTS) await expect(page.getByText(permissionBadge(user, plan), { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
}
```

```ts
// Reports and exports (spec §6.5): each report view shows the run's E2E assets, each export the role may use
// downloads a file that opens, and a role without the export permission does not see it (nor may it download it).
// The users and their permissions are spec §7's (run-plan.ts); export-rights.ts says which export each may use.
import { expect, test, asUser } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { ADMIN_STATE_FILE, fromRoot } from "../support/paths.ts"
import { readRunState } from "../support/state.ts"
import { mayExport } from "./support/export-rights.ts"
import { downloadBy, isPdf, openWorkbook } from "./support/files.ts"
import { createAsset, projectMarker, readAssetChoices, registerAssetsAsItStaff } from "./support/records.ts"
import { expectCatalogRights, inReport, openView } from "./support/reports-ui.ts"
import { statValue } from "./support/ui.ts"

test.describe("IT staff", () => {
  asUser("itStaff")

  test("IT staff see the run's assets in every report view and download the register and the overview, which open", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
    const choices = await readAssetChoices(page, measure)
    const assets = []
    for (const [index, price] of [1000, 2000, 3000].entries()) {
      assets.push(await createAsset(page.request, testInfo, choices, { slug: `RPT${index + 1}`, name: `รายงาน ${index + 1}`, purchasePrice: price }))
    }
    const search = `${projectMarker(testInfo)}-RPT`

    // Overview: the totals, the preview of the three, and the run's department in the breakdown.
    await measure.visit(`/th/reports?search=${encodeURIComponent(search)}`, "reports: overview")
    await expect(statValue(page, "ทรัพย์สินทั้งหมด")).toHaveText("3")
    await expect(statValue(page, "มูลค่ารวม")).toHaveText("6,000.00")
    for (const asset of assets) await expect(inReport(page, asset.assetTag)).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.locator("[data-report-view-content]").getByText(new RegExp(`^${readRunState().runId}-D - `)).filter({ visible: true }).first()).toBeVisible()

    // The two exports of the header, each a file that opens with the three assets.
    const register = await downloadBy(page, () => page.getByRole("link", { name: "ส่งออกทะเบียนทรัพย์สิน" }).click(), "register", (name) => testInfo.outputPath(name))
    expect(register.filename).toMatch(/^assets-export-\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}\.xlsx$/)
    expect((await openWorkbook(register.bytes)).Assets.slice(1).map((row) => row[0]).sort()).toEqual(assets.map((asset) => asset.assetTag).sort())
    const overview = await downloadBy(page, () => page.getByRole("link", { name: "ส่งออกรายงานภาพรวม" }).click(), "overview", (name) => testInfo.outputPath(name))
    expect(overview.filename).toMatch(/^asset-overview-report-\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}\.xlsx$/)
    const overviewSheets = await openWorkbook(overview.bytes)
    expect(overviewSheets.Overview).toContainEqual(["Total Assets", 3])
    expect(overviewSheets.Overview).toContainEqual(["Total Purchase Value", 6000])

    // Accounting: the purchase cost of the three, each in the book-value list.
    await openView(page, "accounting")
    await measure.recordPage("reports: accounting")
    await expect(statValue(page, "ต้นทุนซื้อ", "[data-report-view-content]")).toHaveText("6,000.00")
    for (const asset of assets) await expect(inReport(page, `${asset.assetTag} - ${asset.name}`)).toBeVisible()

    // Operations: no serial number on any of the three, each in the list to fix.
    await openView(page, "operations")
    await measure.recordPage("reports: operations")
    await expect(statValue(page, "ไม่มีหมายเลขเครื่อง", "[data-report-view-content]")).toHaveText("3")
    for (const asset of assets) await expect(inReport(page, asset.assetTag)).toBeVisible()

    // The catalog: the exports IT staff may use, and the ones they may not.
    await openView(page, "catalog")
    await measure.recordPage("reports: catalog")
    await expectCatalogRights(page, "itStaff")
  })
})

test.describe("the viewer", () => {
  asUser("viewer")

  test("the viewer reads the reports and downloads the register, with no overview export", async ({ page, measure, browser }, testInfo) => {
    const desk = await registerAssetsAsItStaff(browser, testInfo, [
      { slug: "VWR1", name: "รายงานผู้ดู 1", purchasePrice: 4000 },
      { slug: "VWR2", name: "รายงานผู้ดู 2", purchasePrice: 5000 },

    await expect(statValue(page, "ทรัพย์สินทั้งหมด")).toHaveText("2")
    for (const asset of desk.assets) await expect(inReport(page, asset.assetTag)).toBeVisible()
    // The register export needs asset:view only; the overview export needs report:export, which the viewer lacks.
    expect(mayExport("viewer", "register") && !mayExport("viewer", "overview")).toBe(true)
    await expect(page.getByRole("link", { name: "ส่งออกรายงานภาพรวม" })).toHaveCount(0)
    const register = await downloadBy(page, () => page.getByRole("link", { name: "ส่งออกทะเบียนทรัพย์สิน" }).click(), "register", (name) => testInfo.outputPath(name))
    expect((await openWorkbook(register.bytes)).Assets.slice(1).map((row) => row[0]).sort()).toEqual(desk.assets.map((asset) => asset.assetTag).sort())
    // A direct request is refused too.
    expect((await page.request.get(`/api/reports/assets-overview/export?search=${encodeURIComponent(search)}`)).status()).toBe(403)

    await openView(page, "catalog")

  // Finding M2 (expected to fail until fixed): the import and export page shows the import template's download to a
  // role without asset:create, and the template route answers it 403.
  test("the import and export page offers no import template without asset:create (finding M2)", async ({ page, measure }) => {
    test.fail(true, "M2: /th/asset-management/import-export shows the import template link to a role without asset:create")
    await measure.visit("/th/asset-management/import-export", "asset import and export (viewer)")
    expect((await page.request.get("/api/assets/import-template")).status()).toBe(403)
    await expect(page.locator('main a[href^="/api/assets/import-template"]')).toHaveCount(0)
  })
})

test.describe("the IT head", () => {
  asUser("itHead")

  test("the IT head sees no export of a module whose export permission the role lacks, and the routes refuse it", async ({ page, measure }) => {
    // Spec §7 gives the IT head no maintenance:export, disposal:export or audit:export.
    for (const key of ["maintenance", "disposal", "audit"] as const) expect(mayExport("itHead", key), key).toBe(false)
    await measure.visit("/th/reports?view=catalog", "reports: catalog (IT head)")
    await expectCatalogRights(page, "itHead")
    await measure.visit("/th/maintenance", "maintenance list (IT head)")
    await expect(page.locator('main a[href^="/api/maintenance-tickets/export"]')).toHaveCount(0)
    await measure.visit("/th/disposal", "disposal list (IT head)")
    await expect(page.locator('main a[href^="/api/disposal-requests/export"]')).toHaveCount(0)
    for (const route of ["/api/maintenance-tickets/export", "/api/disposal-requests/export", "/api/audit-findings/export?status=all", "/api/audit-findings/export-pdf?status=all"]) {
      expect((await page.request.get(route)).status(), route).toBe(403)
    }
  })

  // Finding M1 (expected to fail until fixed): the audit findings page and an audit round's page show their exports to
  // a role without audit:export, and the export routes answer it 403.
  test("the audit findings and round pages offer no export without audit:export (finding M1)", async ({ page, measure }) => {
    test.fail(true, "M1: /th/audit/findings and /th/audit/rounds/<id> show the exports to a role without audit:export")
    const rounds = (await (await page.request.get("/api/audit-rounds")).json()) as { id: string; status: string }[]
    const round = rounds.find((candidate) => candidate.status !== "cancelled")
    if (!round) throw new Error("no audit round that is not cancelled")
    await measure.visit("/th/audit/findings", "audit findings (IT head)")
    await expect(page.locator('main a[href^="/api/audit-findings/export"]')).toHaveCount(0)
    await measure.visit(`/th/audit/rounds/${round.id}`, "audit round (IT head)")
    await expect(page.locator(`main a[href^="/api/audit-rounds/${round.id}/"]`)).toHaveCount(0)
  })
})

// The module exports no E2E role may use under spec §7 (repair, disposal, findings): the seed admin's session, read
// only, proves the files open. A GET of each, no page: it runs in one project, since no device or theme takes part.
test("the repair, disposal and findings exports download files that open (the seed admin's session)", async ({ playwright }, testInfo) => {
  test.skip(testInfo.project.name !== "desktop-light", "a GET of each file: no device or theme takes part")
  const admin = await playwright.request.newContext({ baseURL: readRunState().baseURL, storageState: fromRoot(ADMIN_STATE_FILE) })
  try {
    for (const [route, sheet] of [
      ["/api/maintenance-tickets/export", null],
      ["/api/disposal-requests/export", null],
      ["/api/audit-findings/export?status=all", "Audit Findings"],
    ] as const) {
      const response = await admin.get(route)
      expect(response.status(), route).toBe(200)
      expect(response.headers()["content-type"], route).toBe("application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet")
      const sheets = await openWorkbook(await response.body())
      const names = Object.keys(sheets)
      expect(names.length, route).toBeGreaterThan(0)
      if (sheet) expect(names, route).toContain(sheet)
      expect(sheets[names[0]][0]?.length ?? 0, `${route}: a header row`).toBeGreaterThan(1)
    }
    const pdf = await admin.get("/api/audit-findings/export-pdf?status=all")
    expect(pdf.status()).toBe(200)
    expect(isPdf(await pdf.body()), "the findings PDF opens").toBe(true)
  } finally {
    await admin.dispose()
  }
})
```
