```ts
// Roles and permissions (spec §6.6) as plain data the unit test pins against the code: the create, edit and approve
// pages and requests each E2E user is refused, the permission each needs (the page's requirePagePermission, the
// route's requirePermission), and the controls a read-only list must not offer. Every request carries an empty body
// and a record id that does not exist, so even a route that failed to check the permission could not change a record:
// each checks the permission before it reads the body or a record (the unit test pins that order).
import { E2E_ROLE_PERMISSIONS, type E2eUserKey } from "../../support/run-plan.ts"

// The id of no record: every route checks the permission before it looks the record up.
export const NO_RECORD = "e2e-no-such-record"

export type PageRefusal = {
  // The page a person opens; ":asset" is the test's own E2E asset, any other id is NO_RECORD.
  path: string
  // The page file under src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/.
  file: string
  // The permission requirePagePermission asks for, or "approve-inbox" for the approval inbox (a 404 without
  // disposal:approve or audit:approve, src/lib/approval-inbox-query.ts).
  permission: string
}

export const VIEWER_PAGE_REFUSALS: readonly PageRefusal[] = [
  { path: "/th/assets/new", file: "assets/new/page.tsx", permission: "asset:create" },
  { path: "/th/assets/:asset/edit", file: "assets/[id]/edit/page.tsx", permission: "asset:edit" },
  { path: "/th/asset-management/checkout", file: "asset-management/checkout/page.tsx", permission: "asset:edit" },
  { path: "/th/asset-management/checkin", file: "asset-management/checkin/page.tsx", permission: "asset:edit" },
  { path: "/th/asset-management/transfer", file: "asset-management/transfer/page.tsx", permission: "asset:edit" },
  { path: "/th/asset-management/bulk-move", file: "asset-management/bulk-move/page.tsx", permission: "asset:edit" },
  { path: "/th/audit/rounds/new", file: "audit/rounds/new/page.tsx", permission: "audit:create" },
  { path: `/th/audit/rounds/${NO_RECORD}/scan`, file: "audit/rounds/[id]/scan/page.tsx", permission: "audit:edit" },
  { path: "/th/maintenance/new", file: "maintenance/new/page.tsx", permission: "maintenance:create" },
  { path: "/th/maintenance/pm/new", file: "maintenance/pm/new/page.tsx", permission: "maintenance:create" },
  { path: `/th/maintenance/pm/${NO_RECORD}/edit`, file: "maintenance/pm/[id]/edit/page.tsx", permission: "maintenance:edit" },
  { path: "/th/disposal/new", file: "disposal/new/page.tsx", permission: "disposal:create" },
  { path: "/th/disposal/batch/new", file: "disposal/batch/new/page.tsx", permission: "disposal:create" },
  { path: "/th/admin/approvals", file: "admin/approvals/page.tsx", permission: "approve-inbox" },
]

export type ApiRefusal = {
  method: "POST" | "PUT" | "PATCH"
  // The request's path, with NO_RECORD for a record id.
  path: string
  // The route file under src/app/api/ and its handler.
  file: string
  permission: string
  // The body, empty unless the route picks its permission from it (the disposal PATCH: "decision" is approve).
  body: Record<string, unknown>
}

export const VIEWER_API_REFUSALS: readonly ApiRefusal[] = [
  { method: "POST", path: "/api/assets", file: "assets/route.ts", permission: "asset:create", body: {} },
  { method: "PUT", path: `/api/assets/${NO_RECORD}`, file: "assets/[id]/route.ts", permission: "asset:edit", body: {} },
  { method: "POST", path: `/api/assets/${NO_RECORD}/checkout`, file: "assets/[id]/checkout/route.ts", permission: "asset:edit", body: {} },
  { method: "POST", path: `/api/assets/${NO_RECORD}/checkin`, file: "assets/[id]/checkin/route.ts", permission: "asset:edit", body: {} },
  { method: "POST", path: `/api/assets/${NO_RECORD}/transfer`, file: "assets/[id]/transfer/route.ts", permission: "asset:edit", body: {} },
  { method: "POST", path: "/api/assets/bulk-move", file: "assets/bulk-move/route.ts", permission: "asset:edit", body: {} },
  { method: "POST", path: "/api/audit-rounds", file: "audit-rounds/route.ts", permission: "audit:create", body: {} },
  { method: "POST", path: `/api/audit-rounds/${NO_RECORD}/scan`, file: "audit-rounds/[id]/scan/route.ts", permission: "audit:edit", body: {} },
  { method: "POST", path: "/api/maintenance-tickets", file: "maintenance-tickets/route.ts", permission: "maintenance:create", body: {} },
  { method: "PATCH", path: `/api/maintenance-tickets/${NO_RECORD}`, file: "maintenance-tickets/[id]/route.ts", permission: "maintenance:edit", body: {} },
  { method: "POST", path: "/api/maintenance-plans", file: "maintenance-plans/route.ts", permission: "maintenance:create", body: {} },
  { method: "PATCH", path: `/api/maintenance-plans/${NO_RECORD}`, file: "maintenance-plans/[id]/route.ts", permission: "maintenance:edit", body: {} },
  { method: "POST", path: "/api/disposal-requests", file: "disposal-requests/route.ts", permission: "disposal:create", body: {} },
  { method: "POST", path: "/api/disposal-batches", file: "disposal-batches/route.ts", permission: "disposal:create", body: {} },
  { method: "POST", path: "/api/disposal-requests/bulk-execution", file: "disposal-requests/bulk-execution/route.ts", permission: "disposal:edit", body: {} },
]

// Spec §6.6 "IT staff are refused the approve and review actions": the review of a finding (approve, reject, plan and
// close all go through it), the round's close, a disposal decision and the bulk approval. The viewer is refused
// them too.
export const APPROVE_API_REFUSALS: readonly ApiRefusal[] = [
  { method: "POST", path: `/api/audit-findings/${NO_RECORD}/review`, file: "audit-findings/[id]/review/route.ts", permission: "audit:approve", body: {} },
  { method: "PATCH", path: `/api/audit-rounds/${NO_RECORD}`, file: "audit-rounds/[id]/route.ts", permission: "audit:approve", body: {} },
  { method: "PATCH", path: `/api/disposal-requests/${NO_RECORD}`, file: "disposal-requests/[id]/route.ts", permission: "disposal:approve", body: { action: "decision" } },
  { method: "POST", path: "/api/disposal-requests/bulk-decision", file: "disposal-requests/bulk-decision/route.ts", permission: "disposal:approve", body: {} },
]

// The pages a view-only person reads (spec §6.6 "sees read-only lists"): the asset register, the run's asset, the
// audit rounds and findings, repair records, PM plans, disposal requests and batches.
export const READ_ONLY_LISTS: readonly string[] = [
  "/th/assets",
  "/th/assets/:asset",
  "/th/audit/rounds",
  "/th/audit/findings",
  "/th/maintenance",
  "/th/maintenance/pm",
  "/th/disposal",
  "/th/disposal/batches",
]

// A link to one of these pages, or a control with one of these names, is an action the viewer cannot take.
export const WRITE_LINK_PATTERNS: readonly RegExp[] = [
  /^\/th\/assets\/new(?:[?#]|$)/,
  /^\/th\/assets\/[^/?#]+\/edit(?:[?#]|$)/,
  /^\/th\/asset-management\/(?:checkout|checkin|transfer|bulk-move)(?:[?#]|$)/,
  /^\/th\/audit\/rounds\/new(?:[?#]|$)/,
  /^\/th\/audit\/rounds\/[^/?#]+\/scan(?:[?#]|$)/,
  /^\/th\/maintenance\/new(?:[?#]|$)/,
  /^\/th\/maintenance\/pm\/new(?:[?#]|$)/,
  /^\/th\/maintenance\/pm\/[^/?#]+\/edit(?:[?#]|$)/,
  /^\/th\/disposal\/(?:new|batch\/new)(?:[?#]|$)/,
  /^\/th\/admin\/approvals(?:[?#]|$)/,
]
// messages/th.json: asset.openImportWizard, auditFinding.approve / reject / actionPlan / closeFinding,
// disposalPage.reviewRequest, and the PM plan's state buttons (maintenancePage).
export const WRITE_BUTTON_NAMES: readonly string[] = ["เปิดตัวช่วยนำเข้า", "อนุมัติ", "ปฏิเสธ", "แผนแก้ไข", "ปิดรายการไม่ตรง", "พิจารณาคำขอ", "พักแผน", "จบแผน", "บันทึกตัดจำหน่ายจริง"]
// The four controls of a finding's review, which all need audit:approve.
export const REVIEW_BUTTON_NAMES: readonly string[] = ["อนุมัติ", "ปฏิเสธ", "แผนแก้ไข", "ปิดรายการไม่ตรง"]

export function lacks(user: E2eUserKey, permission: string): boolean {
  if (permission === "approve-inbox") return !E2E_ROLE_PERMISSIONS[user].some((held) => held === "disposal:approve" || held === "audit:approve")
  return !E2E_ROLE_PERMISSIONS[user].includes(permission)
}

export function isWriteLink(href: string): boolean {
  return WRITE_LINK_PATTERNS.some((pattern) => pattern.test(href))
}
```

  - Create `e2e/cross-cutting/support/roles-ui.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// Roles and permissions in the browser (spec §6.6): the requests refused, the approval inbox not found, and the
// controls a read-only list must not offer. The pages and requests themselves are refusals.ts's.
import type { APIRequestContext, Page, TestInfo } from "@playwright/test"
import { expect } from "../../support/fixtures.ts"
import type { Measure } from "../../support/measure.ts"
import { projectMarker } from "./records.ts"
import { isWriteLink, WRITE_BUTTON_NAMES, type ApiRefusal } from "./refusals.ts"
import { expectNotFound } from "./ui.ts"

// Each request answers 403, listed together so one run shows every answer.
export async function expectRefused(request: APIRequestContext, refusals: readonly ApiRefusal[]) {
  const answers: string[] = []
  for (const refusal of refusals) {
    const response = await request.fetch(refusal.path, { method: refusal.method, data: refusal.body })
    answers.push(`${refusal.method} ${refusal.path} ${response.status()}`)
  }
  expect(answers).toEqual(refusals.map((refusal) => `${refusal.method} ${refusal.path} 403`))
}

// The approval inbox is not found for a person who may approve nothing (src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/admin/approvals).
export async function expectApprovalInboxNotFound(page: Page, measure: Measure, label: string) {
  await measure.visit("/th/admin/approvals", `approval inbox (${label})`)
  await expectNotFound(page)
}

// The controls on screen that lead to an action the person cannot take: links to a create, edit or approve page, and
// buttons named for a write.
export async function writeControls(page: Page): Promise<string[]> {
  const hrefs = await page.locator("main a[href]").filter({ visible: true }).evaluateAll((links) => links.map((link) => link.getAttribute("href") ?? ""))
  const buttons = await page.locator("main button").filter({ visible: true }).evaluateAll((nodes) => nodes.map((node) => (node.textContent ?? "").trim()))
  return [...new Set([...hrefs.filter(isWriteLink).map((href) => `link ${href.split("?")[0]}`), ...buttons.filter((name) => WRITE_BUTTON_NAMES.includes(name)).map((name) => `button ${name}`)])]
}

// A read-only list's address for the test: the register filtered to the test's own asset (the dev database's first
// page holds other runs' assets, whose photos may sit in another checkout's upload folder: findings, note N10), and
// ":asset" the test's own asset.
export function readOnlyUrl(path: string, asset: { id: string }, slug: string, testInfo: TestInfo): string {
  if (path === "/th/assets") return `/th/assets?search=${encodeURIComponent(`${projectMarker(testInfo)}-${slug}`)}`
  return path.replace(":asset", asset.id)
}
```

  - Create `e2e/cross-cutting/roles.spec.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// Roles and permissions (spec §6.6): the view-only user is refused every create, edit and approve page and request
// and reads lists that offer no such action; IT staff are refused the approve and review actions; the employee lands
// on My Assets and sees only their own assets. refusals.ts holds the pages and requests (pinned by the unit test).
import { expect, test, asUser } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { readRunData, readRunState } from "../support/state.ts"
import { createAsset, handOver, projectMarker, readAssetChoices, registerAssetsAsItStaff } from "./support/records.ts"
import { APPROVE_API_REFUSALS, lacks, READ_ONLY_LISTS, REVIEW_BUTTON_NAMES, VIEWER_API_REFUSALS, VIEWER_PAGE_REFUSALS } from "./support/refusals.ts"
import { expectApprovalInboxNotFound, expectRefused, readOnlyUrl, writeControls } from "./support/roles-ui.ts"
import { expectNotFound, visitRefused } from "./support/ui.ts"

test.describe("the viewer", () => {
  asUser("viewer")

  test("the viewer is refused every create, edit and approve page", async ({ page, measure, browser }, testInfo) => {
    const desk = await registerAssetsAsItStaff(browser, testInfo, [{ slug: "ROLE1", name: "สิทธิ์ผู้ดู" }])
    await desk.close()
    for (const refusal of VIEWER_PAGE_REFUSALS) {
      expect(lacks("viewer", refusal.permission), refusal.path).toBe(true)
      if (refusal.permission === "approve-inbox") {
        await expectApprovalInboxNotFound(page, measure, "the viewer")
        continue
      }
      await visitRefused(page, measure, refusal.path.replace(":asset", desk.assets[0].id), refusal.permission, `refused: ${refusal.path}`)
    }
  })

  test("the viewer's create, edit and approve requests are refused with 403", async ({ page }) => {
    for (const refusal of [...VIEWER_API_REFUSALS, ...APPROVE_API_REFUSALS]) expect(lacks("viewer", refusal.permission), refusal.path).toBe(true)
    await expectRefused(page.request, [...VIEWER_API_REFUSALS, ...APPROVE_API_REFUSALS])
  })

  // Finding M5 (expected to fail until fixed): the asset register, an asset's page and the audit rounds offer the
  // viewer create, edit and scan links (and the import helper) that lead to the access-denied page.
  test("the viewer's lists offer no create, edit or approve control (finding M5)", async ({ page, measure, browser }, testInfo) => {
    test.fail(true, "M5: the register's create and import helper, an asset's data-health edit links and the rounds' create and scan links show to a view-only role")
    const desk = await registerAssetsAsItStaff(browser, testInfo, [{ slug: "ROLE2", name: "รายการอ่านอย่างเดียว" }])
    await desk.close()
    const offered: string[] = []
    for (const path of READ_ONLY_LISTS) {
      const url = readOnlyUrl(path, desk.assets[0], "ROLE2", testInfo)
      // The asset's page is measured by the finding M6 test (its QR code fails axe); here only its controls count.
      if (path === "/th/assets/:asset") await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: "load" })
      else await measure.visit(url, `read-only: ${path}`)
      for (const control of await writeControls(page)) offered.push(`${path}: ${control}`)
    }
    expect(offered).toEqual([])
  })

  // Finding M6 (expected to fail until fixed): an asset's page draws its QR code as <svg role="img"> with no name
  // (qrcode.react without a title), an axe serious violation (svg-img-alt, WCAG 1.1.1) for every person who opens it.
  test("an asset's page passes axe: its QR code has a name (finding M6)", async ({ page, measure, browser }, testInfo) => {
    test.fail(true, "M6: the asset page's QR code (src/components/assets/asset-qr-code.tsx) is an <svg role=img> without a name")
    const desk = await registerAssetsAsItStaff(browser, testInfo, [{ slug: "ROLE4", name: "หน้าทรัพย์สิน" }])
    await desk.close()
    await measure.visit(`/th/assets/${desk.assets[0].id}`, "asset page (viewer)")
    await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText(desk.assets[0].assetTag).first()).toBeVisible()
  })
})

test.describe("IT staff", () => {
  asUser("itStaff")

  test("IT staff are refused the approve and review actions, and their own request shows no decision", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
    for (const refusal of APPROVE_API_REFUSALS) expect(lacks("itStaff", refusal.permission), refusal.path).toBe(true)
    await expectRefused(page.request, APPROVE_API_REFUSALS)
    await expectApprovalInboxNotFound(page, measure, "IT staff")

    // Their own disposal request: they may ask, not decide.
    const choices = await readAssetChoices(page, measure)
    const asset = await createAsset(page.request, testInfo, choices, { slug: "ROLE3", name: "คำขอของเจ้าหน้าที่" })
    const run = readRunData()
    const response = await page.request.post("/api/disposal-requests", {
      data: {
        assetId: asset.id,
        disposalType: "dispose",
        reason: `${projectMarker(testInfo)} ขอตัดจำหน่ายเพื่อทดสอบสิทธิ์`,
        requestedById: run.employees.itStaff.id,
        approverId: null,
        saleValue: null,
        salvageValue: null,
        sourceType: null,
        sourceId: null,
      },
    })
    expect(response.status(), await response.text()).toBe(201)
    const request = (await response.json()) as { id: string }
    await measure.visit(`/th/disposal/${request.id}`, "own disposal request (IT staff)")
    await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText(asset.assetTag).first()).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "พิจารณาคำขอ" })).toHaveCount(0)
  })

  // Finding M4 (expected to fail until fixed): the findings page shows IT staff the approve, reject, plan and close
  // controls of each pending finding (canEdit || canApprove), and the review route answers all four 403.
  test("the findings page offers IT staff no review control without audit:approve (finding M4)", async ({ page, measure }) => {
    await measure.visit("/th/audit/findings", "audit findings (IT staff)")
    // The queue's empty state (auditFinding.emptyTitle) shows when there is no pending finding to look at.
    const empty = await page.getByRole("main").getByText("ไม่พบรายการไม่ตรงตามตัวกรอง", { exact: true }).filter({ visible: true }).count()
    test.skip(empty > 0, "no pending finding in the dev database to look at")
    test.fail(true, "M4: /th/audit/findings shows the review controls to a role with audit:edit and no audit:approve")
    for (const name of REVIEW_BUTTON_NAMES) await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByRole("button", { name, exact: true }).filter({ visible: true })).toHaveCount(0)
  })
})

test.describe("the employee", () => {
  asUser("employee")

  test("the employee lands on My Assets and sees only their own assets", async ({ page, measure, browser }, testInfo) => {
    const run = readRunData()
    const desk = await registerAssetsAsItStaff(browser, testInfo, [
      { slug: "OWN", name: "ของพนักงาน", ownershipType: "personal" },
      { slug: "OTHER", name: "ของผู้รับมอบ", ownershipType: "personal" },
    ])
    const [own, other] = desk.assets
    await handOver(desk.request, own, run.employees.employee.id, desk.choices)
    await handOver(desk.request, other, run.employees.receiver1.id, desk.choices)
    await desk.close()

    await measure.visit("/th", "employee landing")
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/th\/my-assets(?:[?#]|$)/)
    const main = page.getByRole("main")
    await expect(main.getByText(own.assetTag, { exact: true }).filter({ visible: true }).first()).toBeVisible()
    await expect(main.getByText(other.assetTag, { exact: true })).toHaveCount(0)
    // Every tag listed is one of the run's handovers to this employee (one per project): a tag names its project.
    const tags = await main.getByText(new RegExp(`^${readRunState().runId}-(?:desktop|phone)-\\S+$`)).filter({ visible: true }).allTextContents()
    expect(tags.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    for (const tag of tags) expect(tag.trim()).toMatch(/-OWN$/)

    await measure.visit(`/th/my-assets/${own.id}`, "my asset detail")
    await expect(main.getByText(own.assetTag).first()).toBeVisible()
    await measure.visit(`/th/my-assets/${other.id}`, "another person's asset (employee)")
    await expectNotFound(page)
    // The register and the reports are not the employee's.
    await visitRefused(page, measure, `/th/assets/${own.id}`, "asset:view", "asset page (employee)")
    await visitRefused(page, measure, "/th/reports", "report:view", "reports (employee)")
    expect((await page.request.get(`/api/assets/${own.id}`)).status()).toBe(403)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** (exact):

  - Create `e2e/cross-cutting/support/forms.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// The workflow forms as IT staff fill them (spec §6.7): their save button, a date a few days from today, and the return
// form filled correctly after a handover to the run's first receiver.
import type { Page } from "@playwright/test"
import { readRunData } from "../../support/state.ts"
import type { AssetChoices } from "./records.ts"
import { choose } from "./ui.ts"

// Every workflow form's submit button is common.save.
export function save(page: Page) {
  return page.getByRole("button", { name: "บันทึก", exact: true }).click()
}

// A date input's value, days from today on this machine (Asia/Bangkok, like the browser's timezoneId).
export function isoDay(offset: number): string {
  const day = new Date()
  day.setDate(day.getDate() + offset)
  return `${day.getFullYear()}-${String(day.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(day.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`
}

// The return form (src/components/asset-operations/checkin-form.tsx) from its first person on: the receiver returns it,
// IT staff receive it, Good, back to Ready in the run's room.
export async function fillReturn(page: Page, choices: AssetChoices) {
  const run = readRunData()
  await choose(page, "checkin-return-by", run.employees.receiver1.code)
  await choose(page, "checkin-receive-by", run.employees.itStaff.code)
  await page.locator("#checkin-condition-after").selectOption(choices.goodConditionId)
  await page.locator("#checkin-next-status").selectOption({ label: "พร้อมใช้งาน" })
  await page.locator("#checkin-next-location").selectOption(run.locationId)
}
```

  - Create `e2e/cross-cutting/field-errors.spec.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
// Errors under the field in the real flow (spec §6.7, the V track): in each workflow's form, IT staff make one wrong
// entry first (an empty required field, or a date pair reversed), the error shows under that field with focus on it,
// and the corrected entry then saves for real. One test per form; each registers its own E2E asset.
import { expect, test, asUser } from "../support/fixtures.ts"
import { readRunData } from "../support/state.ts"
import { fillReturn, isoDay, save } from "./support/forms.ts"
import { createAsset, handOver, projectMarker, readAsset, readAssetChoices, returnAsset } from "./support/records.ts"
import { choose, expectFieldError, expectNoFieldError } from "./support/ui.ts"

asUser("itStaff")

// common.validation and the forms' own sentences (messages/th.json; the unit test pins them).
const REQUIRED = "กรุณาระบุข้อมูลนี้"
const END_BEFORE_START = "วันสิ้นสุดต้องไม่ก่อนวันเริ่มต้น"
const HANDOVER_MODE_REQUIRED = "กรุณาเลือกลักษณะการส่งมอบก่อนดำเนินการต่อ"
const DUPLICATE_TAG = "รหัสทรัพย์สินนี้ถูกใช้งานแล้ว"

test("asset register: a warranty that ends before it starts shows its error, and the corrected asset saves", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
  const run = readRunData()
  const choices = await readAssetChoices(page, measure)
  const tag = `${projectMarker(testInfo)}-FE-ASSET`
  // Opened from the register filtered to this tag, so the save returns there (the unfiltered register's first page
  // holds other runs' assets, whose photos may sit in another checkout's upload folder: findings, note N10).
  const register = `/th/assets?search=${encodeURIComponent(tag)}`
  await measure.visit(`/th/assets/new?returnTo=${encodeURIComponent(register)}`, "asset form")
  await page.locator("#asset-tag").fill(tag)
  await page.locator("#asset-name").fill(`${projectMarker(testInfo)} ฟอร์มทะเบียน`)
  await page.locator("#asset-category").selectOption(choices.categoryId)
  await page.locator("#asset-ownership-type").selectOption("shared")
  await page.locator("#asset-company").selectOption(run.companyId)
  await page.locator("#asset-branch").selectOption(run.branchId)
  await page.locator("#asset-department").selectOption(run.departmentId)
  await page.locator("#asset-current-location").selectOption(run.locationId)
  await page.locator("#asset-status").selectOption(choices.readyStatusId)
  await page.locator("#asset-condition").selectOption(choices.goodConditionId)
  await page.locator("#asset-warranty-start").fill("2026-02-01")
  await page.locator("#asset-warranty-end").fill("2026-01-01")
  await save(page)
  await expectFieldError(page, "asset-warranty-end", END_BEFORE_START)

  await page.locator("#asset-warranty-end").fill("2027-01-31")
  await expectNoFieldError(page, "asset-warranty-end")
  await measure.timed("asset save", async () => {
    await save(page)
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`/th/assets\\?search=${encodeURIComponent(tag)}`))
  })
  await expect(page.getByRole("main").getByText(tag, { exact: true }).filter({ visible: true }).first()).toBeVisible()
  await measure.recordPage("register after the asset save")
})

// Finding M3 (expected to fail until fixed): two saves of one typed tag at once (the form's duplicate check sees
// neither) reach the database's unique constraint, and the asset route answers 500 "Unexpected error" instead of the
// tag's field error.
test("a typed tag that already exists is refused with the tag's field error, not a 500 (finding M3)", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
  test.fail(true, "M3: POST /api/assets with a tag that exists answers 500 (P2002), not 400 fieldErrors.assetTag")
  const choices = await readAssetChoices(page, measure)
  const existing = await createAsset(page.request, testInfo, choices, { slug: "FE-RACE", name: "รหัสซ้ำพร้อมกัน" })
  const again = await page.request.post("/api/assets", {
    data: {
      assetTag: existing.assetTag,
      name: `${projectMarker(testInfo)} รหัสซ้ำพร้อมกัน 2`,
      categoryId: choices.categoryId,
      companyId: readRunData().companyId,
      branchId: readRunData().branchId,
      ownershipType: "shared",
      departmentId: readRunData().departmentId,
      currentLocationId: readRunData().locationId,
      statusId: choices.readyStatusId,
      conditionId: choices.goodConditionId,
      licenseTotalSeats: null,
      licenseUsedSeats: null,
      warrantyStartDate: null,
      warrantyEndDate: null,
    },
  })
  expect(again.status()).toBe(400)
  expect(((await again.json()) as { fieldErrors?: Record<string, string> }).fieldErrors?.assetTag).toBe("duplicate")
})

test("handover: no handover mode shows its error, and the corrected handover saves", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {

  await measure.visit(`/th/asset-management/checkout?assetId=${asset.id}`, "handover form")
  await expect(page.locator("#checkout-asset")).toContainText(asset.assetTag)
  await choose(page, "checkout-destination", run.employees.receiver2.code)
  await page.locator("#checkout-condition").selectOption(choices.goodConditionId)
  await save(page)
  await expectFieldError(page, "checkout-handover-mode", HANDOVER_MODE_REQUIRED, { group: true, focus: 'input[name="handoverModeChoice"][value="permanent_assignment"]' })

  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: /^มอบหมายใช้งานประจำ/ }).check()
  await expectNoFieldError(page, "checkout-handover-mode")

  await measure.timed("handover save", async () => {

    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/th\/asset-management\/checkouts\/[0-9a-f-]{36}(?:[?#]|$)/)
  })

  expect([saved.status.name, saved.custodianId]).toEqual(["In Use", run.employees.receiver2.id])
})

// Finding M8 (expected to fail until fixed): the handover (HO-) and return (RT-) documents lay their fields out as
// <dt>/<dd> pairs outside a <dl>, an axe serious violation (dlitem, WCAG 1.3.1), on every device.
test("the handover and return documents open and pass axe (finding M8)", async ({ page, measure }, testInfo) => {
  test.fail(true, "M8: the HO- and RT- documents put <dt>/<dd> outside a <dl>")
  const run = readRunData()
  const choices = await readAssetChoices(page, measure)
  const asset = await createAsset(page.request, testInfo, choices, { slug: "FE-DOC", name: "เอกสารส่งมอบ", ownershipType: "personal" })
  const checkout = await handOver(page.request, asset, run.employees.receiver1.id, choices)
  const checkin = await returnAsset(page.request, asset, checkout.id, choices)
  await measure.visit(`/th/asset-management/checkouts/${checkout.id}`, "handover document")
  await expect(page.getByText(/HO-\d{6}-\d+/).first()).toBeVisible()
  await measure.visit(`/th/asset-management/checkins/${checkin.id}`, "return document")
  await expect(page.getByText(/RT-\d{6}-\d+/).first()).toBeVisible()
})

})
```

## The fix round (Tasks 28–51)

Spec §9 (the user, 2026-10-19: "แก้ไขให้เลย"): a failure caused by the app gets a fix on this branch, a test first (a unit or source test where one fits, and the E2E step itself), then the fix, then the suite again; a failure caused by the test gets the test fixed, and the report says which was which; a fix that changes what a person sees is listed for the user; one that needs a decision is asked.

### Task 29 (SF1): The summary records no load time for a page reached by a click (suite note SN2)

Findings: SN2 (repair area). Visible to a person: No (the report's load times only).

**Evidence (quoted from the rehearsal):**
- Repair rehearsal, ER1 (`<temp>/area-repair-findings.md` SN2): the six pages of the walk all show the repair list's load, "287 ms ×6": `measure.recordPage` reads `performance.getEntriesByType("navigation")`, whose one entry stays the first document's for every page reached by a client-side navigation.

**Files:**
- Modify: `e2e/support/measure.ts` (each keeps its endings, `git ls-files --eol`)
- Test: `tests/e2e-fixes.test.ts` (new, LF)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the chain after Task 28 (Task 28's red list).
- Produces: the fix and the unmarked tests of SN2 (repair area) (AS-R7).

- [ ] **Step 1: The failing test.** Create `tests/e2e-fixes.test.ts` (LF) with exactly the file's header and this task's test (the two blocks one after the other, as printed):

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from "node:fs"
import path from "node:path"
import test from "node:test"

// The fix round of the E2E workflow suite (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-e2e-workflow-suite-design.md §9):
// one test per app finding the suite found, appended by its fix task. Each pins the fix in the source (or runs the
// pure module) and that the finding's E2E test is no longer marked expected to fail. The working tree may be CRLF:
// every read strips \r.
const read = (file: string) => readFileSync(file, "utf8").replace(/\r/g, "")
const messages = (locale: "th" | "en") => JSON.parse(read(`messages/${locale}.json`)) as Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>

function filesUnder(directory: string): string[] {
  return readdirSync(directory).flatMap((entry) => {
    const full = path.join(directory, entry)
    return statSync(full).isDirectory() ? filesUnder(full) : [full.replaceAll("\\", "/")]
  })
}
```

```ts
test("a page reached by a client-side navigation records no load time of its own (e2e suite fix task SF1: SN2)", () => {
  const measure = read("e2e/support/measure.ts")
  assert.match(measure, /if \(!navigation \|\| navigation\.loadEventEnd === 0 \|\| new URL\(navigation\.name\)\.pathname !== location\.pathname\) return null/)
})
```

```ts
  // Record the page on screen now (after a client-side navigation or with a dialog open): timing when it is a fresh
  // document, and axe. A page reached by a client-side navigation keeps the first document's navigation entry, so its
  // path differs from the entry's: it records no load time (null), never the first document's again (suite note SN2).
  async recordPage(label: string): Promise<PageRecord> {
    const timing = await this.page.evaluate(() => {
      const [navigation] = performance.getEntriesByType("navigation") as PerformanceNavigationTiming[]
      if (!navigation || navigation.loadEventEnd === 0 || new URL(navigation.name).pathname !== location.pathname) return null
```

### Task 30 (FX1): The A4 documents' fields are a description list (TX-A3, AR1, D1, M8)

Findings: TX-A3 (transactions), AR1 (repair), D1 (disposal), M8 (cross-cutting). Visible to a person: No (the `<dl>` takes the grid's classes; Tailwind's preflight zeroes its margins). Print preview of an HO- and an RT- document stays one A4 page (UAT).

**Evidence (quoted from the rehearsal):**
- axe `dlitem` (serious) "`<dt>` and `<dd>` elements must be contained by a `<dl>`" on the HO-, RT-, TR- and VOID documents (TX-A3), the repair print (AR1, target `.grid-cols-\[38\%_1fr\].border-r.border-slate-200:nth-child(1) > dt`), the disposal print (D1) and the HO-/RT- documents (M8), in all four projects of every run (`<temp>/area-tx-findings.md`, `area-repair-findings.md`, `area-disposal-findings.md`, `area-misc-findings.md`; screenshots and `error-context.md` in `<temp>/repair-evidence/final-run-T2340/`, `<temp>/disposal-evidence/replay-ed1-results/`).

### Task 31 (FX2): The asset page's QR code has a name (TX-A2, AR2, M6)

Findings: TX-A2, AR2, M6. Visible to a person: Read aloud only: a screen reader says "QR Code ของ <tag>" (new message `asset.qrCodeTitle`; the user checks the Thai). Nothing changes on screen.

**Evidence (quoted from the rehearsal):**
- axe `svg-img-alt` (serious) "`<svg>` elements with an img or image role must have alternative text [svg[height="160"]]" on every asset page, all four projects, light and dark (`<temp>/area-tx-findings.md` TX-A2; `area-repair-findings.md` AR2, `<temp>/repair-evidence/final-run-T2340/…-AR2-…/`; `area-misc-findings.md` M6, `misc-em2-summary.json`).
- Cause: `src/components/assets/asset-qr-code.tsx:10` `<QRCodeSVG value={value} size={160} level="M" includeMargin />`: qrcode.react draws `role="img"` and names it only through its `title` prop.

```tsx
import { QRCodeSVG } from "qrcode.react"

// The QR panel of the asset page is the frame (spec 2D D9): no box inside it. includeMargin keeps the white quiet zone
// that a scanner needs, drawn by the SVG itself.
export function AssetQrCode({ value, label }: { value: string; label: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="inline-flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
      <QRCodeSVG value={value} size={160} level="M" includeMargin />
```

    New:

```tsx
import { useTranslations } from "next-intl"
import { QRCodeSVG } from "qrcode.react"

// The QR panel of the asset page is the frame (spec 2D D9): no box inside it. includeMargin keeps the white quiet zone
// that a scanner needs, drawn by the SVG itself. qrcode.react draws <svg role="img">: its title is the image's name for
// a screen reader (WCAG 1.1.1; the E2E suite's findings TX-A2, AR2, M6).
export function AssetQrCode({ value, label }: { value: string; label: string }) {
  const t = useTranslations("asset")
  return (
    <div className="inline-flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
      <QRCodeSVG value={value} size={160} level="M" includeMargin title={t("qrCodeTitle", { label })} />
```

### Task 32 (FX3): A region that scrolls takes the keyboard: the dialog body and the batch receipt's table (TX-A8, D3, M9)

Findings: TX-A8, D3, M9. Visible to a person: Keyboard only: a long dialog body (on a phone, or a long preview) and the batch receipt's wide table on a phone take a Tab stop and show the focus ring; the receipt's table shows the shared hint "เลื่อนตารางซ้าย/ขวาเพื่อดูคอลัมน์เพิ่มเติม" (`common.tableScrollHint`) while it overflows.

**Evidence (quoted from the rehearsal):**
- axe `scrollable-region-focusable` (serious) "Scrollable region must have keyboard access": `div[data-slot="accessible-dialog-body"]` of the return and transfer reviews on the phone (TX-A8, M9), the batch receipt's `[data-slot="data-table-scroll"]` on the phone (TX-A8), and `.space-y-5` of the bulk execution preview in all four projects (D3) (`<temp>/area-tx-findings.md`, `area-disposal-findings.md` with `<temp>/disposal-evidence/replay-ed2-results/…-D3-…/`, `area-misc-findings.md`).
- Cause: `AccessibleDialogBody` (`src/components/ui/accessible-dialog.tsx:104`) is `overflow-y-auto` with no `tabIndex`; the batch receipt's table (`src/components/assets/asset-batch-form.tsx:1247`) takes no `scrollHint`, so it renders the plain scroll div instead of `DataTableScrollArea` (which already becomes a labelled focusable region when it overflows).

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/components/ui/accessible-dialog.tsx`, `src/components/assets/asset-batch-form.tsx`, `e2e/transactions/findings.spec.ts`, `e2e/transactions/known-findings.ts`, `tests/e2e-transactions.test.ts`, `e2e/disposal/batch-bulk-approval.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e-disposal.test.ts`, `e2e/cross-cutting/field-errors.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e-cross-cutting.test.ts` (each keeps its endings, `git ls-files --eol`)
- Test: `tests/e2e-fixes.test.ts` (append)

```tsx
// The parts are optional. Children that do not use them keep their own height, so the content slot scrolls them as before.
export function AccessibleDialogBody({ className, ...props }: ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return <div data-slot="accessible-dialog-body" className={cn("min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4 sm:px-5", className)} {...props} />
}
```

    New:

```tsx
// The parts are optional. Children that do not use them keep their own height, so the content slot scrolls them as before.
// A body that scrolls takes the keyboard (WCAG 2.1.1, axe scrollable-region-focusable; the E2E suite's findings TX-A8,
// D3, M9): while its content overflows it is a focusable region named by the dialog's title, as a wide table's scroll
// area is (DataTableScrollArea). The observers run on the content's changes too (a preview that loads after the open).
export function AccessibleDialogBody({ className, ...props }: ComponentProps<"div">) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
  const [overflowing, setOverflowing] = useState(false)
  const [labelledBy, setLabelledBy] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined)

  useEffect(() => {
    const element = ref.current
    if (!element) return
    const measure = () => {
      setOverflowing(element.scrollHeight > element.clientHeight + 1)
      setLabelledBy(element.closest("[aria-labelledby]")?.getAttribute("aria-labelledby") ?? undefined)
    }
    const resize = new ResizeObserver(measure)
    resize.observe(element)
    const mutation = new MutationObserver(measure)
    mutation.observe(element, { childList: true, subtree: true, characterData: true })
    return () => {
      resize.disconnect()
      mutation.disconnect()
    }
  }, [])

  return <div ref={ref} role={overflowing ? "region" : undefined} aria-labelledby={overflowing ? labelledBy : undefined} tabIndex={overflowing ? 0 : undefined} data-slot="accessible-dialog-body" className={cn("min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4 sm:px-5", className)} {...props} />
}
```

  - `src/components/assets/asset-batch-form.tsx`, edit 3 of 11. Old:

```tsx
              <DataTable framed={false} minWidth={640} caption={t("batchReceiptAssets")} className="mt-2">
```

    New:

```tsx
              <DataTable framed={false} minWidth={640} caption={t("batchReceiptAssets")} scrollHint={tCommon("tableScrollHint")} className="mt-2">
```

  - `e2e/transactions/findings.spec.ts`, edit 4 of 11. Old:

```ts
// phone, FX3) are fixed: no known finding is dropped. A new one is added here with its own expected-to-fail test.
```

Findings: AU1 (audit), M1 (cross-cutting). Visible to a person: Yes: the export buttons go away for roles without `audit:export` (with spec §7's roles, every E2E person; the seed admin keeps them). Who should hold it is DX6 (U1).

### Task 35 (FX6): A view-only role is offered no create, edit or scan link (M5)

Findings: M5 (cross-cutting). Visible to a person: Yes: a view-only role sees no create button on the register and the rounds list, no "continue scanning" link (the panel opens the pending rounds list instead), and a data-health "fix" opens the section that shows the item instead of the edit page it is refused.

**Evidence (quoted from the rehearsal):**
- The viewer's lists (`roles.spec.ts`, all four projects, the same in each): `"/th/assets: link /th/assets/new"`, `"/th/assets: button เปิดตัวช่วยนำเข้า"` (FX5), `"/th/assets/:asset: link /th/assets/<id>/edit"`, `"/th/audit/rounds: link /th/audit/rounds/new"`, `"/th/audit/rounds: link /th/audit/rounds/<id>/scan"`; each ends on the access-denied page (`<temp>/area-misc-findings.md` M5).
- Cause: the register header's create link (`assets/page.tsx:336`, no `canCreateAssets`), the asset page's data-health "fix" links to the edit page (`assets/[id]/page.tsx:706–736`, outside `canEditAsset`), the rounds list's create (`audit/rounds/page.tsx:279`) and "continue scanning" (`:230`) links.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/assets/page.tsx`, `src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/assets/[id]/page.tsx`, `src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/audit/rounds/page.tsx`, `tests/asset-register-filter-ui.test.ts`, `e2e/cross-cutting/roles.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e-cross-cutting.test.ts` (each keeps its endings, `git ls-files --eol`)
- Test: `tests/e2e-fixes.test.ts` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the chain after Task 34.
- Produces: the fix and the unmarked tests of M5 (cross-cutting) (AS-R7).

- [ ] **Step 1: The failing test.** Append to `tests/e2e-fixes.test.ts` exactly (the first line shown empty is the blank line before it):

```ts

test("a view-only role is offered no create, edit or scan link (e2e fix task FX6: M5)", () => {
  const register = read("src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/assets/page.tsx")
  assert.match(register, /actions=\{canCreateAssets \? \(\n\s*<Link href=\{`\/\$\{locale\}\/assets\/new`\}/)
  const detail = read("src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/assets/[id]/page.tsx")
  assert.match(detail, /const healthFixHref = \(section: string\) => \(canEditAsset \? editHref : section\)/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(detail, /createHealthItem\([^\n]*, editHref\)/)
  const rounds = read("src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/audit/rounds/page.tsx")
  assert.match(rounds, /const canCreateRound = hasPermission\(user, "audit", "create"\)/)
  assert.match(rounds, /const canScan = hasPermission\(user, "audit", "edit"\)/)
  assert.match(rounds, /href: canScan && pendingRounds\[0\] \? appendOperationalReturnTo\(/)
  assert.match(rounds, /actions=\{canCreateRound \? \(\n\s*<Link href=\{`\/\$\{locale\}\/audit\/rounds\/new`\}/)
  assert.ok(!read("e2e/cross-cutting/roles.spec.ts").includes(`test.fail(true, "M5: `))
})
```

```ts
// A typed asset tag that another asset holds is the tag's field error, not the database's refusal (a 500; the E2E
// suite's M3). The tag is unique over every row, active or not, as the unique index is.
async function assertUniqueAssetTag(assetTag?: string | null, excludeId?: string) {
  if (!assetTag) return

  const existing = await prisma.asset.findFirst({
    where: {
      assetTag,
      ...(excludeId ? { id: { not: excludeId } } : {}),
    },
    select: { id: true },
  })

  if (existing) {
    throw new FieldRefusalError("Asset tag already exists", { assetTag: "duplicate" })
  }
}
```

  - `src/app/api/assets/[id]/route.ts`, edit 2 of 7. Old:

```ts

  }
}
```

```ts
    throw new FieldRefusalError("Serial Number already exists", { serialNumber: "duplicate" })
  }
}

// A typed asset tag that another asset holds is the tag's field error, not the database's refusal (a 500; the E2E
// suite's M3). The tag is unique over every row, active or not, as the unique index is.
async function assertUniqueAssetTag(assetTag?: string | null, excludeId?: string) {
  if (!assetTag) return

  const existing = await prisma.asset.findFirst({
    where: {
      assetTag,
      ...(excludeId ? { id: { not: excludeId } } : {}),
    },
    select: { id: true },
  })

  if (existing) {
    throw new FieldRefusalError("Asset tag already exists", { assetTag: "duplicate" })
  }
}
```

Findings: M7 (cross-cutting); TX-R9's allow-list entry. Visible to a person: Yes, a little: a photo whose file is not on this server shows the thumbnail's placeholder (as a missing file does) instead of a broken image after a 500; the download route still refuses the path.

- Cause: `src/lib/uploads.ts:43–49` `assertSafeUploadPath` throws `Error("Invalid attachment path")` for a path outside the upload root; `src/app/api/attachments/[id]/thumbnail/route.ts:49–55` turns only `ENOENT` into 404.

```ts
export function assertSafeUploadPath(filePath: string) {
  const uploadRoot = getUploadRoot()
  const resolved = path.resolve(filePath)
  if (resolved !== uploadRoot && !resolved.startsWith(`${uploadRoot}${path.sep}`)) {
    throw new Error("Invalid attachment path")
  }
  return resolved
}
```

    New:

```ts
// A stored path outside this server's upload folder (a row another server wrote, or a Production path in a copy of its
// database) is refused, as a path traversal is, with an error of its own: a thumbnail answers "not found" for it rather
// than 500 (the E2E suite's M7). The download route still refuses it through the API's error answer.
export class OutsideUploadRootError extends Error {
  constructor() {
    super("Invalid attachment path")
    this.name = "OutsideUploadRootError"
  }
}

export function isOutsideUploadRootError(error: unknown): error is OutsideUploadRootError {
  return error instanceof OutsideUploadRootError
}

export function assertSafeUploadPath(filePath: string) {
  const uploadRoot = getUploadRoot()
  const resolved = path.resolve(filePath)
  if (resolved !== uploadRoot && !resolved.startsWith(`${uploadRoot}${path.sep}`)) {
    throw new OutsideUploadRootError()
  }
  return resolved
}
```

### Task 38 (FX9): Every asset lifecycle refusal has its Thai sentence (TX-A1)

Findings: TX-A1 (transactions). Visible to a person: Yes: the refusal reads "สถานะทรัพย์สินนี้ส่งมอบหรือยืมไม่ได้" (and the six other codes their own sentence: four new keys `apiErrors.statusBlocksCheckin`, `statusBlocksCreate`, `statusBlocksEdit`, `statusBlocksMaintenance`; the user checks the Thai). Whether it should also say "reload, the asset changed since the form opened" is a question in the report (open issue 8).

**Evidence (quoted from the rehearsal):**
- The stale second handover form's save: `POST /api/assets/<id>/checkout` → `409 {"code":"ASSET_STATUS_CHECKOUT_NOT_ALLOWED","error":"ASSET_STATUS_CHECKOUT_NOT_ALLOWED"}`; the person reads the toast `เกิดข้อผิดพลาด` with the description `ASSET_STATUS_CHECKOUT_NOT_ALLOWED` (annotation "refusal shown", `<temp>/tx-runs/evidence.json`, run `E2E-1020V`, desktop-light and phone-light).
- Cause: the route answers the lifecycle code as its error (`checkout/route.ts:44–45`, `transfer/route.ts:43–44`), the form passes an unknown code on, and `src/lib/api-error-catalog.ts` has the English sentences but not the seven `ASSET_STATUS_*_NOT_ALLOWED` codes, so `describeApiError` shows `apiErrors.unknown` with the raw code.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/lib/api-error-catalog.ts`, `messages/th.json`, `messages/en.json`, `e2e/transactions/handover.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e-transactions.test.ts` (each keeps its endings, `git ls-files --eol`)
- Test: `tests/e2e-fixes.test.ts` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the chain after Task 37.
- Produces: the fix and the unmarked tests of TX-A1 (transactions) (AS-R7).

- [ ] **Step 1: The failing test.** Append to `tests/e2e-fixes.test.ts` exactly (the first line shown empty is the blank line before it):

```ts

test("every asset lifecycle refusal has its Thai sentence, and the handover form shows it (e2e fix task FX9: TX-A1)", async () => {
  const { apiErrorKeyByMessage } = await import("../src/lib/api-error-catalog.ts")
  const policy = read("src/lib/asset-lifecycle-policy.ts")
  const union = policy.slice(policy.indexOf("export type AssetLifecycleErrorCode ="), policy.indexOf("export type AssetCustodyContext"))
  const codes = [...union.matchAll(/"(ASSET_STATUS_[A-Z_]+)"/g)].map((match) => match[1])
  assert.equal(codes.length, 7)
  const th = messages("th").apiErrors as Record<string, string>
  for (const code of codes) {
    const key = apiErrorKeyByMessage[code]
    assert.ok(key, `${code} has a catalog key`)
    assert.ok(th[key] && !/ASSET_STATUS/.test(th[key]), `${code} → apiErrors.${key}`)
  }
  assert.equal(apiErrorKeyByMessage.ASSET_STATUS_CHECKOUT_NOT_ALLOWED, "statusBlocksCheckout")
  assert.equal(apiErrorKeyByMessage.ASSET_STATUS_TRANSFER_NOT_ALLOWED, "statusBlocksTransfer")
  // The handover and transfer forms pass the code on to the toast, which reads it through the catalog (describeApiError).
  assert.match(read("src/components/asset-operations/checkout-form.tsx"), /throw new Error\(getCheckoutErrorMessage\(payload\?\.code \?\? payload\?\.error\)\)/)
  assert.ok(!read("e2e/transactions/handover.spec.ts").includes(`test.fail(true, "TX-A1: `))
})
```

### Task 39 (FX10): A transfer's TR- document is linked from the asset's history (TX-A4)

Findings: TX-A4 (transactions). Visible to a person: Yes: each transfer in the asset's history shows its TR- number as a link to the document. The transfer form still returns to where it came from; whether it should open the TR- document after the save, as the handover and the return open theirs, is the user's call (open issue 7: it changes TX4's and EM3's landing too).

**Evidence (quoted from the rehearsal):**
- After a transfer, no `a[href*="/asset-management/transfers/<id>"]` on the asset page's overview or its operations view (`links to the TR- document: 0`, all four projects); `grep -rn "asset-management/transfers/" src` outside `src/app/api` finds no link. The TR- document opens and prints at its address (TX4's main test). This is why it was never previewed (wiki #22).
- Cause: the timeline links the HO- and RT- documents only (`assets/[id]/page.tsx:588–633`: `href` for `referenceType` `checkout` and `checkin`, none for `transfer`).

  - Create `src/lib/transaction-operator-label.ts` (LF) with exactly:

```ts
import { prisma } from "@/lib/db"

// Who made a handover or a transfer, as a person reads it: the record stores the user's id (checkedOutBy, createdBy),
// which the cancel dialog showed as a UUID (the E2E suite's TX-A7). The label is the asset history's own: the linked
// employee's code and Thai name, else the display name and the username, else the email or the username. An id with no
// user left (a deleted account) stays as it is.
export async function getTransactionOperatorLabel(userId: string): Promise<string> {
  const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
    where: { id: userId },
    select: { username: true, displayName: true, email: true, employee: { select: { code: true, fullNameTh: true } } },
  })
  if (!user) return userId
  if (user.employee) return `${user.employee.code} - ${user.employee.fullNameTh}`
  if (user.displayName) return `${user.displayName} (${user.username})`
  return user.email ?? user.username
}
```

### Task 43 (FX14): Two audit rounds opened at once both get a number (D4)

Findings: D4 (disposal). Visible to a person: No (a second IT staff opening a round in the same second no longer sees "Unexpected error").

**Evidence (quoted from the rehearsal):**
- Draft run `E2E-1019T2310`, phone-dark: IT staff `POST /api/audit-rounds` → `500 {"error":"Unexpected error · ref 665daf4a"}`; server log `Invalid prisma.auditRound.create() invocation: Unique constraint failed on the constraint: dbo.audit_rounds`, `code: 'P2002'` (`<temp>/disposal-evidence/d-run9.log`); a `SELECT` showed `AUD-2026-0010` created in the same second by another run (`<temp>/disposal-evidence/select-audit-rounds.txt`).
- Cause: `generateAuditNo` (`src/lib/audit-round.ts:51–60`) takes the latest number plus one with no retry; disposal and repair numbers already retry (`withPrismaUniqueRetry`).

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/app/api/audit-rounds/route.ts` (each keeps its endings, `git ls-files --eol`)
- Test: `tests/e2e-fixes.test.ts` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the chain after Task 42.
- Produces: the fix and the unmarked tests of D4 (disposal) (AS-R7).

- [ ] **Step 1: The failing test.** Append to `tests/e2e-fixes.test.ts` exactly (the first line shown empty is the blank line before it):

```ts
  const route = read("src/app/api/audit-rounds/route.ts")
  assert.match(route, /const round = await withPrismaUniqueRetry\(async \(\) => \{\n\s*const auditNo = await generateAuditNo\(input\.auditYear\)/)
  assert.match(route, /import \{ withPrismaUniqueRetry \} from "@\/lib\/prisma-unique-retry"/)
})
```

  - `src/app/api/audit-rounds/route.ts`, edit 1 of 3. Old:

```ts
import { generateAuditNo, getAuditRoundSelection } from "@/lib/audit-round"
```

    New:

```ts

```

  - `src/app/api/audit-rounds/route.ts`, edit 2 of 3. Old:

```ts
    const auditNo = await generateAuditNo(input.auditYear)
    const round = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      const record = await tx.auditRound.create({
        data: {
          auditNo,
          name: input.name,
          auditYear: input.auditYear,
          scopeCompanyId: input.scopeCompanyId,
          scopeBranchId: input.scopeBranchId,
          scopeDepartmentId: input.scopeDepartmentId,
          scopeLocationId: input.scopeLocationId,

      })

          assetId: asset.id,
          expectedCompanyId: asset.companyId,

          expectedDepartmentId: asset.departmentId,
          expectedLocationId: asset.currentLocationId,

```

    New:

```ts
    // Two rounds opened at once can compute the same next number; the unique index refuses the second (P2002), which
    // then takes the number after it, as disposal and repair numbers do (the E2E suite's D4).
    const round = await withPrismaUniqueRetry(async () => {
      const auditNo = await generateAuditNo(input.auditYear)
      return prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
        const record = await tx.auditRound.create({
          data: {
            auditNo,
            name: input.name,
            auditYear: input.auditYear,
            scopeCompanyId: input.scopeCompanyId,
            scopeBranchId: input.scopeBranchId,
            scopeDepartmentId: input.scopeDepartmentId,
            scopeLocationId: input.scopeLocationId,
            scopeCategoryId: input.scopeCategoryId,
            scopeCustodianId: input.scopeCustodianId,
            scopeStatusId: input.scopeStatusId,
            scopeConditionId: input.scopeConditionId,
            startDate: input.startDate,
            endDate: input.endDate,
            status: input.status,
            createdBy: user.id,
            updatedBy: user.id,
          },
        })

        await tx.auditItem.createMany({
          data: assets.map((asset) => ({
            auditRoundId: record.id,
            assetId: asset.id,
            expectedCompanyId: asset.companyId,
            expectedBranchId: asset.branchId,
            expectedDepartmentId: asset.departmentId,
            expectedLocationId: asset.currentLocationId,
            expectedCustodianId: asset.custodianId,
            expectedConditionId: asset.conditionId,
            auditStatus: "pending",
          })),
        })

        return record
      })
    })
```

```ts
        auditNo,
        matchedAssets: selection.matchedAssets,
```

    New:

```ts

        matchedAssets: selection.matchedAssets,
```

### Task 44 (FX15): A form proposes today's date in Bangkok, not the UTC date (TX-N1, AU3)

Findings: TX-N1 (transactions, code read), AU3 (audit). Visible to a person: Yes, between midnight and 07:00: the handover, the round, the disposal execution and the two CSV names take today's date in Bangkok.

**Evidence (quoted from the rehearsal):**
- `src/components/asset-operations/checkout-form.tsx:135` and `src/components/audit/audit-round-form.tsx:116–117` default to `new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)`, the UTC date: from 00:00 to 06:59 Bangkok time the form proposes yesterday (the audit rehearsal crossed midnight in runs `E2E-1019T2348` → `E2E-1020A`). The return form already uses `toLocalDateInputValue()` (`checkin-form.tsx:154`). The same expression names two CSV files and sets the disposal execution's default date.

- Test: `tests/e2e-fixes.test.ts` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the chain after Task 43.
- Produces: the fix and the unmarked tests of TX-N1 (transactions, code read), AU3 (audit) (AS-R7).

## The decisions (Tasks 45–50): asked first, fixed on the answer

Each task opens with its question to the user, in Thai, in chat (AS-R9), with the options, what each changes for a person, and a recommendation; the controller sends the six together after Task 44 so the user answers once. A task stops at its question until the user answers it. An answer is that task's only: it never covers another. On an answer that asks for a fix, the task is written like the fix tasks (AS-R12: the test first, the finding's E2E step red, the fix, its markers and pins, the green run, one commit), from the code as it stands then; these were not rehearsed, so the implementer's brief holds the exact edits the controller writes from the answer, and the controller reviews them before the commit. A deferred decision leaves its test expected to fail and goes into the report and the wiki's open questions.

### Task 45 (DX1): A second user with no employee link (A1) — a schema change

**The question (Thai, to the user):** ระบบสร้างบัญชีผู้ใช้คนที่สองที่ไม่ผูกกับพนักงานไม่ได้ (ตอบ 500): ฐานข้อมูลใช้ unique constraint ธรรมดาบน `users.employeeId` ซึ่ง SQL Server ยอมให้มีค่าว่างได้แค่แถวเดียว และ seed admin ใช้แถวนั้นอยู่แล้ว · ผลใน Production: สร้างบัญชีผู้ดูแลคนที่สอง บัญชีผู้ตรวจ หรือบัญชีบริการที่ไม่ใช่พนักงานไม่ได้ และผู้ใช้ LDAP ที่หาพนักงานไม่เจอก็จะสร้างไม่ได้เช่นกัน
- (ก) แก้: migration ใหม่ `prisma/manual-migrations/<date>-users-employee-filtered-unique.sql` ลบ constraint `users_employeeId_key` แล้วสร้าง unique index ชื่อเดิมแบบ `WHERE employeeId IS NOT NULL` (รูปแบบเดียวกับ `2026-10-07-add-active-checkout-unique-index.sql`) · ใช้กับ `asset_management_dev` ก่อน โดยผู้ใช้อนุมัติ SQL เต็มครั้งนี้ · Production ทำตอน deploy พร้อมอนุมัติแยก · route ตอบใต้ช่องพนักงานแทน 500 ถ้าชนอีก · ชุด E2E กลับไปใช้ viewer ที่ไม่ผูกพนักงานตาม spec §7
- (ข) ยังไม่แก้: viewer ของชุดทดสอบผูกพนักงานต่อไป และบันทึกเป็นข้อค้างก่อน deploy
- **แนะนำ (ก)**: เป็นเรื่องที่ Production จะเจอแน่เมื่อมีบัญชีที่ไม่ใช่พนักงาน

**On (ก):** Files: the migration file (new, LF), `prisma/schema.prisma` only if the controller finds the filtered index needs a note there (Prisma keeps `@unique`; manual migrations own the index, `docs/03_DATABASE.md`), `src/app/api/admin/users/route.ts` (a `P2002` on `employeeId` → `fieldErrors.employeeId`), `e2e/support/run-plan.ts` (`viewer.linked: false`), `e2e/setup/run-data.setup.ts` (A1's `setup.fail` line removed), `tests/e2e-suite.test.ts` (its pin `/setup\.fail\(true, "A1: /` turns into its absence), `tests/e2e-fixes.test.ts` (+1: the migration file holds `CREATE UNIQUE NONCLUSTERED INDEX users_employeeId_key ON dbo.users(employeeId) WHERE employeeId IS NOT NULL` and the drop of the constraint; the route's field error), `docs/03_DATABASE.md` (the migration's line). Red: Task 28's setup ("expected to fail" A1). The controller shows the user the full SQL and applies it to `asset_management_dev` with `npm run migration:apply` only after the user's yes to that SQL (AGENTS.md); never to `asset_management`. Green: the setup's A1 test passes as a plain test; the viewer smoke lands on the dashboard unlinked; `npm test` +1.

### Task 51: The whole suite in all projects, fixed until green (controller runs this task, not an implementer)

**Files:** whatever a fix of the loop needs (each its own commit, test first). Evidence (git-ignored): `.superpowers/sdd/e2e-suite/task-51-findings.md`, `task-51-summary.json`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the chain after Task 50 (or after Task 44 and the decisions answered so far); Task 28's red list.
- Produces: `task-51-findings.md` (Task 52 and Task 53 read it).

- [ ] **Step 1: Verify the tree.** `npm test` (2356 plus one per landed decision, 0 fail, 1 skipped), `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npm run lint` (0 errors, 146 warnings). `rm -rf .next/cache/turbopack`, `npm run build`. AS-R11's probe; the port is free.
- [ ] **Step 2: The whole suite.** `E2E_PORT=<port> npm run test:e2e`. Expected: exit 0; every test "passed" but the expected failures of the decisions not yet landed (A1 in the setup unless DX1 landed; D2 unless DX2; M4 unless DX3; AR3 unless DX4; U1 unless DX6) and the skips of Task 28; no "failed", no "flaky", no "expected to fail but passed".
- [ ] **Step 3: The loop.** For each failure: read its `error-context.md`, screenshot and trace (`e2e-results/artifacts/…`) and its summary row; decide with spec §9 whether the app or the test is wrong; write the finding into `task-51-findings.md` (step, evidence, cause, the decision); fix it with a test first (a unit or source test where one fits, and the failing E2E step as the red), in its own commit (`fix(<area>): … (e2e fix loop <n>)` or `test(e2e): … (e2e fix loop <n>)` with the attribution line); a fix that needs a decision goes to the user as Tasks 45–50 did. Then Step 1 and Step 2 again, until Step 2 holds. A test that fails once and passes on a run with nothing changed is a flake: it is a finding too (the suite has `retries: 0` on purpose), fixed in the test or the app, never by a retry.
- [ ] **Step 4: The numbers.** Write into `task-51-findings.md`: per project the counts and the wall time; per workflow (each spec's tests) × device × theme pass or fail; axe totals (serious, critical, moderate, minor; every remaining one named); console errors (none expected); the allowed records per entry with counts; load times per page kind (median and slowest; `null` for client navigations, SF1); the saves' timings (`measure.timed`); the run id; `node <tools>/scan-secrets.mjs` (0 hits); nothing on the port; `node <tools>/select-e2e-counts.cjs` (the rows the suite wrote across the plan, against Task 0's). List every fix of the round and of the loop, which were the app's and which the test's, and every change a person sees (the "Visible" column below plus the loop's).
- [ ] **Step 5: Clean up.** Nothing listens on the port; `git status --short` shows only the untracked items and the evidence folder's files (git-ignored).

### Task 52: The report to the user in Thai, with the human UAT checklist (controller runs this task, not an implementer)

**Files:** none in the repo. The report goes to the user in chat (and, if the user wants a page to share, as a private Artifact).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the report in Thai** from `task-51-findings.md` and `task-28-red.md`, with the glossary's words, in this order:
  1. **สรุป:** ผ่านหรือไม่ผ่าน, จำนวน test ต่อโครงการ (คอม/มือถือ × สว่าง/มืด), เวลาที่ใช้, รอบที่ (run id), ฐานข้อมูล `asset_management_dev` (ไม่แตะ `asset_management`).
  2. **ตารางขั้นตอนงาน × อุปกรณ์ × ธีม:** ส่งมอบ/รับคืน/โอนย้าย/ย้ายหลายรายการ/ยกเลิก/ส่งมอบที่โต๊ะ, รอบตรวจนับ/ตรวจนับหน้างาน/ออฟไลน์/พิจารณาและปิดรอบ, บันทึกซ่อม/แผน PM, ตัดจำหน่าย, รายงานและการส่งออก, สิทธิ์, ข้อความผิดพลาดใต้ช่องกรอก: ผ่าน/ไม่ผ่าน/รอการตัดสินใจ ในแต่ละช่อง.
  3. **สิ่งที่แก้ (spec §9):** FX1–FX15 และที่แก้ในรอบวน (Task 51) แต่ละข้อ: พบอะไร หลักฐาน แก้อย่างไร เป็นของแอปหรือของ test.
  4. **สิ่งที่เปลี่ยนให้คนเห็น ให้ผู้ใช้ตรวจ:** ทุกข้อในคอลัมน์ "Visible" ของตารางด้านล่าง พร้อมประโยคไทยใหม่ 10 คีย์ (FX2 `asset.qrCodeTitle`, FX7 `apiErrors.assetTagExists`, FX9 สี่คีย์ `apiErrors.statusBlocks*`, FX11 สามคีย์ `asset.movementTypes.*_cancel`, FX13 `transfer.fromCustodian`).
  5. **ผล axe / console / คำขอที่ล้มเหลว:** จำนวนต่อระดับ ก่อน (Task 28) และหลัง (Task 51), รายการยกเว้นที่ตรวจทานแล้ว 4 รายการและจำนวนที่เกิด.
  6. **เวลาโหลดหน้า:** ค่ากลางและช้าสุดต่อชนิดหน้า (บันทึกไว้ ไม่ได้ใช้ตัดสินผ่าน/ไม่ผ่าน), เวลาบันทึกของแต่ละขั้นตอน.
  7. **สิ่งที่ spec ขอแต่ทำต่างไป (การตัดสินใจของผู้สร้าง):** ส่งมอบที่โต๊ะบนมือถือรันบน Chromium ไม่ใช่ WebKit (TX-R3), ไม่มี CPU throttle บนมือถือ WebKit ของการตรวจนับ (EA-R4), viewer ผูกพนักงาน (A1 ถ้ายังไม่แก้), ไม่มีการส่งออกรายการรับส่ง (N15).
  8. **คำถามที่รอผู้ใช้:** DX1–DX6 ที่ยังไม่ตอบ, N-AU1 (ความไม่ตรงของแผนก/ที่ตั้งควรต้องแนบรูปหรือไม่), ลายเซ็นผู้รับบนเอกสาร TR- (FX13), หลังบันทึกโอนย้ายควรเปิดเอกสาร TR- เลยหรือไม่ (FX10), ข้อความ "โหลดหน้าใหม่" ในการปฏิเสธเพราะสถานะเปลี่ยน (FX9).

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import test from "node:test"

// The E2E workflow suite in the docs (plan docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-20-e2e-suite.md, Task 53): the handoff
// section, the Thai changelog entry and the UAT checklist's pointer, each in its place, every marker filled, and the
// names they give checked against the files that hold them. Reads strip \r (the working tree may be CRLF).
const read = (file: string) => readFileSync(file, "utf8").replace(/\r/g, "")

test("the handoff, the changelog and the UAT checklist record the E2E workflow suite in place, with every marker filled (e2e suite task 53)", () => {
  const handoff = read("DEVELOPER_HANDOFF.md")
  const section = handoff.indexOf("\n## E2E Workflow Suite (")
  assert.ok(section > handoff.indexOf("\n## V Track Final Plan ("), "the suite's section follows the V track's final plan")
  assert.ok(section < handoff.indexOf("\n## Open Go-Live Decisions"), "and comes before Open Go-Live Decisions")
  const record = handoff.slice(section, handoff.indexOf("\n## Open Go-Live Decisions"))
  for (const name of ["`npm run test:e2e`", "`asset_management_dev`", "`e2e-results/summary.json`", "`tests/e2e-fixes.test.ts`"]) assert.ok(record.includes(name), name)
  assert.match(record, /\n- Whole run: \(Task 51\) \S/, "the green run's bullet")
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(read("package.json")).scripts["test:e2e"], "playwright test")

  const changelog = read("docs/99_CHANGELOG.md")
  const newest = changelog.indexOf("\n## ")
  const entry = changelog.indexOf("\n### ชุดทดสอบ E2E ตามขั้นตอนงานจริง (งาน E2E)\n")
  assert.ok(newest !== -1 && entry !== -1, "the newest date and the suite's entry")
  assert.equal(changelog.indexOf("\n### ", newest), entry, "the suite's entry is the first one under the newest date")
  assert.match(changelog.slice(entry, changelog.indexOf("\n### ", entry + 1)), /\n- \*\*ผลรอบสุดท้าย:\*\* \(Task 51\) \S/, "the green run's bullet")

  const uat = read("docs/07_UAT_CHECKLIST.md")
  const pointer = uat.indexOf("\n## E2E Suite Follow-up (")
  assert.ok(pointer !== -1 && pointer < uat.indexOf("\n## Sign-Off"), "the UAT pointer comes before Sign-Off")
  const items = uat.slice(pointer, uat.indexOf("\n## Sign-Off"))
  for (const item of ["VoiceOver", "TalkBack", "NVDA", "iOS Safari", "Android Chrome", "QR", "docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-20-e2e-suite.md"]) assert.ok(items.includes(item), item)

  // Every marker of the templates is filled; a failure lists the path and the markers left.
  const markers = /<today>|<N>|<from task-51-findings>|<จาก task-51-findings>|<from the report>|<จากรายงาน>/g
  for (const [path, text] of [["DEVELOPER_HANDOFF.md", handoff], ["docs/99_CHANGELOG.md", changelog], ["docs/07_UAT_CHECKLIST.md", uat]]) {
    assert.deepEqual([path, text.match(markers) ?? []], [path, []])
  }
})
```
