## Spec coverage

- §3.1 the suite in the repo: Tasks 1–5. §3.2 the business rules: status, location, custodian (TX2–TX6, ER1–ER3); no double checkout (TX2); segregation of duties (EA4, EA5, ED1, ED2, EM2); disposal blocked by an open round (ED3); the repair status mapping (ER1–ER3); the PM due date (ER4); errors under the field (every area, EM3); the documents open (TX2–TX4, TX6, ER3, ED1, EM3). §3.3 per step: EF4's measurement, SF1. §3.4 run, fix, run again, report: Tasks 28, 29–51, 52. §3.5 the UAT checklist: Task 52 Step 1.9, Task 53.
- §5 the database, the guard, the hazards, the app under test, the repo: Task 0, EF1–EF4, AS-R10, AS-R11, AS-R14. §6.1: TX1–TX7. §6.2: EA1–EA5 (the export expected to fail until FX4 and DX6). §6.3: ER1–ER4. §6.4: ED1–ED3 (the bulk execution until DX2). §6.5: EM1 (+ FX4, FX5). §6.6: EM2 (+ FX6, DX3). §6.7: EM3 and every area's first wrong entry. §7: EF3 (the viewer linked until DX1; the exports until DX6). §8: EF1, EF4, EA-R4, TX-R8, EA-R10. §9: AS-R7, AS-R9, AS-R12, Tasks 28–51. §11: no failed login (EF-R6); approval settings read, never changed (EF2); uploads local (EF2); new `E2E-` roles (EF-R5); run order (EF-R11); the settings save excluded (everywhere); WebKit for the phone where asked (EF-R8; the desk delivery on Chromium, TX-R3); load times recorded, not asserted (EF-R9).

## Open issues

1. **The assembly's whole-suite run.** Not run during the assembly: the dev SQL Server stopped answering at 03:57 on 2026-10-20 (the guard passed, the seed sign-in timed out at the database, the SELECT probe timed out 45 times until 05:37). The chain is ready (`<temp>/sim-asm`, `e2e-asm` at `941a880d`, built, `.env` and `.env.e2e` in place): `E2E_PORT=3127 npm run test:e2e` there, or Task 28 in the real checkout, gives the numbers. Each area ran green alone on the foundation; the chain changes only the allow-list order (AS-R2) and two unit tests (AS-R3, AS-R4), so a cross-area interaction is the risk to watch (the shared database rows: each area makes its own `E2E-` records per project).
2. **A1, D2, M4/U3, AR3, AU2, U1** wait for the user (Tasks 45–50); **N-AU1**, the TR- signature box, the transfer's landing and the "reload" hint are questions in the report.
3. **The fix round was rehearsed at source level only** (unit tests, `tsc`, lint): each fix's Step 5 E2E numbers are to be measured by the controller; FX3 (every dialog body) and FX12 (the cancel dialog) are the ones most likely to need a second look in the browser.
4. **Emulation is not a phone** (TX-R3, TX-R4, EA-R4): the desk delivery's finger signature runs on Chromium, the walk's WebKit phone has no CPU throttle; both stay UAT items.
5. **Run ids after `Z`** (`E2E-MMDDT<hhmm>`) collide between runs started in one minute (TX-S1, N-AU4, SN1, DN3, N12); one run at a time (AS-R10) never meets it. A change to `nextRunId` (seconds, or a letter after the minute) is a foundation edit for later if parallel runs are ever wanted.
6. **Helpers repeated per area** (AS-R6): a small follow-up plan can move `readAssetMasters`/`createAsset`, the Bangkok date, the toast settle (into `measure.recordPage`) and `actAs` into `e2e/support/`, with the areas' unit pins, once the suite is green.
7. **The dev database grows** by one run's setup (4 users, 4 roles, 6 employees, a department, a room) plus roughly 100 assets per full run, with their transactions and the uploads in `./uploads`; no cleanup (spec §5). A restore from Production resets it (and empties the PM list's ended plans, SN4).
8. **FX9's sentence** says why the status refuses, not that the form is stale; adding "โหลดหน้าใหม่" is the user's copy decision.
9. **FX7 checks before it writes:** two saves of one typed tag in the very same moment can still meet the unique index (a 500, rarer than today); catching `P2002` on `assetTag` in the route would close it, a small follow-up if the user wants it.
10. **Run time:** one worker, about 50–60 minutes for the whole suite (the areas' own runs add up to 52 min, with five suites sharing the database at the time).

## Appendix: the chapters' rehearsal records

The drafts' own records, as their agents wrote them (their "Task n" is the area's own number; the chapter tables map it). Logs and evidence are under `<temp>`.

### The foundation (Tasks 1–5, EF1–EF5)

**Rehearsal (done, 2026-10-19).** Drafted and replayed in a scratch clone, against `asset_management_dev`, with real writes through the app:
- **Clone:** `<temp>/sim-f`, `git clone --no-hardlinks D:/Antigravity/asset-system` with `core.autocrlf=true`, branch `e2e-f` from `feat/e2e-suite` at `fd74d004`; `<tools>/fix-eol.mjs` set 613 working-tree files to the real checkout's endings byte for byte (no content change; `git ls-files --eol` equal for all 1490 entries); its own `node_modules` by `npm ci` (with `PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BROWSER_DOWNLOAD=1`), `npx prisma generate`; the real `.env` copied with `cp`, never opened; `.env.e2e` written by `<tools>/write-env-e2e.mjs`.
- **Draft, then replay:** the suite was drafted to green (runs `E2E-1019A`–`F`, which found finding A1 and the allow-list entry), then the clone was reset to `fd74d004` and Tasks 1–5 were replayed in order from the files this text prints (`<tools>/replay.mjs`; runs `G`–`J`). That replay showed the guard reading the five approval defaults the settings GET fills in as stored rows (a `SELECT` found none): `checkApprovalPolicy` now tells a stored row by its id, and Tasks 2–5 were replayed again on EF1's commit. In that second replay every Step 2 failed as written and every Step 5 gave the numbers written; commits `394a5bfc`, `97c15d43`, `47816315`, `a056b87d`, `8bbf7217` on `e2e-f`; runs `E2E-1019K` (EF2 without tests, then EF3's setup), `L` (EF4), `M` (EF5; EF4's negative check wrote the run id `M` and created nothing).
- **Text replay:** `<tools>/text-replay.mjs` read this file back and applied each task's Steps 1, 3 and 4 to the files of the commit before (from `fd74d004`): every file each task touches equals that task's commit (`package-lock.json` excepted: `npm install` writes it; it was compared with the draft's lock byte for byte in the replay).
- **The app:** a production build of the clone (`rm -rf .next/cache/turbopack`, `npm run build`: 51 s the first time, 33 s in the second replay with TypeScript's incremental file warm), served by the suite's own `next start -p 3121` (`E2E_PORT=3121`), stopped by Playwright after each run (port 3121 not listening after the last run).

#### Findings of the rehearsal (details and evidence in `<temp>/foundation-findings.md`)

- **A1 (app, needs the user's decision before a fix):** Admin > Users cannot save a second user with no employee link (500 "Unexpected error", `P2002` on `dbo.users`): `users_employeeId_key` is a plain unique constraint and SQL Server allows one NULL (the seed admin holds it). The same would stop any non-employee account in Production, and LDAP auto-provision of a user with no employee match. A fix changes the schema (a manual migration: drop the constraint, add a unique index `WHERE employeeId IS NOT NULL`, plus `@unique` handling in `schema.prisma`), which spec §9 says needs the user. The suite links the viewer meanwhile (EF-R7) and keeps the expected-to-fail test.
- **N1 (suite note, fixed in the draft):** `GET /api/admin/settings` answers every known setting, filling a default the database does not hold in with its key as its id (`src/app/api/admin/settings/route.ts:24–37`); the first replay's guard printed the five approval defaults as stored. `checkApprovalPolicy` now counts a row as stored only when its id is not its key; the dev database holds none (a `SELECT` count is 0), as spec §11 says.
- **N2 (app note, not a failure):** a dashboard visit cancels up to 18 RSC prefetches (Chromium; none on WebKit) — the reason for EF-R10.
- **N3 (note):** `POST /api/employees` refuses a body with no `email` key (`fieldErrors.email: "required"`): the zod 4 `preprocess` with `.optional()` treats a missing key as missing; the form always sends the key.

#### Handed on (to the workflow tasks)

- Throttling for the audit walk (spec §8: slow 4G and a CPU throttle) is not in the foundation: CPU throttle is a Chromium CDP call and the audit walk runs WebKit on the phone; the audit task decides (network emulation by `page.route` delay, or the CPU throttle on the Chromium desktop run only).
- The keyboard-open height for the forms with a sticky save bar (spec §8): a helper that sets the phone viewport to the keyboard-open height (for example 390×360) belongs with the first form workflow.
- The phone's full-page screenshot is the viewport (the app shell scrolls inside `main`, not the page); a workflow that needs the whole form scrolls and takes several.
- `measure.expectRefusal` is the way to assert a refusal (403, 409, 400) without failing the request measurement.
- Records per project: a workflow that creates assets uses `${runId}-${testInfo.project.name}` in its tags so the four projects never share one.

### Asset transactions, the user's first worry (Tasks 6–12, TX1–TX7)

**Rehearsal (done, 2026-10-19 to 2026-10-20).** Drafted and replayed in a scratch clone against `asset_management_dev`, with real writes through the app:
- **Clone:** `<temp>/sim-tx`, `git clone --no-hardlinks <temp>/sim-f` with `core.autocrlf=true`, branch `e2e-tx` from `e2e-f` at `8bbf7217` (EF5); `<tools>/fix-eol.mjs` set 635 working-tree files to `sim-f`'s endings byte for byte (no content change; `git ls-files --eol` equal for every entry, and equal to the real checkout's for every file the foundation does not touch), then `git read-tree HEAD` refreshed the index; its own `node_modules` by `npm ci` with `PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BROWSER_DOWNLOAD=1` (no link of any kind), `npx prisma generate`; the real `.env` copied with `cp`, never opened; `.env.e2e` written by `<tools>/write-env-e2e.mjs`.
- **The app:** a production build of the clone (`rm -rf .next/cache/turbopack`, `npm run build`: 111 s, cold, with other agents' builds on the machine), served by the suite's own `next start -p 3122` (`E2E_PORT=3122`), stopped by Playwright after each run (port 3122 not listening after every run). The app code does not change in this plan: one build serves every task.
- **Draft, then replay:** the area was drafted to green (runs `E2E-1019W`, `T2308`–`T2354`; the draft found the eight findings and rulings TX-R4, TX-R6, TX-R9, TX-R10, TX-R13), then the clone was reset to `8bbf7217` and Tasks 1–7 were replayed in order from the files this text prints (`<tools-tx>/replay-task.sh`, which runs Steps 1, 2, 3, 5 and 6 of a task and keeps every output in `<temp>/tx-runs/`). Every Step 2 failed as written and every Step 5 gave the numbers written; commits `21180859`, `340840f8`, `fb5f0a1d`, `d8a87cbd`, `3b213b61`, `3ae13089`, `435f6f8f` on `e2e-tx`; runs `E2E-1020B` (TX1), `D` (TX2), `F` (TX3), `I` (TX4), `L` (TX5), `O` (TX6), `Q` (TX7); the letters between were other agents' runs on the same database.
- **Final run:** all eight specs in the four projects at `435f6f8f`, run `E2E-1020R`: 70 passed in 12.6 min, 0 failed, every expected failure a finding (Task 8 Step 2 below). An evidence run of the handover and cancel specs with Playwright's JSON reporter (run `E2E-1020V`, desktop-light and phone-light) recorded what the findings' tests read.
- **Text replay:** `<tools-tx>/text-replay-tx.mjs` read this file back and applied each task's Steps 1 and 3 to the files of the commit before (from `8bbf7217`): every file each task touches equals that task's commit.

#### Findings of the rehearsal (details and evidence in `<temp>/area-tx-findings.md`)

Each is a test marked expected to fail (`test.fail(true, "TX-A<n>: …")`) in every project it runs in; none is fixed here. The fix round (spec §9) writes a test first, then the fix, and removes the `test.fail` line (and, for TX-A2, TX-A3, TX-A8, the `known-findings.ts` entry) in the same commit.

- **TX-A1, the double checkout's refusal says nothing useful.** The second form's save is refused (409, right), but the toast reads `เกิดข้อผิดพลาด` with the raw `ASSET_STATUS_CHECKOUT_NOT_ALLOWED`: the route answers the lifecycle code as its error, the form passes an unknown code on, and the error catalog has no `ASSET_STATUS_*` key. Fix: catalog keys for the seven lifecycle codes (the Thai sentences exist: `statusBlocksCheckout`, `statusBlocksTransfer`) and a "reload" hint. Thai copy: for the user to check.
- **TX-A2, the asset page's QR code has no text alternative** (axe serious `svg-img-alt`, every asset page, every project). Fix: a `title` on `QRCodeSVG` (`src/components/assets/asset-qr-code.tsx`). Other areas meet it.
- **TX-A3, the A4 documents' `<dt>`/`<dd>` are outside a `<dl>`** (axe serious `dlitem`, HO-, RT-, TR-, VOID; the same component prints the disposal and repair documents). Fix: the field grid becomes a `<dl>` (`operation-document-print.tsx`), keeping the 1-page A4 layout.
- **TX-A4, no page links to the TR- document.** The transfer form returns to the asset page, the timeline links only HO- and RT-. Fix: go to the TR- document after the save, link it in the timeline. Changes what a person sees: for the user.
- **TX-A5, the cancel's history entry reads "Checkout Cancel".** `checkout_cancel`, `checkin_cancel`, `transfer_cancel` have no `asset.movementTypes` message. Fix: three messages (through `scripts/messages-edit.mjs`) and their keys. Thai copy: for the user.
- **TX-A6, the TR- document names no custodian.** A transfer to a new holder prints status, room and department only. Fix: the from and to custodian from the snapshots; whether it needs the holder's signature box is the user's question (asked, not guessed).
- **TX-A7, the cancel dialog's ผู้ทำรายการเดิม is a user id** (a UUID) for handovers and transfers. Fix: the user's display name.
- **TX-A8, on the phone a scrolling region takes no keyboard focus** (axe serious `scrollable-region-focusable`: the review dialog's body of a return or a transfer, the batch receipt's table). Fix: `tabIndex={0}` and a label on the two scroll containers when they overflow.
- **TX-N1 (code read, not rehearsed):** the handover form's date defaults to the UTC date (`toISOString`), so from 00:00 to 06:59 in Bangkok it proposes yesterday; the return form uses the local date. A unit or source test fits it better than an E2E one.
- **Suite notes TX-S1–TX-S6** (run-id collisions between parallel runs, the emulation's lost tap, missing JSON keys, attachment paths, settle before axe, load times under load): `<temp>/area-tx-findings.md`.

#### Handed on and unsettled

1. **The known axe findings belong in the foundation.** TX-A2 (every asset page) and TX-A3 (the disposal and repair documents use the same component) will trip the audit, repair and disposal areas too. Either those areas import `visit`/`record`/`visitAssetPage` from `e2e/transactions/tx-support.ts`, or the controller moves `known-findings.ts` and `settle()` into `e2e/support/` (with `Measure.recordPage` applying the list) and keeps one `findings.spec.ts` for the suite. Same for `settle()` (TX-R6): any area that records a page while a toast fades in can meet the contrast false positive.
2. **The shared allow-list edit** (TX-R9) must merge with any other area's edit of `e2e/support/allow-list.ts`; the list has 3 entries (the EF4 test allows 5).
3. **The desk delivery is on Chromium, not WebKit** (TX-R3): a WebKit pass of the desk delivery (layout, iOS keyboard, Safari) would need a mouse stroke instead of a finger; the real iPhone stays in the human UAT list (spec §3.5). The user may want to know the spec's "WebKit for the desk delivery" became the builder's call here.
4. **TX-A6's signature box** is a question for the user (the TR- paper names no holder; should the new holder sign it?).
5. **TX-A1's and TX-A5's Thai sentences** are the user's to check when the fix round writes them.
6. **Run time:** the area alone takes 12.6 min in the four projects (one worker); with the other areas a full suite stays near the foundation's 30–60 min estimate.
7. **The dev database grows** by the area's ~16 assets per project per run (277 assets in the 17 rehearsal runs), with their handovers, returns, transfers, repair records and attachments in the clone's `./uploads`; no cleanup (spec §5).
8. **Parallel suites collide on the run id** (TX-S1); one controller running one suite does not.
9. **The asset register is where the asset form and the bulk move land** (TX-R10): its first page shows the newest assets, so its thumbnails depend on what other runs created; the allow-list (TX-R9) covers a missing file, never an error of the test's own photo.

### The field audit walk and its review, the user's second worry (Tasks 13–17, EA1–EA5)

**Rehearsal (done, 2026-10-19/20).** Drafted and replayed in a scratch clone against `asset_management_dev`, with real writes through the app:
- **Clone:** `<temp>/sim-audit`, `git clone --no-hardlinks <temp>/sim-f` with `core.autocrlf=true`, branch `e2e-audit` from `e2e-f` at `8bbf7217` (the foundation's EF5); `<temp>/tools/fix-eol.mjs` against the real checkout (609 files) and then against `sim-f` (26 files, the foundation's new files) made `git ls-files --eol` equal to `sim-f`'s for every entry, then `git add -u` refreshed the index's stat data (no content change: `git diff --cached` empty); its own `node_modules` by `npm ci` (`PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BROWSER_DOWNLOAD=1`; `@playwright/test` 1.61.1, `playwright-core` 1.61.1), `npx prisma generate`; the real `.env` copied with `cp`, never opened; `.env.e2e` written by `<temp>/tools/write-env-e2e.mjs`.
- **Draft:** the area was drafted to green on `e2e-audit` in fifteen runs from `E2E-1019T2311` to `E2E-1020C` (desktop-light first, then phone-light on WebKit, then all four; the drafting found the rehearsal notes N-AU2, N-AU3, N-AU5, N-AU7, N-AU8 and the findings AU1–AU3); a full four-project draft run (`E2E-1019T2348`, 7.4 min) passed every project but phone-dark, whose walk lost its typed search to the closing sheet (N-AU7, fixed in `typeSearch`), and two phone-dark runs then found the 15 s wait for the queued send (fixed: 90 s) and the WebKit body-resend failure under the slow-network route (N-AU8, fixed: the API calls are not routed); phone-dark then passed (`E2E-1020C`).
- **Replay:** the clone was reset to `8bbf7217` and Tasks 1–5 were replayed in order from this file's text by `<atools>/replay.mjs` (it applies each Step 1 and Step 3 as written: create, append, exact old/new edits that must occur once); every Step 2 failed as written and every Step 5 gave the numbers written; after Task 5 every file equals the draft byte for byte (LF). Commits `b49fcf30` EA1, `ef7d41a1` EA2, `84a69da7` EA3, `4d2d3e41` EA4, `327d7221` EA5 on `e2e-audit`; runs `E2E-1020E` (EA1), `H` (EA2), `K` (EA3), `N` (EA4), `S` and `U` (EA5: `S` before the last change to block 7's download, see EA5 Step 5).
- **Text replay:** this file, read back by `<atools>/replay.mjs` in a fresh clone at `8bbf7217`, gives every file each of the five commits touches, byte for byte (LF compared), task by task.
- **The app:** a production build of the clone (`rm -rf .next/cache/turbopack`, `npm run build`: 1 min 26 s, cold; the replay reused it (no task changes app code)), served by the suite's own `next start -p 3123` (`E2E_PORT=3123`), stopped by Playwright after each run; nothing listened on 3123 after the last run.

#### Findings of the rehearsal (details and evidence in `<temp>/area-audit-findings.md`)

- **AU1 (app; a fix task, its test is EA5's expected failure):** the audit exports are offered to every audit viewer, but the routes need `audit:export`. The findings page shows "ส่งออกรายการไม่ตรง" and "ส่งออก PDF" with no permission check (`src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/audit/findings/page.tsx:307`, `:311`), and the round page shows "ส่งออกผลตรวจ", its PDF and the variance export (`src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/audit/rounds/[id]/page.tsx:462`–`470`, gated only by the round not being cancelled). For IT staff and the IT head (no `audit:export`, spec §7) the click downloads the route's 403 body: WebKit saves `export.json` holding `{"error":"Forbidden: insufficient permissions"}`, Chromium cancels the download. Spec §6.5: "a role without the export permission does not see it". Fix: pass `hasPermission(user, "audit", "export")` to both pages and render the links only then (a source test, then the EA5 AU1 test passes and Playwright reports it). It changes what a person sees (the links go away for these roles): list it for the user.
- **AU2 (app, needs the user's look):** the check's save that waits for a photo ignores a tap. `save()` means a press of the waiting save to open the note-and-photo section (`src/components/audit/audit-scan-check-form.tsx:254`–`257`), and the button stays focusable (`aria-disabled`, spec §4.4 of the V track), but every `aria-disabled` button has `aria-disabled:pointer-events-none` (`src/components/ui/button-variants.ts:18`): a tap or click does nothing, only Enter or Space reach it. On the phone the person sees the reason line above the bar ("สภาพไม่ตรง ต้องแนบรูปก่อนบันทึก") and must find "หมายเหตุ / รูป" themselves; the walk does so (EA2). Evidence: the draft's tap on the waiting save timed out ("element is not enabled", `audit-evidence/AU2-run-d7.log`). A decision: let this one button take the tap (open the section), or accept.
- **AU3 (app, minor, no test):** the round form's default start and end dates are the UTC date (`new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)`, `src/components/audit/audit-round-form.tsx:116`–`117`): from 00:00 to 07:00 Bangkok time the form proposes yesterday. The walk fills both dates itself.
- **N-AU1 (spec against code, for the user):** spec §6.2.2 asks for the mismatch "a different department or branch, with the required photo"; the check asks for a photo only when the condition differs (`requiresCheckPhoto`, `src/lib/audit-scan-session.ts:275`; an out-of-scope record when anything differs). A department or location mismatch alone saves with no photo. The walk changes the condition too (EA-R8). Whether a department or location mismatch should need a photo is the user's call (the wiki's open questions).
- **Suite notes (handled in this plan):** N-AU2 a toast sliding in reads as low contrast to axe (4.04:1 at 0 ms, none at 1.5 s; `settle.ts`); N-AU3 WebKit's offline error is "WebKit encountered an internal error" (`<atools>/offline-probe.cjs`; hence `measure.whileOffline`, EA-R5); N-AU5 `POST /api/assets` answers 400 "required" unless the keys `licenseTotalSeats`, `licenseUsedSeats`, `purchaseDate`, `purchasePrice`, `warrantyStartDate`, `warrantyEndDate` are present (null), and the component install unless `installedAt` is (the foundation's N3 again: `z.preprocess` reads a missing key as missing; the forms send every key; API clients only); N-AU7 on the WebKit phone, text typed while the check sheet was still closing went nowhere (`typeSearch` waits for the sheet to go and checks the box's value); N-AU8 WebKit cannot resend an intercepted request body on a fresh connection ("Send failed since rewinding of the data stream failed"), and the app then queued a save as if offline (the slow-network route leaves `/api/` alone).
- **Foundation note N-AU4 (for the controller):** run ids collided twice between parallel rehearsals: after Z the id is `T` and the minute, and two runs in the same minute got `E2E-1019T2313` and `E2E-1019T2357`; the second setup's department `POST` answered 409 "Department code already exists" and the run stopped before anything else was created. One run at a time (the real use) never meets it; a parallel run would need seconds in the id or a retry.

#### Unsettled

1. **U1 (the user):** who holds `audit:export`? Spec §7 gives it to no E2E role, so the findings export (spec §6.2.5) cannot be walked; EA5's U1 test stays expected to fail until a role has it (the IT head, most likely). With AU1 fixed and no role holding it, U1's test still fails (no link).
2. **The CPU throttle of spec §8** is not applied (EA-R4): the walk's phone is WebKit, which has none. A Chromium phone run of the walk (one more project, or the desktop projects throttled) would add it; the user's call.
3. **AU2 and N-AU1** need the user's look (above).
4. **`settleToasts` belongs in `measure.recordPage`** for every area (a toast after a save is common); left in `e2e/audit/settle.ts` so this area changes no more of the foundation than the offline window.
5. **Open E2E rounds are left in the dev database by partial runs.** A run of Tasks 1–3 alone (or a chain that fails before block 5) leaves its round open; the rehearsal left 22 `E2E-` rounds open (19 closed, 14 cancelled). They hold only `E2E-` assets, so they block only those assets' disposal (spec §6.4.4 uses an open round on purpose). No cleanup, as the spec says.
6. **Run time:** this area adds 32 tests and about 7.5 minutes to a full run (one worker).

- **Clone:** `<temp>/sim-repair`, `git clone --no-hardlinks <temp>/sim-f` with `core.autocrlf=true`, branch `e2e-repair` from `e2e-f` at `8bbf7217` (the foundation's EF5); `<tools>/fix-eol.mjs <temp>/sim-f <temp>/sim-repair` set 635 working-tree files to the foundation clone's endings byte for byte (no content change; `git ls-files --eol` equal for all 1514 entries; `git add -u` refreshed the index stat and staged nothing); its own `node_modules` by `npm ci` with `PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BROWSER_DOWNLOAD=1` (not a link; `npx playwright --version` 1.61.1), `npx prisma generate`; the real `.env` copied with `cp`, never opened; `.env.e2e` written by `<tools>/write-env-e2e.mjs`.
- **The app:** one production build (`rm -rf .next/cache/turbopack`, `npm run build`, 1 min 43 s with `prisma generate`), served by the suite's own `next start -p 3124` (`E2E_PORT=3124`), stopped by Playwright after each run. The app code does not change in this plan, so the one build serves every task.
- **Draft, then replay:** the area was drafted to green (runs `E2E-1019O`–`T2307`; three found findings AR1–AR3), the drafted files were saved, the clone went back to `8bbf7217` with a clean tree, and Tasks 1–4 were replayed in order from the files this text prints (`<rtools>/apply-task.mjs` reads this file); every Step 2 failed as written and every Step 5 gave the numbers written. Commits `e6b042ba` (ER1), `7a23c77f` (ER2), `04a0952a` (ER3), `47d5b9d8` (ER4) on `e2e-repair`; runs `E2E-1019T2324` (ER1), `T2328` (ER2), `T2333` (ER3), `T2340` (ER4; the run before it, `T2339`, stopped at the setup's first write because another area's rehearsal had just taken the same id: suite note SN1, nothing was created).
- **Text replay:** `<rtools>/text-replay.mjs` read this file back and applied each task's Steps 1 and 3 to the files of the commit before: every file each task touches equals that task's commit.

#### Findings of the rehearsal (details and evidence in `<temp>/area-repair-findings.md`)

- **No workflow bug.** Every status, custodian, outcome, refusal, history line, due date and plan state came out as the app's rules say, in all four projects.
- **AR1 (app, accessibility; a fix task):** every print sheet (handover, return, transfer, disposal, repair: `src/components/asset-operations/operation-document-print.tsx`) puts its `<dt>`/`<dd>` pairs in `<div>` rows with no `<dl>` around them: axe `dlitem` (serious) on every print, both themes, both devices. Fix: the field grid becomes a `<dl>` (no visual change); then the AR1 test passes and its `test.fail` line goes. The other areas' print checks will see the same.
- **AR2 (app, accessibility; a fix task with copy for the user to check):** the asset page's QR code (`src/components/assets/asset-qr-code.tsx`, qrcode.react `<svg role="img">` with no `title`) has no name: axe `svg-img-alt` (serious). Fix: a name (a new Thai message through `scripts/messages-edit.mjs`, read aloud, so the user checks it) or `aria-hidden` beside the visible tag.
- **AR3 (app, accessibility; needs the controller's decision):** an open searchable select (`src/components/ui/searchable-select.tsx`, Radix `Popover modal`) hides the page with `aria-hidden` while the page's controls stay focusable (axe `aria-hidden-focus`, serious; focus is trapped in the popover), and its empty list is a `listbox` with no `option` (axe `aria-required-children`, critical). Every searchable select in the app. Fix (no modal popover or `inert`; no listbox role while empty) or a reviewed allow-list entry.
- **Suite notes** (no app change): SN1 two areas rehearsed at once took the same run id three times (the setup stops at its first write with 409, nothing created; one run at a time cannot collide); SN2 `measure.recordPage` after a client-side navigation repeats the document's load time (recorded, not asserted; the foundation's `measure.ts`); SN3 the asset and handover APIs answer `400 required` for a missing optional key (zod 4 preprocess, as the foundation's N3); SN4 the PM list has no search (the test opens 100 rows; an ended row can pass page 1 after about 25 full runs without a dev database restore); SN5 the PM due box shows 20 plans; SN6 one PM done save took 4.1 s in a draft run.

#### Handed on (to the controller)

- **Fix tasks for AR1 and AR2** (test first: a source test of the print's `<dl>` and of the QR's name; then the E2E test's `test.fail` line goes), and the **decision on AR3**. AR1 and AR3 reach every area: the controller may move their tests to one place when the areas are merged.
- **Shared files:** this area changed none (only `e2e/repair/*` and `tests/e2e-repair.test.ts`). `repair-data.ts`'s `createAsset`, `handOverPermanently`, `readAsset` and `readAssetMasters` overlap with what the transaction and disposal areas need; the controller may move them to `e2e/support/` when merging (then this area's import paths change, nothing else).
- **SN1:** one suite run at a time, or a guard that takes the next id on the department's 409.
- **SN2:** `measure.recordPage` could record `null` for a page that is not the navigation's own document.
- **SN4/SN5:** a restore of the dev database (spec §5) keeps the PM list and the due box short; a search on the PM list would remove the 100-row limit of ER4.

### Disposal (Tasks 22–24, ED1–ED3)

**Rehearsal (done, 2026-10-19).** Drafted and replayed in a scratch clone against `asset_management_dev`, with real writes through the app:
- **Clone:** `<temp>/sim-disposal`, `git clone --no-hardlinks <temp>/sim-f` with `core.autocrlf=true`, branch `e2e-disposal` from `e2e-f` (the foundation's `8bbf7217`); `<tools>/fix-eol.mjs <temp>/sim-f <temp>/sim-disposal` set 635 working-tree files to the foundation clone's endings (no content change; `git ls-files --eol` equal for every entry; `git add -u` then refreshed the index with no staged change); its own `node_modules` by `npm ci` (`PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BROWSER_DOWNLOAD=1`) and `npx prisma generate`; the real `.env` copied with `cp`, never opened; `.env.e2e` written by `<tools>/write-env-e2e.mjs`.
- **The app:** `rm -rf .next/cache/turbopack`, `npm run build` (110 s, cold), served by the suite's own `next start -p 3125` (`E2E_PORT=3125`), stopped by Playwright after each run. No app code changes in this plan, so one build serves every task.
- **Draft, then replay:** the specs were drafted to green in 12 runs, then removed from the clone, and Tasks ED1–ED3 were replayed in order from the files this text prints (`<temp>/tools-disposal/apply-area.mjs`, which reads this file's code blocks), each task's Step 2 and Step 5 run as written and committed. In the replay every Step 2 failed as written and every Step 5 gave the numbers written; the files each task wrote are byte-equal to the draft's (`cmp`), and `<temp>/tools-disposal/check-plan.mjs` reads this text back and finds every file equal to its commit. Commits `522a0f50` (ED1), `a5d40bfc` (ED2), `8dc4f1e0` (ED3) on `e2e-disposal`; runs `E2E-1019T2342` (ED1), `T2349` (ED2), `T2356` (ED3), and `T2357`, the whole suite with the foundation's smokes (50 tests, 0 failed).

#### Findings of the rehearsal (details and evidence in `<temp>/area-disposal-findings.md`)

- **D1 (app, a11y, every print page):** the shared print layout (`src/components/asset-operations/operation-document-print.tsx:107–111`) puts each field's `dt` and `dd` in a `div` grid with no `dl`: axe `dlitem`, serious, on the disposal print in all four projects; the same component prints handover, return, transfer and repair records. Fix: the grid `div` at line 107 becomes a `dl` (a `dl` may hold `div` groups of `dt` + `dd`), with a source test. ED1's "the disposal print has no serious or critical axe violation (finding D1)" is expected to fail until then.
- **D2 (app, workflow dead end):** a bulk execution never executes for IT staff. `buildDisposalExecutionInput` (`src/lib/disposal-execution-service.ts:374–397`) takes each request's own `documentNo`, which only an execution writes (no request, batch or approval form has the field), and the bulk dialog has no document number field, so every row is blocked "กรุณาระบุเลขที่เอกสารอ้างอิงในคำขอตัดจำหน่ายนี้ก่อนดำเนินการ" (`DISPOSAL_DOCUMENT_REQUIRED`) and the preview says "ไม่มีคำขอตัดจำหน่ายที่อนุมัติแล้วซึ่งดำเนินการได้ด้วยข้อมูลร่วมนี้". Only a system admin's historical evidence exception lifts the rule. The fix needs a decision (a shared document number in the dialog, a per-row number, or the batch's number), so it is the user's to choose. ED2's "IT staff execute the other batch request in a bulk execution (finding D2)" is expected to fail until then.
- **D3 (app, a11y):** `AccessibleDialogBody` (`src/components/ui/accessible-dialog.tsx:103–105`) scrolls (`overflow-y-auto`) with nothing focusable in it when the content is read-only: axe `scrollable-region-focusable`, serious, on the bulk execution preview with nothing to execute (the approver's own, and IT staff's while D2 stands), in all four projects. Fix: the body takes `tabIndex={0}` (and a label from the dialog) when it can scroll, with a source test. ED2's "the approver's bulk execution preview is blocked with the reason, and its body scrolls from the keyboard (finding D3)" is expected to fail until then.
- **D4 (app, race):** `generateAuditNo` (`src/lib/audit-round.ts:51–60`) takes the latest number plus one with no retry, so two rounds created in the same moment collide on the unique `auditNo` and the second answers 500 "Unexpected error" (`P2002` on `dbo.audit_rounds`). Seen once in the rehearsal, when another agent's run created `AUD-2026-0010` in the same second (`2026-10-19 16:16:18.792`). Disposal and repair numbers already retry (`withPrismaUniqueRetry`). Fix: the same retry around the round's create, with a unit test. Not an expected failure (it needs two writers at once).
- **Suite notes (no app change):** DN1, the toast fade (ED-R6); DN2, a refused seed sign-in that was the database not answering (the global setup stopped, as designed, and no LDAP bind was made: `authenticateCredentials` failed at its first `prisma.user.findUnique`, `src/lib/auth.ts:108`, before the password check and LDAP); DN3, two runs in the same minute after `Z` share a run id (`E2E-1019T2325`: another agent's run created the department first, this run's setup stopped on `409 DUPLICATE`), which only parallel runs can meet; DN4, the asset and execution schemas need every key (ED-R8); DN5, the bulk execution's row box is named "เลือกรายการ <no> ทรัพย์สิน <tag> - <name>" and the bulk approval's "เลือกรายการ <no> ทรัพย์สิน <tag>".

#### Handed on

- **D1, D3, D4** are fixes with a test first, for the fix round (spec §9); D1 also clears the print checks of §6.1 and §6.3. **D2 needs the user's decision** before a fix.
- When D2 is fixed, ED2's D2 test executes the second batch request: "What a run writes" then counts 12 disposed and none approved per run.
- `actAs` and `expectToast` would serve the other areas (a handover's receiver, an audit review by the IT head); moving them to `e2e/support/` is a foundation change, left to the controller.
- The repair workflow's "cannot repair, propose disposal" (spec §6.3.2) opens `/th/disposal/new?assetId=…&sourceType=maintenance…` from the repair page; this area does not walk that link (the repair area does), but its request form is the one ED1 walks.

### Reports and exports, roles and permissions, errors under the field (Tasks 25–27, EM1–EM3)

**Rehearsal (done, 2026-10-19/20).** Drafted and replayed in a scratch clone against `asset_management_dev`, with real writes through the app:
- **Clone:** `<temp>/sim-misc`, `git clone --no-hardlinks <temp>/sim-f`, `core.autocrlf=true`, branch `e2e-misc` from `e2e-f` at `8bbf7217` (the foundation's EF5); `<tools>/fix-eol.mjs` from the real checkout and then from `sim-f` set every working-tree ending (`git ls-files --eol` equal to `sim-f`'s for all 1514 entries); its own `node_modules` by `npm ci` with `PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BROWSER_DOWNLOAD=1` (no link of any kind), `npx prisma generate`; the real `.env` copied with `cp`, never opened; `.env.e2e` by `<tools>/write-env-e2e.mjs`.
- **The app:** one production build (`rm -rf .next/cache/turbopack`, `npm run build`, 82 s), served by the suite's own `next start -p 3126` (`E2E_PORT=3126`), stopped by Playwright after each run (port 3126 not listening after every run). The plan changes no app code, so one build serves all three tasks.
- **Draft, then replay:** the specs were drafted to green against one setup run (`E2E-1019N`) through a draft-only config (no global setup, the projects renamed per draft so each rerun made fresh tags; deleted before the replay). Then the clone was reset to `8bbf7217` and Tasks EM1–EM3 were replayed in order from the files this text prints, each with a real full run (global setup, setup project, the task's spec in the four projects): runs `E2E-1020G` (EM1), `J` (EM2 before its allow-list edit, desktop-light only), `L` (EM2, stopped by a run-id collision with another agent's parallel run, note N12), `M` (EM2), `P` (EM3), and a final full suite run ``E2E-1020T``. Every Step 2 failed as written and every Step 5 gave the numbers written; commits `2dcb2db3`, `6911fb28`, `7197b41b` on `e2e-misc`.
- **Text replay:** `<tools>/misc-text-replay.mjs` read this file back and applied each task's Steps 1, 3 and 4 to the files of the commit before (from `8bbf7217`): every file each task touches equals that task's commit byte for byte.

#### Findings of the rehearsal (details and evidence in `<temp>/area-misc-findings.md`)

Each is a test marked expected to fail until its fix lands (spec §9); none is fixed in this plan.

- **M1:** the audit findings page and an audit round's page show their exports (Excel, PDF, variance) to a role without `audit:export`; the routes answer 403 (`audit/findings/page.tsx:307–314`, `audit/rounds/[id]/page.tsx:263`). Fix: gate on `hasPermission(user, "audit", "export")`. Visible change. With spec §7's roles nobody but the seed admin may then export findings: decision U1.
- **M2:** the asset import and export page (and the register's export menu and import wizard) offer the import template and the import to a role without `asset:create`; the template route answers 403 (`asset-management/import-export/page.tsx:26–49`, `assets/page.tsx:435`, `asset-register-table.tsx:515`). Fix: gate on `asset:create`. Visible change.
- **M3:** `POST /api/assets` with a typed tag that exists answers 500 "Unexpected error" (`P2002` on `dbo.assets`) instead of `fieldErrors.assetTag = "duplicate"` (`api/assets/route.ts:71` checks the serial only). Fix: an `assertUniqueAssetTag` with a field refusal, in the create, update and batch routes.
- **M4:** the findings page shows IT staff (audit:edit, no approve) the approve, reject, plan and close controls (`canEdit || canApprove`, `audit/findings/page.tsx:388`, `:521`); the review route answers all four 403. Fix needs decision U3 (gate on `canApprove`, or let `audit:edit` plan and close).
- **M5:** view-only lists offer create, edit, scan and import controls: the register's create (`assets/page.tsx:336`) and import helper (`:435`), an asset's data-health edit links (`assets/[id]/page.tsx:706–736`), the rounds' create (`audit/rounds/page.tsx:279`) and continue-scan link (`:230`). Fix: gate each by its page's permission. Visible change.
- **M6:** every asset page's QR code is an `<svg role="img">` with no name (axe serious `svg-img-alt`, `asset-qr-code.tsx:10`). Fix: `title` on `QRCodeSVG` (or `aria-hidden`). Reaches every area that measures an asset page.
- **M7:** a thumbnail whose file lies outside this server's upload folder answers 500 "Invalid attachment path" (`lib/uploads.ts:47`, `attachments/[id]/thumbnail/route.ts:49–55`); seen on the unfiltered register with other runs' photos and the dev data's Production paths. Fix: 404 like a missing file (or a reviewed allow-list entry: the controller's call). No test of its own (EM-R8).
- **M8:** the HO- and RT- documents (and by source TR-, disposal and repair prints) put `<dt>`/`<dd>` outside a `<dl>` (axe serious `dlitem`, `operation-document-print.tsx:107–113`). Fix: the grid element becomes a `<dl>`; no visual change.
- **M9:** on the phone the return's review dialog scrolls and its body cannot take focus (axe serious `scrollable-region-focusable`, `ui/accessible-dialog.tsx:104`). Fix: `tabIndex={0}` on a scrolling body (the dialog tests' pins move with it).
- **Notes N10–N17:** other runs' photos on the unfiltered register (N10, M7); a read chosen as a deadlock victim once under parallel writes (N11, `notification-summary.ts:59`, `P2034`); a run-id collision between parallel runs (N12); the access-denied and not-found screens have no heading (N13); refusals arrive as client navigations (N14, EM-R7); there is no transaction export (N15); the asset API needs its optional keys (N16); the asset form's save waits for its duplicate check (N17).

#### Needs the user

- **U1:** should an E2E role (the IT head) hold `maintenance:export`, `disposal:export` and `audit:export`, so the repair, disposal and findings exports are walked by a person (spec §6.2.5, §6.5 vs §7)? Until then the suite asserts them hidden and refused for every E2E person and opens the files with the seed admin's session.
- **U3:** may `audit:edit` record a finding's action plan and close it, or are both review actions that need `audit:approve` (finding M4)?
- **A1** (the foundation's): unchanged.

#### Handed on

- **The bulk move** (§6.1.6) and its wrong entry: the run has one room, so a bulk move needs a second E2E room (a `run-plan.ts` record) or would move E2E assets into a real room; the transactions area decides (EM-R12).
- **The findings' and the disposal decisions' dialogs** (approve, reject, execute) and the audit scan's mismatch photo: their wrong entries belong to the audit and disposal areas, which own those records.
- **M6, M8 and M9 reach the other areas:** a workflow test of theirs that measures an asset page, a document or a scrolling dialog on the phone fails on the same violation; they may measure those pages in their own expected-to-fail tests or leave them to these.
- **`records.ts`, `files.ts` and `ui.ts` are reusable** by the other areas (Interfaces above), from `../cross-cutting/support/`.
